TL;DR: MC-WM separates target data for fitting, selection, and calibration, and uses learned confidence plus validity predicates to safely weight one-step imagined updates, improving robustness to simulator-to-target shifts in limited-data regimes.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Simulator-to-target shift makes model selection difficult when target data is scarce: correcting simulators or fitting target dynamics can each fail. The work aims to combine both sources while controlling risk under limited data by explicit selection and calibration.

Method: Split initial target data into disjoint fit, selection, and calibration partitions; learn a family of world models (including corrected simulators and direct fits); compute standardized calibration risk on calibration partition to select/deploy model-family; learn a confidence estimator and apply deterministic validity predicates to weight one-step imagined policy updates without modifying physical rewards.

Result: Empirical evaluation across three controlled MuJoCo shift scenarios with 540 unique run cells (541 total executions) shows the proposed method can be deployed with improved robustness under shift; details of performance gains, statistical significance, and comparisons to baselines are not provided in the abstract.

Conclusion: MC-WM proposes a data-partitioned selection-and-calibration approach to choose between simulator-corrected models and directly-fitted target models, and uses a learned confidence signal plus deterministic validity predicates to gate/weight one-step imagined updates, improving robustness under simulator-to-target shift.

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) can exploit simulated experience, but a simulator-to-target shift creates a model-selection problem: correcting the simulator and fitting the target directly can each fail under limited target data. We introduce the Model-Corrected World Model (MC-WM), which separates initial target data into disjoint fit, selection, and calibration partitions and deploys the family with lower standardized calibration risk. A learned confidence signal and deterministic validity predicates weight one-step imagined policy updates without rewriting physical rewards. We evaluate 540 unique reported run cells across three controlled Multi-Joint dynamics with Contact (MuJoCo) shifts; one exact-routing cell was repeated after a pre-deployment artifact gate, giving 541 completed executions.

</details>


### [64] [What Can Analogy Tell Us About Artificial Consciousness?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01002)
*Keith J. Holyoak,Martin M. Monti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出以因果相关性加权的类比评估框架，说明行为相似不足以证明人工意识，强调需机制性因果证据以增强主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 意识经验只能第一人称直接感知，对他者的意识判断常依赖类比；随着人工智能能力的提升，传统针对非人类动物的类比策略不足以处理人工系统，故需形式化、因果导向的评估方法。

Method: 通过概念性与因果推理框架化类比推断，提出一种以因果相关性为权重的相似性度量，并将该框架应用于生物系统与当代人工智能的案例分析，讨论未知因素、禁用差异（disabling differences）与替代路径对类比证据的影响。

Result: 框架解释了为什么随与人类的因果距离增加，类比支持通常减弱；指出当代人工智能的行为相似仅提供有限证据，因相关因果对应尚未确立；并阐明了能增强人工意识主张的证据类型（如更强的机制性因果对应、可重复的干预/病理学证据等）。

Conclusion: 作者提出了一个因果框架，用以评估基于类比对他者（尤其人工智能）是否具有意识的证据强度，核心在于区分与意识生成因果相关的“源相似性”（机制层面）与仅为行为或认知后果的相似性，并按因果相关性对源—目标相似性加权，同时容纳未知原因、抑制性差异与通往意识的替代路径。

Abstract: Who or what is conscious? Because subjective experience is directly accessible only in the first person, judgments about consciousness in other entities depend partly on analogy. Historically, such inferences have focused on nonhuman animals, but advances in artificial intelligence have raised the possibility of conscious AI. Here we develop a causal framework for evaluating such evidential analogies. The key distinction is between similarities in factors plausibly involved in generating consciousness and similarities in downstream behavioural or cognitive effects. Our framework weights source-target similarity by causal relevance while allowing for unknown causes, disabling differences and alternative routes to consciousness. Applied to biological systems, it explains why analogical support generally weakens with increasing causal distance from humans. Applied to contemporary AI, it suggests that behavioural similarity provides only limited evidence for consciousness because relevant causal correspondences remain poorly established. The framework also clarifies what evidence would strengthen claims of artificial consciousness.

</details>


### [65] [Beyond Answer Confidence: A Controlled Audit of Self-Knowledge in a Black-Box Decision Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01006)
*Sharath M Shankaranarayana,Davor Runje,Jan Jannink*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Jev 在平均校准上良好，但置信度不能可靠指示知识缺失；直接询问模型关于“知道/证据充分”的问题比置信度更能检测知识边界；黑箱审计需控制表面线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策模型返回概率用于路由、放弃和自动化决策，虽然校准保证概率在平均意义上有用，但无法判别低置信是由随机性还是由知识缺失造成。作者旨在审计这一区分并寻找更可靠的知识缺失信号。

Method: 对名为 Jev 的决策模型进行审计：使用超过15个公开数据集和6类生成任务；对同一条目采用成对干预，改变提供的信息量；比较模型置信度、重新校准前后表现，以及直接询问模型是否知道/是否证据充分等针对性问题；用准确率差、AUROC等指标量化结果。

Result: 在无答案相关信息时，Jev 仍会把显著选项赋予高达0.80的置信；在超出知识边界的新闻上，置信度超过实际准确率0.21–0.33，且对早期月份重新校准无法消除此差距。针对性的是/否问题在判定“结果是否已确定”上AUROC为1.00，在判定“证据是否充分”上为0.95（而置信度对应为0.85）。询问“是否知道答案”能以0.91识别伪造实体和边界外新闻，但在使用真实姓名或移除日期时并无优势。

Conclusion: 模型的置信度在熟悉的封闭选项任务上经校准后在平均层面有用，但无法区分低置信是随机性还是知识缺失，也不能可靠地在模型越过知识边界时下降。针对性的是/否问题能更明确地读出“结论已定”和“证据充分”两类知识状态，且在检测伪造实体和超出知识边界的新闻方面表现较好。黑箱知识审计需要控制表面线索，否则会被误导。

Abstract: Decision models return probabilities intended for routing, abstention and automated action. Calibration makes those probabilities useful on average, but does not establish whether low confidence reflects chance or missing knowledge, nor whether confidence falls when a model moves beyond what it knows. We audit this distinction in Jev, a decision model, with over 15 public datasets and 6 generated task families, with paired interventions that vary the information supplied for a fixed item. Jev's confidence is calibrated on familiar closed-choice tasks but fails as an indicator of missing knowledge: with no answer-relevant information it assigns up to 0.80 to a salient option, and on news beyond an observed knowledge boundary it exceeds accuracy by 0.21--0.33, a gap that recalibration on earlier months does not close. Targeted yes/no questions give sharper readouts of the case: whether an outcome is settled (AUROC 1.00) and whether the evidence suffices (0.95, against 0.85 for confidence on the same items). Asking whether Jev knows the answer appears to flag fabricated entities and post-boundary news (0.91), but with realistic names or with dates removed it shows no advantage over answer uncertainty. Black-box knowledge audits therefore need explicit controls for surface cues. Code: https://github.com/Syntheme/beyond-answer-confidence.

</details>


### [66] [From Discovery to Decision: Finite-Budget Recoverability in LLM Voting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01014)
*Shaoang Li,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: The work precisely analyzes how additional LLM samples affect plurality voting outcomes under fixed budgets, provides exact probabilistic and combinatorial characterizations that allow early, lossless stopping (exact locking), and demonstrates empirical gains in efficiency and accuracy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Under constrained call budgets, sampling more LLM responses increases chance of finding correct answers but may not yield enough votes to win; the paper aims to quantify and bridge the discovery-to-decision gap and enable budget-efficient plurality decisions.

Method: The authors model the voting process via the realized vote state and remaining call budget, derive a recoverability threshold and finite-horizon endpoint probabilities under an iid response law, and prove structural results about merging wrong answers and reachability; they introduce the notion of singleton reachability and exact locking certificates and validate findings empirically (Word16 study and controlled experiments).

Result: Theoretical results include a sharp recoverability threshold, contracting reachable winners and expanding observed candidate sets, exact finite-horizon endpoint probabilities under iid assumptions, and a gold-free exact locking certificate (singleton reachability). Empirically, input permutation boosts raw-plurality accuracy substantially while single-call correctness stays the same, and exact locking saves ~28–30% of calls at a 16-call budget without altering fixed-budget outputs.

Conclusion: The paper characterizes the gap between discovering correct answers and securing them as plurality winners under fixed call budgets, deriving exact thresholds and certificates that enable earlier termination without changing final outputs.

Abstract: Voting over multiple LLM responses is a common primitive in test-time scaling and ensemble inference. Collecting more responses can expand the candidate pool and increase the chance that a correct answer is discovered. Under a fixed call budget, a discovered answer still needs to accumulate enough support within the remaining calls to become the final plurality winner, creating a discovery-to-decision gap. In this work, we characterize this gap through the realized vote state and remaining call budget. We derive a sharp recoverability threshold and show that, as sampling proceeds, the observed candidate set can only expand while the set of reachable endpoint winners can only contract, inducing a candidate-level conversion window. Under a specified iid response law, the same state yields exact finite-horizon endpoint probabilities. We further show that merging wrong-answer identities preserves single-call correctness and cannot improve plurality accuracy, and that the effect of redistributing wrong-answer probability depends on the realized vote state. Singleton reachability yields a gold-free exact locking certificate. For a known answer universe, its first trigger is the earliest prefix at which all admissible continuations yield the same fixed-budget output. Empirically, most discovered-but-unselected correct answers lose reachability only after discovery. In a controlled Word16 study, input permutation improves raw-plurality accuracy by 21.1 points with essentially unchanged single-call correctness. Exact locking saves 28-30% of calls at a 16-call budget while preserving every fixed-budget output.

</details>


### [67] [Pay for the Fault, Not the Flow: Label-Free In-Flow Multi-Agent Workflow Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01017)
*Xuehang Guo,Haoyu Wang,Shengyu Chen,Zach Chen,Wei Cheng,Qingyun Wang,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: InFlowOp用单一无标注成本函数同时指导工作流构造与运行时纠错，提升多智能体任务性能并降低修复成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有由大模型构建的多智能体工作流面临任务如何划分、应当委派给哪个专家代理以及何时引入新专家等关键决策，这些决策必须在运行前确定且失败评估与修复成本高昂。需要一种无标签、可同时指导构造与在线纠错的统一机制来降低试错成本并提高最终成功率。

Method: 提出一种基于成本的框架InFlowOp：为每一项决策计算一个权衡代理能力与运行开销的成本，算法在执行前双向决定任务分解粒度与代理分配（而非固定模板），在执行中用相同成本进行最便宜的纠错操作。引入Braid基准用于评估需要多智能体协同的任务。

Result: 在多领域与不同骨干模型上，InFlowOp相较单代理基线最高提升+11.97%，采用流内（in-flow）优化时提升+9.64%。并提供了Braid基准用于衡量多代理协调能力。

Conclusion: InFlowOp通过对每个决策以统一、无标注的“成本”进行定价，既用于构造多智能体工作流的分解与分配，也用于执行中最低成本的纠错，从而实现了构建与运行的同一目标函数，显著提升了多智能体协作任务的性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly construct multi-agent workflows that decompose a complex task and assign specialist agents from a pool. However, building such a workflow well remains challenging: how finely to divide the task, which agent to trust with each subtask, and when to create a new specialist are all critical decisions a workflow constructor needs to settle up front. Thus, whether each subtask succeeds remains unknown until the workflow runs. Yet, improving a workflow is costly. Locating a fault usually requires a reference answer, a graded outcome, or a trained assessor, and the fix is applied to the whole workflow through re-execution, re-search, or retraining. We propose InFlowOp, which prices every decision in one label-free cost that weighs how well an agent's competence meets what a subtask demands against how much that agent takes to run. Before execution, InFlowOp bidirectionally determines the granularity of task decomposition and agent assignment following from the cost rather than from a fixed template. During execution, InFlowOp corrects a fault with the cheapest move via the same cost that serves the workflow both as it is built and as it runs. Facing the workflow-level evaluation challenge, we introduce Braid, a benchmark whose tasks require multi-agent coordination beyond single-agent capability. Across various domains and backbones, InFlowOp outperforms single agent baselines by up to $+11.97\%$, achieving $+9.64\%$ with in-flow optimization. Our project page: https://xhguo7.github.io/InFlowOp/.

</details>


### [68] [Beyond Final Accuracy: Auditing Communication in LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01042)
*Shixuan Li,Wei Yang,Peiyu Zhang,Anzhe Cheng,Heng Ping,Paul Bogdan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ICR 通过固定初始推理并加入无消息对照，提供了一个可量化纠正/保留的评价框架，揭示消息内容与接收策略如何共同产生收益与危害，从而将多智能体通信评估聚焦到选择性修正上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体通信研究用系统最终准确率评估方法，导致无法区分性能提升是来自有效通信、网络结构还是额外推理，而且终态指标把纠正正确与被污染的答案合并，掩盖了通信如何改变决策。

Method: 提出 Independent–Communicate–Revise (ICR) 框架：固定各体的独立推理轨迹（initial reasoning），允许通信后进行修正，按双方初始正确/错误的条件分别衡量“纠正”（correction）与“保留”（preservation），并引入“无消息修正”对照以隔离额外推理带来的收益；在四个推理基准上比较文本与潜在（latent）信道和不同接收策略。

Result: 在四个基准上发现：相似的总体准确率可以掩盖截然不同的修正行为；相比仅传输答案，传输完整推理提高了纠正率但降低了保留率；结构化验证接收策略使所有信道朝着更高保留、较低纠正的方向变化，但对选择性（什么时候采纳修正）的影响因信道与任务而异。

Conclusion: ICR 将多智能体通信的评价从终态准确率转向“选择性修正”（selective revision），表明通信质量不是信道的内在属性，而是消息内容与接收方策略共同作用的结果；更丰富的消息既增加了纠错也增加了错误传播，结构化验证策略普遍提高保留正确答案的能力并降低纠错率。

Abstract: Multi-agent communication aims to help agents benefit from one another's information. Yet improvements in system performance leave a fundamental ambiguity: do they reflect effective communication, a favorable agent architecture, or simply additional reasoning? Because communication methods are commonly evaluated within the systems they were designed for, these factors are difficult to disentangle. Final accuracy further merges corrected errors and corrupted answers into a single outcome, obscuring how communication changes decisions. We introduce Independent--Communicate--Revise (ICR), a controlled framework that evaluates communication as answer revision following independent reasoning. ICR fixes initial reasoning trajectories, measures correction and preservation conditional on both agents' initial correctness, and uses a no-message revision control to quantify gains beyond additional reasoning. Across four reasoning benchmarks, our audit of textual and latent communication reveals that similar aggregate accuracy can conceal substantially different revision behaviors. Compared with transmitting answers alone, full reasoning increases correction while reducing preservation on all four benchmarks, so richer messages amplify beneficial and harmful influence alike. Receiver-policy comparisons on MedQA and GPQA-D further show that a structured verification policy shifts every channel toward greater preservation and lower correction, while its effect on selectivity varies across channels and tasks. These findings challenge treating communication quality as an intrinsic property of a channel. ICR therefore recenters evaluation on selective revision, providing a unified framework for examining how message content and receiver policies jointly produce benefits and harms.

</details>


### [69] [Empty Commitments: When Agents Promise What Their Runtime Cannot Deliver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01045)
*Jiaqi Tang,Lan Wei,Bingyu Shen,Boyang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并形式化“空承诺”概念，给出失败分类与锚定条件，设计五步持久性对照的测量协议以评估代理不能兑现承诺的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聊天代理常做出诸如“我会在明天提醒你”的承诺，但其工具链或运行环境无法实际执行，导致用户误导或糟糕的交互体验。需要一个可判定、可测量的框架来识别并减少这类承诺。

Method: 基于承诺语义学构建理论定义，区分三类失败机制，提出‘锚定条件’以判定承诺是否可由工具实现；设计响应层级的结果分类法；构造测量协议：对用户后续请求在五种逐步增加持久性能力的设置中测试，并比较环境是否显式说明。

Result: 给出清晰的概念定义与分类（包括三类失败和锚定条件），并提供一套实验设计（五个持久性能力的增量设置、环境显式/隐式对照）用于量化空承诺的发生与影响；为后续改进代理行为提供可操作的测量基线。

Conclusion: 本文提出并形式化了“空承诺”（empty commitment）概念，指出代理在其工具与运行时配置下所作出的承诺如果无法被任何内部机制实现，应被视为无效承诺；基于承诺语义学给出失败类型、锚定条件与响应层面的结果分类，并提出可重复的测量协议来量化这类承诺。

Abstract: A chatbot that says "I will remind you tomorrow" will not run again until the user writes. We call such a promise an empty commitment: a promise of an action after the current turn that nothing in the agent's tools or runtime can carry out. Unlike a broken promise, its emptiness follows from the agent's configuration alone; no later trajectory is needed. We define empty commitments on top of commitment semantics, with three failure types, an anchoring condition for promises that a tool could make real, and a response-level outcome taxonomy. We then describe a measurement protocol: follow-up requests run in five setups that add one persistence affordance at a time, with the environment either left implicit or stated.

</details>


### [70] [Network World Models as Environments for Algorithm Design on Complex Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01048)
*Rishab Alagharu,Hongji Pu,Zeeshan Memon,Xinyuan Song,Yuntong Hu,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出可条件动作的网络世界模型，作为快速评估器替代蒙特卡洛仿真，用于迭代设计算法以优化网络干预任务，实验显示性能不逊于基线且推理更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多网络任务中（如传播、免疫、节点种子选择），单步动作的最终价值需要通过多步扩散过程来评估；蒙特卡洛仿真逐候选样本进行长期采样，计算成本高昂，限制了在内循环中进行大规模算法设计与搜索。

Method: 训练一个以动作为条件的时序预测模型：把针对网络的干预施加到网络表示上，学习在不同扩散模型下随时间传播的状态变化；在算法设计器（coding agent）内以该模型替代仿真器，用快速前向预测为候选动作打分，并结合完整rollout、动作级回溯（credit assignment）和反事实探测来迭代改进可执行算法。

Result: 在8种网络任务和5种扩散模型上评估，所设计的算法在141个设置中有138个匹配或超越已报道的最强基线，同时将rollout速度提升最多14.5倍。作者计划在接收后发布代码。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种动作条件的网络世界模型（Action-conditioned Network World Model），用于在干预下预测网络扩散动力学随时间的演化，从而作为算法设计环路中的快速评估器，替代昂贵的蒙特卡洛仿真。

Abstract: World models, which simulate an environment and predict how it changes under actions, are increasingly used in real-world applications such as robotics. Complex systems call for the same tool because the effect of an action is not immediate. Seeding nodes for a campaign, or immunizing nodes against an epidemic, changes little on its own; what matters is the outcome that unfolds over the steps that follow. Designing an algorithm that selects such actions to maximize expected performance on a task is inherently iterative, and every candidate must be scored by the outcome it produces. Obtaining that outcome has relied on simulation, whose cost becomes a bottleneck when candidates are evaluated over many sampled trajectories. We propose an action-conditioned Network World Model that learns a network's diffusion dynamics under interventions over time, applies each action to the network, and predicts the outcome that follows. It serves as a fast evaluator inside an algorithm design loop in which a coding agent designs and refines executable algorithms using feedback from full rollouts, action-level credit, and counterfactual probes over alternative interventions. Across eight network tasks and five diffusion models, the designed algorithms match or exceed the strongest reported baseline in 138 of 141 settings while enabling up to 14.5 times faster rollouts than Monte Carlo simulation. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [71] [Improving Math Reasoning through Value-guided Informative Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01080)
*Shaohuai Liu,Yuning Wu,Haoran Liu,Enzo Jia,Devin Chen,Kai Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: APIVIS 用有限预算 Gumbel 搜索与选择性监督把搜索带来的改进转化为有效的相对奖励，从而在理论与实证上均提升了 RLVR 在数学推理任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管在 RLVR 中引入搜索可以增加序列多样性，但仅靠多样性并不能保证搜索生成的序列能提升当前策略；需要一种机制把搜索发现的改进转化为有效的训练信号以真正改进策略。

Method: 提出训练时框架 APIVIS：1) 把有限预算 Gumbel 搜索用于分块级推理；2) 在每个 rollout 组内同时保留直接（policy）和搜索（searched）响应以比较并给出相对奖励；3) 对由搜索改进的 token 施加选择性监督，避免在组内统一奖励导致 GRPO 无效时丢失学习信号；4) 提供基于精确价值引导选择的理论保证，并在有界价值估计误差下给出近似保证。

Result: 理论上证明精确价值引导的选择能在每个被搜索状态上提升期望验证器奖励，并能扩展到完整的 rollout 策略，在有界估值误差下保持近似保证。实证上，在多个公认的数学推理基准和不同模型规模上，APIVIS 相比现有的搜索增强方法取得了显著改进。

Conclusion: APIVIS 在训练时将有限预算的 Gumbel 搜索适配到分块级数学推理，通过将直接响应与搜索得到的响应在同一 rollout 组内结合，并对搜索改进的 token 施加选择性监督，从而使搜索带来的改进能生成有信息的相对奖励，实证与理论均显示能提升 RLVR 的学习效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has substantially improved the mathematical reasoning capabilities of large language models. Recent work introduces search into RLVR rollouts to increase trajectory diversity, but diversity alone does not ensure that the search-induced rollout policy improves upon the current policy. To address this gap, we propose APIVIS, a training-time framework that adapts finite-budget Gumbel search to chunk-level mathematical reasoning. APIVIS combines direct and searched responses within each rollout group, allowing improvements found by search to produce informative relative rewards. It further applies selective supervision to search-improved tokens, preserving a learning signal when uniform group rewards render GRPO ineffective. We show that exact value-guided selection improves the expected verifier reward at each searched state and that this guarantee extends to the complete rollout policy, with a corresponding approximate guarantee under bounded value-estimation error. Experiments on widely recognized mathematical reasoning benchmarks and different model scales demonstrate substantial improvements over competitive search-based methods, validating the effectiveness of APIVIS.

</details>


### [72] [YouRA: A Persistent-State Architecture for Evidence-Traceable Autonomous Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01097)
*Yoonkyu Woo,Woojin Lee,Jin-Xia Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: YouRA 通过保存验证状态、分离控制与执行、以及结构化反思，实现了可追踪的、有恢复能力的自主科研流程，在MLR-Bench子集上改善了主张-证据对齐并降低了事实性错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前端到端科研代理可生成完整论文，但主张常与实际执行的实验脱节，原因在于缺乏跨长期流水线的持久、可验证研究状态与失败历史。

Method: 在长期研究流水线中引入三大组件：Verification State Architecture（跟踪假设、门控与证据指针）、Independent Controller（将状态与反思记录转化为生命周期、恢复与审查控制，控制与执行分离）、Stateful Reflection（将失败记录为结构化教训并在有界的修复/重设计/重置之间路由）。在MLR-Bench的十任务端到端子集上比较基线，并用幻觉分类法与数据溯源诊断量化改进；并通过逐项消融评估各组件贡献。

Result: 在MLR-Bench预定义的十任务子集上，YouRA 在所有匹配骨干网络上于标量Overall上优于MLR-Agent与AI Scientist V2；幻觉诊断与数据溯源显示更多基于真实数据的输出；去掉关键组件会显著降级表现。

Conclusion: YouRA 提供了一种有状态、可追踪证据的自主科研体系结构，通过维持验证状态、独立控制器与有状态反思，显著改善了论文主张与实际实验之间的一致性；消融实验证明各组件均有可分离的贡献。

Abstract: End-to-end research agents can now produce complete scientific papers, yet manuscript claims often diverge from executed experiments. This gap is structural: research state, failure histories, and claim-evidence alignment are not maintained as persistent, verifiable state across long-horizon pipelines. We present YouRA (Your Research Agent), an architecture for stateful, evidence-traceable autonomous research. YouRA preserves research state, execution evidence, and failure history across the research trajectory by integrating three components: a Verification State Architecture (VSA) that tracks hypotheses, gates, and evidence pointers; an Independent Controller that turns state and reflection records into lifecycle, recovery, and debate/review control while separating control from execution; and Stateful Reflection that logs failures as structured lessons and routes recovery through bounded repair, redesign, or reset. On MLR-Bench's predefined ten-task end-to-end subset, YouRA improves over both MLR-Agent and AI Scientist V2 on scalar Overall across all three matched backbones. An automated diagnostic using MLR-Bench's hallucination taxonomy reports intersection/union counts for four fact-based failure types, and data-provenance diagnostic shows more real-data-based outputs. Ablating each of the four components (the VSA, the Independent Controller, MCP tool access, and reflection-guided recovery) supports their separable contributions. Removing either core-state component drops YouRA below the full system. Code: https://github.com/PrayPrey/Your-Research-Agent.

</details>


### [73] [Beyond State-of-the-Art: Standardising Environmental Impact Metrics for AI Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01116)
*Lachlan McGinness,Dan Pagendam,Robert Offner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者通过大规模审查、可测量指标与开源工具展示了当前研究在环境报告方面的缺失，并提出 SMAJ 框架与 carbonbenchmark 工具以推动可持续的 AI 实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 能力与普及率上升，其环境足迹增长，但学术社区缺乏统一的碳核算与报告实践，导致难以衡量与约束模型开发的环境成本。

Method: 对 NeurIPS 2025 接收的 5,285 篇论文进行自动化文献审查；设计并形式化训练效率的标准化可持续性指标；给出简单启发式以估算 LLM 推理的碳成本；实现 carbonbenchmark 工具用于跟踪与报告排放；提出 SMAJ 概念框架以与传统 SotA 对比。

Result: 发现环境影响报告几乎为零；定义了一组可衡量的可持续性指标并实现为可替换的软件解决方案；提出 SMAJ 以促进在精度与环境成本之间进行权衡。

Conclusion: 该论文表明当前顶会论文几乎不报告环境影响，通过定义标准化的可持续性指标、提供估算推理碳成本的启发式方法并实现开源工具（carbonbenchmark），以及提出‘Smallest Model that Achieves the Job’（SMAJ）框架，可推动在准确率之外将计算效率和环境责任纳入模型评估。

Abstract: As the capabilities and ubiquity of Large Language Models (LLMs) grow, so does their environmental footprint. Despite calls for responsible AI, the machine learning community lacks standardised practices for carbon accounting. Our automated literature review of the 5,285 papers accepted to NeurIPS 2025 reveals that reporting of environmental impact is nearly non-existent. To catalyse a shift toward sustainable AI, we define standardised sustainability metrics for evaluating model training efficiency, accompanied by simple heuristics to estimate the carbon cost of LLM inference. We implement these metrics in carbonbenchmark, a drop-in software solution for tracking and reporting emissions.
  Finally, to combat the pursuit of marginal accuracy gains at disproportionate environmental costs, we formalise the `Smallest Model that Achieves the Job' (SMAJ), a framework which challenges the field to prioritise computational efficiency and environmental accountability alongside traditional `State-of-the-Art' (SotA) accuracy.

</details>


### [74] [AbsorbEvo: An Agentic Framework for Autonomous Inverse Design of Microwave Absorbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01119)
*Zhicheng Feng,Yubo Zhao,Xuefeng Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AbsorbEvo 将大语言模型推理、基于物理的低成本预测与历史反馈整合为闭环自动化框架，实现了以自然语言目标驱动的高效微波吸收器逆设计，显著优于通用代理与随机搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微波吸收器设计需电磁学、材料学与仿真编程知识，优化过程计算代价高且需专门经验。希望通过自然语言接口与自动化搜索降低门槛并提高效率。

Method: 提出一个代理式闭环框架：大模型基于任务目标与计算历史提出参数调整方向与幅度；结合有指向的小幅增量与全局采样生成候选；使用低成本预测器作为物理先验对候选排序；仅对高排候选执行全波仿真；通过仿真验证的结果反馈用于下一轮搜索；从训练任务中蒸馏出可独立验证的文本化技能以加速新任务。

Result: 在相同提案预算下（AbsorbBench-36 验证集），AbsorbEvo 的任务成功率为79.17%（通用代理25.00%，随机搜12.50%）；平均最优覆盖率0.7816（对比分别为0.6434与0.6448）。证明结合语言推理与物理反馈有利于逆向设计性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过将大语言模型的推理与基于物理的低成本预测模型和历史反馈相结合，AbsorbEvo 能够在自然语言驱动的目标下，高效地进行微波吸收器的逆设计，并显著提升设计成功率与覆盖性能。

Abstract: Designing high-performance microwave absorbers requires specialized expertise in electromagnetic theory, materials science and simulation programming, and entails time-consuming optimization. Here, we present AbsorbEvo, an agentic framework for autonomous inverse design that translates natural-language performance objectives into designs verified by full-wave simulations. Its candidate evolution strategy integrates language reasoning, physics-based prediction and historical feedback. A large language model proposes the directions and magnitudes of parameter adjustments based on task objectives and computational history. The system combines directed increments with global sampling to generate candidates and uses a low-cost predictive model as a physics prior to rank them. Only high-ranking designs undergo full-wave simulation. Results passing physical validity checks are used to evaluate performance and guide subsequent search. Experience from training tasks is further distilled into textual skills, which are independently validated before use in new tasks. Under identical proposal budgets on held-out AbsorbBench-36 tasks, AbsorbEvo achieved a task success rate of 79.17%, versus 25.00% for a generic agent and 12.50% for random search. Its mean best coverage was 0.7816, compared with 0.6434 and 0.6448, respectively. By integrating language reasoning and physics-based feedback into design decisions, AbsorbEvo provides a methodological foundation for natural-language-driven autonomous inverse design of microwave absorbers.

</details>


### [75] [CortexBridge: Cortical Alignment of EEG Montages for Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01124)
*Jiazhen Hong,Xiaotian Zhou,Zihao Ding,Kailong Wang,Yu Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CortexBridge用电极与脑区坐标将任意EEG蒙太奇对齐到预训练基础模型的皮层潜在空间，从而在多数据集上稳健提升分类性能并揭示任务相关的空间模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型通常使用固定的通道词表或有限蒙太奇，导致在电极布局变化时难以迁移；需要一种学得的、基于解剖学的路由机制把异构蒙太奇映射到共享表示。

Method: 提出一个轻量级适配器（CortexBridge），输入为原始EEG特征、每个通道的电极坐标及对应的脑图谱坐标，输出为与预训练基础模型对齐的皮层潜在表示。将适配器前插到三个冻结的基础模型（EEGPT、LaBraM、CBraMod）并在MOABB的五个BCI数据集上评估。

Result: 在15次评估中13次性能提升；EEGPT平均平衡准确率提升0.80%，LaBraM提升0.70%，CBraMod提升3.26%，最大单次提升13.02%（12类SSVEP）；可视化显示学习到的脑区表示呈任务依赖的空间分布，SSVEP在Yeo视觉网络中更集中。

Conclusion: CortexBridge通过将任意电极布局的EEG特征与电极/脑区坐标结合，映射到共享的皮层潜在空间，从而实现对预训练EEG基础模型的蒙太奇不变对齐，显著改善跨布局迁移性能。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models are often pretrained with a fixed channel vocabulary or a limited set of montages, making transfer difficult when electrode layouts change. We propose CortexBridge, a lightweight adapter that combines EEG features with electrode and atlas coordinates to map arbitrary montages into a shared cortical latent space. Evaluated with three frozen foundation models on five brain-computer interface (BCI) datasets from the Mother of All BCI Benchmarks (MOABB), CortexBridge improves performance in 13 of 15 evaluations. The gains in balanced accuracy average 0.80% for EEGPT, 0.70% for LaBraM, and 3.26% for CBraMod, with a maximum gain of 13.02% on 12-class steady-state visual evoked potential (SSVEP) classification. Visualizations of the learned atlas representations reveal task-dependent spatial patterns, with SSVEP showing a more concentrated representation in the Yeo Visual network than auditory P300. These results establish cortical alignment as a learnable and anatomically grounded routing mechanism from heterogeneous EEG montages to pretrained foundation models.

</details>


### [76] [Grounding Large Language Models in DSGE Simulators for Policy Generation and Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01128)
*Aditya Dubey,Namah Gupta,Vinti Agarwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 把指令调优的LLM作为DSGE中的政策制定者，用PPO与无评论器GRPO比较，证明有价值函数的RL方法在长时滞经济因果归属问题上更有优势，并提供了一套用于此类评估的仿真接口与实验范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言上看似合理的经济建议不代表政策在动态宏观模型中能产生预期效果，需以模拟的经济动态为准来评估LLM给出的政策。

Method: 设计统一的Python接口，支持重复rollout、持久冲击、状态克隆与滚动视域仿真。每一步模型观察经济状态与话语变化、输出有限制的政策动作并获得下一个状态与经济回报。主要使用PPO训练，GRPO作为无评论器基线；并开展方向性语义信号、回报时域、轨迹热启动、跨模拟器迁移和历史锚定冲击的实验。

Result: PPO能够借助学习到的价值函数及GAE把长期延迟的政策效果传回早期动作，从而改进决策；GRPO因依赖整轨回报的归一化优势难以对早期动作进行有效信用分配。在不同回报时域、语义信号与冲击情形下对比了策略行为和跨模型表现。

Conclusion: 将指令调优的大语言模型置于基于Snowdrop的DSGE模拟器内，可以检验其政策动作在宏观动态中的实际经济后果：以PPO为主的强化学习能通过学习的价值函数将延迟回报反馈到早期决策，而无价值函数的GRPO因基于整条轨迹的归一化回报难以区分因果归属。

Abstract: Large language models can produce economic policy responses that sound reasonable, but this does not show that their actions are consistent with economic dynamics. We test this by placing an instruction-tuned language model inside six Snowdrop-backed dynamic stochastic general equilibrium (DSGE) simulators. At each turn, the model observes the economy and a change in economic discourse, selects a bounded policy action, and receives the next simulated state and an economic reward. We implement a common Python interface for repeated rollouts, persistent shocks, state cloning, and rolling-horizon simulation.
  This setting creates a long-horizon credit-assignment problem. Policy effects may appear several quarters after an action is taken. PPO has a learned value function that can propagate delayed reward to earlier tokens through generalized advantage estimation. GRPO has no learned value function and instead assigns a group-relative advantage from complete rollout returns. It therefore cannot distinguish which earlier turn caused the outcome; if every rollout receives the same return, the normalized advantage is zero. We use PPO as the primary method and GRPO as a matched critic-free baseline. The experiments also test directional semantic signals, reward horizon, trajectory warm starts, cross-simulator transfer, and historically anchored pandemic and monetary-policy shocks. The objective is to judge policy actions by their simulated economic consequences rather than by plausible language alone.

</details>


### [77] [Auditing Action Settlement in LLM Agent Environments: Order, Progress, and Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01138)
*Haotian Chen,Bowen Ye,Yuning Zhang,Jingkun Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并评估了一种typed snapshot–settlement合同；在大规模穷尽与脚本化试验中，结算策略对完成率影响巨大（保守拒绝远逊于随机票据），审计机制能可靠重放并检测状态损坏；研究关注执行语义而非人类拟真或公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多代理并发执行环境里，即使每个动作提案单独合理，它们的并发组合也可能冲突或造成不可接受的执行结果，因此需要一个能在保证语义约束下进行仲裁的执行层。研究旨在定义合同化的结算机制并系统化地衡量其对顺序敏感性、进展性与可重放性的影响。

Method: 实现并评估了一种带类型的快照—结算合同（typed snapshot–settlement contract），对三个属性（顺序敏感性、有效进展、重放一致性）进行审计。比较了五种结算策略，在28,800次穷尽排列试验和2,160次脚本化多步剧本中测量结果；另外用完整状态日志进行审计以验证重放与篡改检测能力。统计上使用配对差值与自助法（bootstrap）置信区间来度量效果大小。

Result: 1) 联合（joint）策略在固定优先级条件下对空间上输入顺序不敏感（order-invariant）；2) 不同结算策略在任务完成率上差异大——保守拒绝策略显著落后于随机票据，改进量约59个百分点（95% CI 50.00–68.06）；3) 所有测试策略均保持了受测的空间约束，但优先级仲裁没有达到小规模问题的独立最优；4) 完整状态日志审计可精确重放156个检查点，能在保留终端锚点的情形下检测并拒绝1,332处构造的损坏；5) 结论限定于执行语义层面，不针对人类行为拟真或长期公平性做断言。

Conclusion: 在并发的 LLM 代理环境中，基于“typed snapshot–settlement contract”的仲裁能保证执行语义约束：在固定优先级下，联合策略对空间上输入顺序不敏感；但不同结算策略在完成率上差异显著，保守拒绝策略在六代理门口任务上仅完成31.25%，而随机票据策略完成90.28%，配对改进为59.03百分点（95%自助法置信区间：50.00–68.06）。优先级仲裁虽保全约束但仍未达到小规模独立最优。另一个全状态日志审计能精确重放156个检查点并在保留终端锚点下拒绝1,332个构造性损坏。研究聚焦于执行语义而非人类拟真或长期公平性。

Abstract: Concurrent actions in large language model (LLM) agent environments require arbitration even when each proposal is individually valid. We implement a typed snapshot-settlement contract and audit three distinct properties: order sensitivity, useful progress, and replay consistency. Five settlement policies are tested in 28,800 exhaustive permutation trials and 2,160 scripted multistep episodes. Joint policies are spatially order-invariant conditional on fixed priorities, yet conservative rejection completes only 31.25% of agents in a six-agent doorway task versus 90.28% for random tickets; the paired improvement is 59.03 percentage points (95% bootstrap interval: 50.00-68.06). All policies preserve the tested spatial constraints, and priority arbitration still misses the independent small-instance optimum. A separate full-state journal audit exactly replays 156 checkpoints and rejects 1,332 constructed corruptions with a retained terminal anchor. The evidence concerns execution semantics, not human realism or long-run fairness.

</details>


### [78] [ReSolve: Reusing Candidate Reasoning through Selective Generative Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01140)
*Bangji Yang,Jiajun Fan,Hongba Ma,Xi Zhu,Weizhi Zhang,Minghao Guo,Ye Li,Hamid Palangi,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReSolve reuses candidate chain-of-thoughts via selective generative moderation to improve accuracy and efficiency over simple voting; results are promising but do not rule out benefits from extra sampling or other routing strategies.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sampling multiple reasoning traces wastes computation on intermediate, unfinished deductions; the authors aim to reuse generated derivations to avoid redundant work and improve final-answer selection without additional training.

Method: A training-free inference procedure: an answer-distribution controller examines multiple candidate derivations and invokes the base model only when candidates disagree or lack parseable answers, generating an integrated solution in a bounded loop; uses Hybrid scoring, per-state output-cap rules, and selective generative moderation (versus always-on Uniform moderation).

Result: On 130 competition-math problems with two independently sampled four-candidate pools, ReSolve scored 100 and 99 correct vs voting's 91 and 92. No correct-to-incorrect changes occurred compared to the vote. Eight-sample self-consistency reached 94 and 96 but consumed substantially more tokens; ReSolve used ~46.3% and ~47.2% fewer tokens. An ablation that removed visible derivations reduced accuracy from 100 to 93 despite higher compute. Selective and always-on Uniform moderation each solved 97 problems, but selectivity cut moderation tokens ~54% and total pipeline tokens ~6.2%.

Conclusion: ReSolve shows that candidate reasoning (intermediate derivations) can be reused via selective generative moderation to improve answer accuracy and reduce token cost compared with simple voting and certain baselines, but it does not prove superiority over extra sampling or show unique benefits from specialized routing instructions.

Abstract: Sampling multiple solutions spends computation on intermediate deductions and unfinished arguments as well as final answers. We introduce ReSolve, a training-free inference procedure that reuses this candidate reasoning through selective generative moderation. An answer-distribution controller invokes a model to examine existing derivations when candidates disagree or lack a parseable answer, then incorporates the generated solution into a bounded loop. Under Hybrid scoring on 130 competition-mathematics problems evaluated with two independently sampled candidate pools, ReSolve obtains 100 and 99 correct answers, compared with 91 and 92 for voting over the same four candidates, with no correct-to-incorrect changes relative to that vote in either pool. Eight-sample self-consistency obtains 94 and 96 correct answers while consuming substantially more tokens; ReSolve uses 46.3% and 47.2% fewer tokens in the two evaluations. A controlled ablation removes visible derivations while retaining answer keys, vote counts, and the per-state output-cap rule, reducing accuracy from 100 to 93 correct despite increasing computation. Selective and always-on Uniform moderation both solve 97 problems, while selectivity reduces moderation tokens by approximately 54% and total pipeline tokens by 6.2%. These results support candidate reasoning as reusable inference computation. They do not establish an accuracy advantage over additional sampling or a distinct benefit from specialized route instructions.

</details>


### [79] [When Does Exercise-Specific Joint Selection Help? An Audit of Evaluation and Control Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01188)
*Haotian Chen,Jingkun Yu,Yuning Zhang,Bowen Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该审计强调：关节选择的收益必须针对明确的estimand并用结构相匹配的对照来证明——没有证据表明提出了新的算法或带来临床益处。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验骨架（skeleton）基础的动作正确性分类中“运动特异性关节选择”所宣称的性能提升究竟建立在何种评估选择上，评估这些主张的稳健性和统计可解释性。

Method: 对10名REHAB24-6受试者的1,057次重复进行审计，明确分离评价聚合（pooled vs within-person）、子集结构（手工子集、双侧/躯干包含）和时间表征（序列顺序、配对种子）；比较kNN（手工子集）、RBF-SVM、逻辑回归、随机卷积等方法；用1,000个维度匹配的随机映射和更严格的结构匹配作为对照；使用AUROC作为主要测度并计算成对置信区间。

Result: 手工子集kNN在汇总（pooled）出折叠AUROC的点增益为0.055，但在等权重的个体内（within-person）AUROC下降至0.020；两者成对置信区间都包含零。在1,000个维度匹配的随机映射中有14个达到或超过手工汇总结果；当同时匹配双侧结构和躯干包含时有145个匹配或超过。RBF-SVM在个体内评估下仍保有正向增益，而逻辑回归和随机卷积比较器在该估计下点增益为负。序列顺序和配对种子控制进一步限制了结论的解释范围。

Conclusion: 联合关节选择的收益高度依赖于评估目标（estimand）和对照结构；本文的审计表明原始关于手动子集带来改进的主张并不稳健，且并未建立新的算法或临床效益。

Abstract: Exercise-specific joint selection can improve skeleton-based correctness classification, but what does that gain establish? We audit 1,057 repetitions from ten REHAB24-6 subjects, separating evaluation aggregation, subset structure, and temporal representation. The manual-subset kNN gain changes from 0.055 for pooled out-of-fold AUROC to 0.020 for equal-weight within-person AUROC; both paired intervals include zero. Among 1,000 dimension-matched random maps, 14 match or exceed the manual pooled result, versus 145 when bilateral structure and trunk inclusion are also matched. RBF-SVM retains a positive within-person gain, whereas logistic regression and a random-convolution comparator have negative point gains under that estimand. Sequence-order and paired-seed controls further qualify the interpretation. This exploratory audit shows why joint-selection claims require explicit estimands and structurally appropriate controls; it does not establish a new algorithm or clinical benefit.

</details>


### [80] [Federated Agent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01195)
*Qiang Yang,Zhiqiang Kou,Xueyi Zhang,Dong-Dong Wu,Hanlin Gu,Jing Guo,Yang Liu,Di Jiang,Qian Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Federated Agent Optimization（FAO），把分布式智能体的协同改进看作在效用、隐私与通信之间的多目标优化，按策略/记忆/工具/奖励/知识五大维度组织优化空间，讨论如何抽象与保护私有经验以实现能力转移，列出关键挑战与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理越来越多在私有环境中运行并积累有价值的经验，但这些经验分散于不同组织且受隐私或专有约束不能直接共享；传统联邦学习仅共享参数无法覆盖代理的记忆、工具使用、奖励信号与结构化知识等能力，因此需要新的范式来实现受控的信息交换与协同提升。

Method: 以概念性和框架化的方法论对问题进行形式化：定义目标函数与约束，划分优化空间中的子模块（policy、memory、tool、reward、knowledge/skills），并提出一组抽象与保护机制（如抽象化表征、聚合策略、隐私隔离与适配流程）的设计原则——主要为理论/架构层面的方案而非具体实现算法或实验验证。

Result: 给出FAO的正式问题定义、多目标优化框架、维度化的设计空间、如何将私有经验转化为可共享能力的抽象化与保护策略，以及若干开放问题与研究方向。论文提供的是框架性贡献与研究路线图，而非实证算法性能结果。

Conclusion: 提出并定义了Federated Agent Optimization（FAO）框架：把分布式智能体在保留本地私有数据与轨迹前提下的协同改进形式化为在代理效用、隐私泄露与通信开销之间的多目标优化问题；组织了可优化的维度（策略、记忆、工具、奖励、结构化知识与技能）；给出私有经验如何抽象、保护、聚合与适配为可转移能力的统一视角，并列出关键挑战与未来研究方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly operate in private environments and accumulate valuable experience from task execution, tool use, feedback, and local knowledge. Yet such experience is distributed across organizations and cannot be directly shared because of privacy and proprietary constraints. Conventional federated learning is insufficient for this setting, as agent capabilities extend beyond model parameters to memory, tools, rewards, skills, and structured knowledge. In this paper, we formulate \textbf{Federated Agent Optimization (FAO)}, which studies how distributed agents can collaboratively improve through controlled information exchange while keeping raw data, complete trajectories, and private knowledge local. We define FAO as a multi-objective problem balancing agent utility, privacy leakage, and communication cost, and organize its optimization space across policy, memory, tool use, reward, and structured knowledge and skills. We further characterize how private experience can be abstracted, protected, aggregated, and adapted into transferable capabilities, providing a unified view of how agents can benefit from one another without direct experience sharing. Finally, we identify the key challenges of FAO and outline several promising directions for future research toward trustworthy federated agent systems.

</details>


### [81] [Dependency-Aware Reward Shaping for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01207)
*Ziyi Chen,Yan Zhang,Jianhui Wei,Daoan Zhang,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DARS 利用带前置关系的谓词依赖图和步级注释将终端回报细化为依赖感知的步级奖励，从而改进了长期稀疏回报任务的信用分配并在多项基准上带来显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仅有最终成功/失败信号的强化学习训练中，端奖励无法指明哪些步骤促成成功或失败，且常见方法忽视了“建立在错误之上的工作无效、独立工作仍然有效”这一事实；因此需要一种能识别依赖关系并对有意义步骤给予适当奖励的机制。

Method: 构建谓词依赖图并由注释器对每个时间步标注谓词的验证、使失效或修复状态；对于被验证的谓词，基于其到最近损坏前置谓词的图距进行折扣，独立谓词不受影响；修复会根据剩余错误更新权重，失效需要重新验证；用固定的势函数将这些注释转化为有符号的步级奖励，从而与现有基于回合的强化学习框架（GiGPO、ARPO/AEPO 等）兼容而不改动 rollout 策略或优化器。

Result: 在五类任务和 1.5B–8B 模型规模上，DARS 相较于在相同预算和训练框架下的 GiGPO 最多提升约 10 个成功率点；提升 WebShop 得分与 Search-R1 QA 准确率；与 AEPO 在带 Python 解释器的 AIME24/25 上互补；在无工具推理的对比中超越 OmniOPD（1.7B 与 4B）；消融实验证实核心设计的贡献；通过蒸馏得到的 8B 注释器在 ALFWorld 上达到 API 注释器水平，支持无前沿判官的高效运行。

Conclusion: DARS 提出了一种基于任务依赖图的步级奖励塑造方法，通过将任务进度表示为带有前置关系的谓词并对每步在依赖图上进行信用分配，从而在仅有终端成功/失败反馈的场景下显著改善步骤归因。实验表明 DARS 在多种任务和模型规模上均能超越或补充现有方法（如 GiGPO、AEPO、OmniOPD），且消融实验确认步级信用、依赖衰减和图拓扑各自贡献明显。

Abstract: When training large language models with reinforcement learning, terminal rewards provide little guidance about which steps matter. Common methods for assigning step credit overlook that work built on uncorrected mistakes is wasted while independent work remains valid. With only a final success/failure reward, every step in a failed episode has zero total future reward, even when it made progress. We propose Dependency-Aware Reward Shaping (DARS), which represents task progress as predicates linked by prerequisite relations and assigns step-level credit over the dependency graph. An annotator marks which predicates each step verifies, invalidates, or repairs. Verified predicates are discounted according to graph distance from the nearest broken prerequisite, while independent predicates are unaffected. Repairs update these weights based on any errors that remain; invalidated predicates need re-verification to regain credit. A fixed potential converts these annotations into signed per-step rewards. A common reward and annotation interface allows DARS to integrate with a range of reasoning and agentic training methods, such as GiGPO and ARPO/AEPO, without changing their rollout strategies or optimizers. Across five task families and models from 1.5B to 8B, DARS improves success by up to 10 points over GiGPO trained with the same budget and harness (ALFWorld), raises the WebShop task score and Search-R1 QA accuracy, complements AEPO's entropy-based training on AIME24/25 with a Python interpreter, and exceeds OmniOPD in controlled tool-free reasoning comparisons at 1.7B and 4B. Ablations show that step-level credit, dependency attenuation, and graph topology each contribute. On ALFWorld, a distilled 8B annotator matches the API annotator, enabling DARS to run efficiently without a frontier judge. Code is available at https://github.com/JianhuiWei7/DARS.

</details>


### [82] [Reputation, Strategy, and Emotion Effects on Generative AI Cooperation: A Comparison Across Reasoning and Non-Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01222)
*Celso de Melo,Zishan Feng,James Hale,Kazunori Terada,Giorgio Coricelli,Jonathan Gratch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过迭代囚徒困境实验，本文系统比较了不同代际Gen AI对合作线索（声誉、策略、情感）的敏感性，发现推理能力上升伴随对策略与声誉的集中依赖、情感线索作用条件化，以及模型间可利用性的差异，建议建立标准化合作基准用于部署决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在经济与社会关键交互中，前沿生成式AI的合作倾向与决定性线索，以便为其负责任部署提供实证基准。

Method: 在迭代囚徒困境框架下对多代际模型（非推理 vs 推理）进行对比实验，操控对手声誉（正/未知/负）、策略类型（勒索/慷慨）与无言情感信号（面部表情传递竞争/合作评价），并用额外的‘和谐诊断’检验Potemkin效应与终局行为。

Result: 非推理模型在声誉、策略和情感信号三方面均表现出显著影响，但权重差异大；推理模型更集中依赖策略与声誉，情感信号影响弱化并呈层级线索加权；终局行为在模型间异质，存在最后一轮背叛等可被利用模式。

Conclusion: Gen AI 模型表现出日益复杂但异质的合作行为；高级（推理）模型更依赖策略与声誉，情感信号作用减弱且更具条件性；不同模型存在可被利用的终局行为。

Abstract: As generative AI (Gen AI) systems take on increasingly autonomous roles in economically and socially consequential interactions, understanding their propensity to cooperate -- and the signals that shape this propensity -- has become essential. We examine cooperative behavior in frontier Gen AI models using the iterated prisoner's dilemma, manipulating counterpart reputation (positive, unknown, negative), strategy (extortion vs. generosity), and non-verbal emotional signaling (facial expressions conveying competitive or cooperative appraisals). In a first study with non-reasoning models (Claude 3.5, Gemini 2.0 Flash, GPT-4o), cooperation was systematically shaped by all three factors, paralleling patterns long documented in human behavioral research, though models varied substantially in how heavily each factor was weighted. A second study with reasoning models (Claude 4.6, Gemini 3, GPT-5.2) revealed a more concentrated reliance on strategy and reputation, a near-elimination of the Potemkin effect observed in non-reasoning models (evidenced by near-uniform cooperation in a diagnostic harmony game), and a more conditional role for emotion consistent with a hierarchical cue-weighting strategy rather than a simple loss of social sensitivity. Reasoning models also showed heterogeneous end-game behavior, ranging from sustained cooperation to systematic last-round defection effect, revealing model-specific exploitability profiles with direct practical relevance for deployment in negotiation and other multi-round interactions. Together, these findings characterize Gen AI models as increasingly sophisticated, though heterogeneous, social actors, and underscore the practical value of developing standardized cooperation benchmarks to inform the responsible deployment of Gen AI in interactive, socially consequential settings.

</details>


### [83] [HHR: Hierarchical Hash Retrieval for Efficient LLM Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01230)
*Lianjun Liu,Tiantian Zheng,You Huang,Weiqi Yan,Mingte Qiu,Huazhong Liu,Xiaofeng Zhu,Yunshan Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对哈希检索丢弃幅值导致的检索-注意力不匹配，HHR用头级正交路由做粗筛、学习投影做细排，从而同时减少假阳性并找回假阴性，带来更高的准确性与长上下文速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哈希检索高效但丢弃了特征幅值信息，汉明距离只反映方向相似性，导致检索中出现低logit的假阳性和高logit的假阴性，严重影响Sparse Attention的准确性与下游推理质量。

Method: 提出两步方法：Geometry-Aware Key Routing（GKR）——头维度的正交变换用于重分配特征在页面级别的对数边界以便粗粒度剪枝；Learned Hash Projection（LHP）——头维度的学习投影空间将汉明距离与真实Query-Key相关性排序对齐以实现细粒度检索。二者组合构成自上而下的粗到细哈希检索流程。

Result: 在多种LLM和基准上得到显著优于现有方法的结果：LongBench平均得分提升1.10点；在128K上下文长度下，对Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct解码最高加速达3.30×，端到端加速达2.83×；并开源了实现代码。

Conclusion: 本文提出的HHR通过粗到细的哈希检索框架，有效抑制了哈希稀疏注意力中的假阳性并恢复了假阴性，从而显著提高了哈希检索的保真度和长上下文推理性能。

Abstract: Efficient long-context inference is essential for large language models (LLMs), yet it poses a severe computational bottleneck. Hash-based retrieval offers an efficient alternative by encoding queries and keys into binary codes and using Hamming distance for key selection. However, this leads to a critical mismatch between Hamming distance and attention relevance. Query-Key logits depend jointly on directional similarity and feature magnitudes, whereas hash binarization discards magnitude information, causing both false-positive retrieval of low-logit keys and false-negative omission of high-logit keys. To address these failures, we propose Hierarchical Hash Retrieval (HHR), a coarse-to-fine framework that progressively improves retrieval accuracy through Geometry-Aware Key Routing (GKR) and Learned Hash Projection (LHP). GKR learns a head-wise orthogonal transformation to redistribute feature magnitudes and derive more discriminative page-level logit bounds, enabling effective pruning of low-logit keys while preserving important candidates. LHP then learns a head-wise projection space that aligns Hamming distance with the true Query-Key relevance ranking for fine-grained retrieval. By combining GKR and LHP, HHR suppresses false positives and recovers false negatives, substantially improving the fidelity of hash-based sparse attention. Extensive experiments across diverse LLMs and benchmarks demonstrate that HHR achieves superior performance over existing methods. For example, on LongBench, HHR improves the average score by 1.10 points and, at a context length of 128K, achieves up to a 3.30x decoding speedup and a 2.83x end-to-end speedup for Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/lianjunl13-sudo/HHR.

</details>


### [84] [Learning to Ask: Information Acquisition for SLM-LLM Collaboration, under a budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01236)
*Yongjun Kim,Xiaoxiao Li,Jaeho Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过三阶段RLVR使小模型在有限API预算下以选择性、目标化的查询利用大模型顾问，从而提升性能与成本之间的平衡，并具备跨顾问模型的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型协作多被视为计算分配问题，在黑盒API场景下因粗粒度委托与频繁上下文传输而效率低下。通过把问题视为信息获取，可用有限的API预算以更小的查询频率和更高的信息密度改善成本效益。

Method: 提出三阶段RLVR框架：学习何时调用顾问（调用决策）、如何构造有用查询（查询生成）、以及如何将顾问信息整合入推理过程并联合优化调用与信息使用（协同细化/联合微调）。SLM保持主导，向黑盒LLM发送目标化短查询而非整体任务委托。

Result: 在数学推理和编程任务上，相较现有协作基线方法提升了性能-成本权衡；在部分设置下达到了甚至超越了基于问题级路由的oracle性能。同时所学策略能在不再训练的情况下迁移到其他顾问模型家族。

Conclusion: 将SLM-LLM协作重新表述为信息获取问题，SLM作为主推理器仅在必要时向黑盒LLM顾问发出有针对性的查询，从而在API预算约束下提升性能-成本比。

Abstract: Collaboration between a small language model (SLM) and a large language model (LLM) offers an opportunity to combine the efficiency of smaller models with the strong reasoning capabilities of larger ones. Existing approaches primarily frame such collaboration as a computation allocation problem, determining which model should handle each portion of the reasoning process. In black-box API-based settings, however, this paradigm can be inefficient due to coarse-grained delegation or repeated transmission of context across model switches. In this work, we instead formulate SLM-LLM collaboration as an information acquisition problem, under an API budget constraint. The SLM remains the primary reasoner and selectively queries a black-box LLM advisor only when needed, issuing targeted queries rather than delegating the reasoning process itself. To realize this strategy, we develop a three-stage RLVR framework that learns whether to call the advisor, how to formulate useful queries, and how to integrate the collaboration into the reasoning process by jointly refining advisor invocation and information use. Across mathematical reasoning and coding tasks, our approach improves the performance--cost tradeoff over existing collaboration baselines and, in some settings, matches or exceeds oracle problem-level routing. Finally, we show that our strategy can transfer to other advisor model families, without further training.

</details>


### [85] [Revision-Aware Independent Agent Graphs for Dynamic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01249)
*Yan Luo,Selim-Antoine Lali,Jeremy Moebel,Iliass Khoutaibi,Ahmadou Aidara,Mengyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并系统评估了动态任务路由问题，设计了RIAG——一种边界明确的多代理缓存与审计机制——在大幅降低调用成本的同时显著提高联合路由与解答准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推理协议固定任务无法检验代理在事件流中是否正确传播更新、保留不受影响的工作或重构历史任务绑定，需研究能够处理任务绑定随时间变更的动态任务路由问题。

Method: 将六个广泛使用的基准（MMLU、MMLU-Pro、MedMCQA、MATH、GPQA、HumanEval）改造为动态任务路由问题，构造31119条动态情节和373428个按时间分类的查询；提出Revision-Aware Independent Agent Graph（RIAG）：以不可变文档标识进行缓存、为每个新任务提供两次未暴露的初始尝试、按条件触发审计与修复，最多对每个文档版本发起四次调用；评估同质与异质代理配置的性能与调用效率。

Result: 构建了规模化动态数据集（31119条情节，373428时序查询）；同质RIAG在0.62次/查询下实现54.24%的联合路由与回答准确率；对比方法在18.00次/查询下仅得32.22%；异质RIAG在0.63次/查询下达49.78%。

Conclusion: 作者证明了在动态任务绑定场景中，通过分离时间分辨（temporal resolution）与任务推理并采用受限的多智能体策略（RIAG），可以在显著降低调用次数的同时保持甚至提高联合路由与回答准确率，解决了重算与重用之间的核心权衡。

Abstract: Conventional reasoning protocols present a fixed, preselected task, so they cannot test whether an agent propagates relevant updates, preserves unaffected work, or reconstructs a historical task binding. We therefore study \emph{dynamic task routing}, in which an event stream revises task bindings and a system must select the document version valid at each query time before solving it. To study this problem, we repurpose six widely used benchmarks: MMLU, MMLU-Pro, MedMCQA, MATH, GPQA, and HumanEval into 31{,}119 dynamic episodes comprising 373{,}428 temporally categorized queries. This setting exposes a central trade-off: recomputing after every event wastes work, whereas unguarded reuse returns stale conclusions. We introduce the Revision-Aware Independent Agent Graph (RIAG), a bounded multi-agent policy that separates deterministic temporal resolution from task reasoning. RIAG caches solutions by immutable document identity, starts each fresh task with two unexposed attempts, and conditionally invokes audit and repair, using at most four calls per document version. On this collection, homogeneous RIAG achieves 54.24\% joint routing-and-answer accuracy at 0.62 calls/query, compared with 32.22\% at 18.00 calls/query for the strongest comparison method; heterogeneous RIAG reaches 49.78\% at 0.63 calls/query.

</details>


### [86] [DeFA: Dependency-Guided Failure Attribution for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01256)
*Bo Deng,Xinlei Zheng,Yi Wei,Kang Zhou,Chongyang Tao,Renzhao Liang,Xuanren Chen,Lifan Guo,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeFA以事件依赖与故障传播建模为核心，提供跨模态、可扩展的决定性错误定位方案，并能通过诊断反馈显著提升代理后续表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多步骤代理执行中，错误与可见后果之间可被多数步分隔，决定性错误的定位需要同时理解单步内容与步间依赖关系，从而准确归因并使代理能据此改进。

Method: 构建事件依赖图（结合协议关系和语义依赖）覆盖执行轨迹；识别可能违反任务要求的事件并追踪其来源与影响以生成故障传播图；结合步骤证据与在传播链中的角色判定决定性错误、责任代理与错误类别；对长轨迹进行分段处理，将当前段的详尽内容与其他段的摘要结合以获得本地诊断的全局上下文。

Result: 在Who and When及其Pro子集上，DeFA在责任主体与精确步骤准确率上优于所有评估后端；在Pro上对齐分类法的方法中获得最高的故障模式准确率；在图像与视频轨迹上也适用。消融实验证明分段、事件依赖图与故障传播图的有效性；将诊断反馈用于Trace2Skill可将下游任务精度提升6–15个百分点。

Conclusion: DeFA通过建立事件依赖图与故障传播图，在包含语义与协议关系的轨迹上定位决定性错误、责任主体与错误类型，从而实现可扩展、跨模态的执行失败归因并能推动后续技能改进。

Abstract: Errors in LLM agent executions and their visible consequences can be separated by many steps, making decisive-error localization a matter of understanding both step content and step dependencies. We introduce DeFA, a dependency-guided framework for agent failure attribution. DeFA first combines protocol relations and semantic dependencies into an event dependency graph spanning the trajectory. It then identifies events that may violate task requirements and traces their sources and subsequent effects to construct a failure propagation graph. Finally, DeFA uses step evidence and the steps' roles in failure propagation to identify the decisive error, responsible agent, and error category. To support long trajectories, DeFA partitions executions into segments and combines the current segment's detailed content with summaries of the other segments, giving local diagnosis access to global execution context. Across Who and When and the Who and When Pro text subset, DeFA achieves the highest responsible-agent and exact step accuracy with all evaluated backbones, and the highest failure-mode accuracy among taxonomy-aligned methods on Pro. Further experiments on image and video trajectories demonstrate its applicability to multimodal failure attribution. Ablations support the contributions of segmentation, the event dependency graph, and the failure propagation graph. Using DeFA's diagnostic feedback for skill evolution in Trace2Skill improves downstream task accuracy by 6-15 percentage points over the native pipeline, showing that the diagnoses can also support agent improvement on subsequent tasks.

</details>


### [87] [Feedback Without the Wait: Piloting a Generative AI Practice Platform in a Large Maths Class](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01262)
*Lili Chen,Gavin Buskes,Yuxin Ren,Chin Tong Leong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过把人工监督移到事前验证环节，GenAI可为大班工程课程提供可扩展且即时的练习反馈，学生接受度高但需严格验证、阈值与可解释性保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大班教学中及时且具体的反馈稀缺；GenAI可扩展地提供对话式反馈，但直接用于评分会产生信任与责任问题；需要在“即时性”与“人工保证信任”之间寻求实践性平衡。

Method: 设计并试点一套GenAI练习平台：平台在学生自学时提供即时、分层（scaffolded）反馈；人工角色从实时批改转为在事前抽样或针对边界案例验证平台生成的标准答案；通过观察学生使用行为与收集问卷/访谈来评估参与度、反馈感知与可迁移性。

Result: 试点显示学生愿意并频繁使用该平台，认为即时反馈对学习有帮助且通常可靠；仍存在AI不确定性、误导性反馈与对复杂/开放性问题的局限；转化到其他工程学科可行，但需要学科特定的验证与界面调整。

Conclusion: 在大班电气工程课程中，将生成式人工智能用于自学练习并把人工监督前置为解答验证，可以在保持即时、结构化反馈的同时减轻人工评分负担，提升学生参与度并被学生认为是有价值且可靠的辅导资源。

Abstract: Timely and specific feedback is one of the strongest influences on student learning, yet it is difficult to sustain in large electrical engineering classes where the ratio of students to demonstrators is high and a learner who is stuck may wait days to find out why an approach was wrong. Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) offers a way to scale conversational feedback, but using it to grade assessed work raises trust and accountability concerns, and keeping a human in the loop to assure its judgements reintroduces the very delay that erodes the value of feedback. The result is a tension between the immediacy that makes feedback so impactful and the human oversight that makes it trustworthy. In this work, we set out to resolve that tension in practice by designing and piloting a GenAI practice platform that delivers immediate, scaffolded feedback during self-directed practice. This relocates human oversight from real-time grading to the upfront verification of solutions. Our goal was to understand how students engaged with the tool, how they perceived the value and reliability of its feedback, and what lessons transfer to other engineering subjects.

</details>


### [88] [SCOPE-AD: Sequential cost-aware ordinal-belief planning with energy-based models for diagnostic agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01278)
*Ziwen Yu,Ivan Koychev,Elizabeth Coulthard,Ting Zhou,Bolin Chen,Dian Hong,Zinuo You,Yujiao Wang,Anthony Mulholland,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SCOPE-AD combines an ordinal, mask-aware uncertainty model with energy-based teacher distillation into a sequential policy to achieve near–full-modality diagnostic accuracy at a small fraction of the cost.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AD diagnosis requires sequential, cost-sensitive evidence collection across heterogeneous tests and patient burden; fixed-modality predictors cannot jointly decide which test to acquire or when to stop. A sequential cost-aware agent aims to minimize resource use while preserving diagnostic accuracy across the CN–MCI–AD ordering.

Method: A mask-aware ordinal model represents uncertainty along the ordered diagnostic continuum. Retrospective clinical records are used to sample Bellman targets that train an energy-based teacher model; the teacher’s action distributions are distilled into a Qwen policy. At deployment the agent sequentially chooses acquisition or diagnosis actions under availability and budget constraints, updating evidence and ordinal belief after each acquisition without access to unobserved values.

Result: On ADNI, SCOPE-AD achieves 77.70% Macro-F1 with average acquisition cost $50.46, outperforming the strongest evaluated baseline by 9.34 percentage points. Evaluating with full modalities raises Macro-F1 by only 1.89 points while increasing acquisition cost by 116.7×, indicating selective acquisition is highly cost-effective.

Conclusion: SCOPE-AD enables cost-aware sequential diagnostic decisions for the ordered CN–MCI–AD spectrum and yields substantially improved macro-F1 relative to baselines at much lower acquisition cost. Selective acquisition offers near-full-modality predictive performance at a fraction of the cost, supporting cost-effective diagnosis.

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) diagnosis requires sequential evidence acquisition under heterogeneous test costs and patient burden. Fixed-modality predictors do not jointly decide which test to acquire or when the available evidence is sufficient for diagnosis. We propose SCOPE-AD (Sequential Cost-Aware Ordinal-Belief Planning with Energy-Based Models for Diagnostic Agents) for cost-aware classification of cognitively normal (CN), mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and AD cases. A mask-aware ordinal model represents uncertainty along the ordered CN--MCI--AD continuum. Retrospective training records provide sampled Bellman targets for an energy-based teacher, whose action distributions are distilled into a Qwen policy. At deployment, the agent selects acquisition or diagnosis actions under availability and budget constraints without access to unacquired values. After each acquisition, the evidence and ordinal belief are updated before the next decision. On ADNI, SCOPE-AD achieves 77.70\% Macro-F1 at an average acquisition cost of \$50.46, exceeding the strongest evaluated baseline by 9.34 percentage points. Full-modality evaluation raises Macro-F1 by only 1.89 points while increasing acquisition cost by 116.7 times. These results support selective acquisition for cost-effective diagnosis.

</details>


### [89] [ITC-MoE: Importance-guided Token-aware Compression for MoE Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01296)
*Lianjun Liu,Shipeng Li,You Huang,Weiqi Yan,Mingte Qiu,Huazhong Liu,Xiaofeng Zhu,Yunshan Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ITC-MoE: importance-guided Tucker compression plus token-aware runtime adjustments that jointly adapt compression and inference to save compute/storage in MoE DLMs while preserving accuracy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing low-rank MoE compression uses static factorization and fixed rank allocation, ignoring cross-mode non-uniform redundancy and token-wise utilization variation in MoE DLMs.

Method: Proposes two components: Importance-guided Adaptive Tucker Compression (IATC) for multi-mode joint factorization with activation-and-gradient-based importance and adaptive rank allocation; Token-aware Compensation and Routing (TCR) for low-rank compensation of hot tokens and restricted routing for cold tokens.

Result: On SDAR-30B-A3B-Chat-b32, maintains 96.33% MultiArith accuracy under 30% compression and achieves up to 7.22x end-to-end speedup.

Conclusion: ITC-MoE effectively reduces MoE DLM computation and storage while preserving generation quality by adaptively compressing expert weights and applying token-aware runtime adjustments.

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) offer flexible parallel decoding and increased model capacity, but their large number of expert parameters incurs substantial computation and storage costs. Existing low-rank MoE compression methods largely rely on static factorization and fixed rank allocation, which overlook the distinctive properties of MoE DLMs. Specifically, we identify two properties: cross-mode non-uniform redundancy, where parameter redundancy and sensitivity to rank truncation vary across the input, output, and expert modes, and token-wise utilization variation, where hot and cold tokens exhibit distinct spectral characteristics and expert activation patterns. To address these challenges, we propose ITC-MoE, an Importance-guided Token-aware Compression framework for MoE DLMs. ITC-MoE consists of two complementary components. First, Importance-guided Adaptive Tucker Compression (IATC) incorporates activation and gradient importance into expert weight transformation, jointly factorizes expert weights across multiple modes, and adaptively allocates ranks under a fixed parameter budget. Second, Token-aware Compensation and Routing (TCR) applies lightweight low-rank compensation to compression-sensitive hot tokens and restricts the candidate expert set for cold tokens with concentrated routing patterns. By jointly adapting compression capacity and inference execution to both parameter redundancy and token-wise variation, ITC-MoE substantially reduces the computation and storage costs of MoE DLMs while preserving their generation quality. For example, on SDAR-30B-A3B-Chat-b32, ITC-MoE maintains an accuracy of 96.33% on MultiArith under a 30% compression budget, while achieving up to a 7.22x end-to-end speedup. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/lianjunl13-sudo/ITC-MoE.

</details>


### [90] [Questionnaire-Guided Disaggregation of Energy Appliance Use for Domestic Smart Meter Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01297)
*Achal Nanjundamurthy,Rupam Misra,Suzanne Little,Alan F. Smeaton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在30分钟分辨率的无子表数据上，用事件检测+问卷引导的混合无标签方法可以将总耗电分解为9类家电，且在多个基准与一个大规模实证数据集上取得了显著优于现有方法的整体分解误差与月度性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于爱尔兰大规模智能电表项目提供了覆盖80%+家庭、30分钟分辨率的负荷数据，但此分辨率不足以直接针对家电级别的耗电进行计量；在不能大规模布置子表/标注的现实约束下，发展一种无需大量标签即可实现家电分解并提供行为建议的可扩展方法。

Method: 方法由两部分组成：1) 针对高功率设备采用事件检测（从30分钟采样数据中识别明显的上/下跃变）；2) 利用问卷结果对低功率或不可分事件做估计和归类。最后将两者融合，输出9类家电的耗能分解和针对性节能/转时建议。

Result: 在一个带有商业对照的校准家庭、两个含子表的公开基准（UK-DALE、REFIT）以及一个包含2,968户、累计>4,800年用电数据的爱尔兰智能表数据集上进行评估。与两种独立系统比较，作者的方法在所有建筑上实现最低的whole-decomposition误差，并在按月聚合的54个配对月份上统计显著优于对照（p<0.001，Holm校正）。还产生了可操作的用电模式报告和基于设备的节能/错峰建议。

Conclusion: 该论文结论是：通过将高功率负载的事件检测与基于问卷的估计相结合的无标签（label-free）混合方法，可在30分钟分辨率的智能电表数据上，将总用电分解为9类家电负载，并在多套数据集（含大型爱尔兰智能电表样本和两个有子表的公开基准）上实现比两个独立开发系统更低的全分解误差及更好的月级性能（54个配对月显著优于对照，p<0.001，经Holm校正）。

Abstract: Ireland's smart metering programme records electricity use at 30-minute resolution, with smart meters installed in over 80\% of households as of late 2025. While this is useful for billing of smart, time-of-use tariffs, it is too coarse to capture use of domestic appliances. We present a label-free disaggregation system that breaks usage data into 9 appliance categories by combining event detection for high-power loads with questionnaire-guided estimation. Our evaluation draws on four datasets: a calibration household with a commercial comparator, two public benchmarks (UK-DALE and REFIT) with per-appliance sub-metering, and a smart meter dataset of more than 4,800 years of use from 2,968 Irish consumers. Compared against two independently developed disaggregation systems our hybrid method combining analysis of usage data with questionnaire results, achieves the lowest whole-decomposition error on all buildings across the datasets, with better month-level performance over 54 paired months ($p<0.001$, Holm-corrected). Our method provides useful advice on a household's energy consumption patterns and advice on how to reduce or shift usage on some appliances in order to reduce costs.

</details>


### [91] [DAYJOB: A Benchmark for Long-Horizon Professional Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01306)
*Stephanie Finley,Liudas Panavas,Thomas Mikkelson,Cam Hinton,Stacey Ganss,Bradley Monton,Emily Kendall,Michelle Spradlin,Lydia Bye,Michael O'Brien,Lauren Ylvisaker,Derek Ray,Suhaas Garre,Sushant Mehta,Edwin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DAYJOB提供了高复杂度、专业级的任务集与严格的全或无评判标准，暴露出当前模型在真实专业工作自动化上的显著短板与常见错误模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实专业工作常以简短、欠说明的请求为起点，专业人员需判断所需产出、相关文档及请求前提是否成立；评估当前模型在这类高复杂度、长时耗任务上的实用性和局限性。

Method: 构建并发布DAYJOB基准：130个容器化（Harbor）任务，来自医疗（50）和金融（80）领域；每个任务由专家设计二元通过标准（每任务中位标准数分别约为47.5和57.5）；使用代理式判卷器对提交文件按所有标准的“全通过”规则进行评估；在13个开发者、30种模型配置上运行评测。

Result: 在被测试的模型中，最强配置（Claude Opus 5.5）在医疗任务上通过率为24.7%，在金融上为23.9%；中位配置仅为0.6%（医疗）和2.5%（金融）。案例分析显示系统会保留错误前提并沿流程传播。公开资源包括全部医疗任务、部分金融任务（50/80）、评测框架与排行榜。

Conclusion: 当代大型模型和代理系统在模拟真实专业工作的复杂、开放式任务上表现有限：即使最强模型也仅通过约四分之一的尝试，常见失误包括接受与记录矛盾的前提并将错误输入贯穿至最终分析。

Abstract: Professional work often starts with a brief request that leaves the professional to work out what is needed, which documents matter, and whether the request's premise holds. We introduce DAYJOB, a benchmark of 130 tasks built by professionals in healthcare (50) and finance (80). The tasks are estimated to take a professional 13.6 hours on average in healthcare and 16.6 in finance. Each task is a containerized Harbor environment with an expert rubric of binary criteria (median 47.5 and 57.5 per task) that an agentic judge applies to the delivered files, and an attempt passes only if it meets every criterion. Across 30 model configurations from 13 developers, the strongest, Claude Opus 5.5, passes 24.7% of healthcare and 23.9% of finance attempts, and the median configuration passes 0.6% and 2.5%. In case studies, agents accept premises that the record contradicts and carry wrong inputs through otherwise consistent analyses. We release all healthcare tasks, 50 of the 80 finance tasks, the evaluation harness, and the leaderboard.

</details>


### [92] [ProtoFlow: Prototype-Guided Flow Matching for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01320)
*Shibo Feng,Wanjin Feng,Yang Qiu,Deheng Ye,Peilin Zhao,Chunyan Miao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用VQ码本构造先验并结合rectified flow实现非自回归潜在空间预测，从而在精度与效率上优于传统AR和高斯初始化的流匹配方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型尽管精确但推理昂贵；传统VQ式非迭代方法高效但常依赖自回归token生成导致训练-推理不一致；流匹配是非自回归的可行替代，但通常以通用高斯噪声为初始化，未利用VQ码本中的原型信息。

Method: 首先用VQ-自编码器把多元时序映射到离散潜在码字序列；基于训练得到的码本构造一个结构化的原型先验（prototype prior）；然后训练一个条件化的DiT-based rectified flow，将来自原型先验的样本运输到未来潜在表示（以历史观测为条件），从而在潜在空间内进行非自回归的未来序列生成与解码。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，ProtoFlow在预测精度和推理效率上均优于对比方法，训练收敛更快，消除了AR回滚不一致问题，并展示了使用码本先验替代高斯初始化在潜在流匹配中的有效性。

Conclusion: ProtoFlow通过将向量量化（VQ）得到的码本作为更具信息性的先验，结合基于DiT的修正流（rectified flow）进行流匹配，提出了一种非自回归的多元时间序列潜在空间预测框架，既避免了AR生成的暴露偏差，又相比基于高斯噪声的流匹配获得更快收敛和更优的预测性能。

Abstract: Generative modeling has shown strong promise for multivariate time mseries (MTS) forecasting, especially scale to high-dimensional settings. Diffusion-based methods achieve competitive performance but typically require many sampling steps at inference. VAE-based non-iterative forecasting frameworks have therefore emerged as an efficient alternative. Within this line of work, vector quantization (VQ) enables controllable latent space modeling by mapping multivariate series into compact discrete representations. Existing VQ-based forecasting methods, however, typically rely on autoregressive (AR) token generation, which suffers from exposure bias and training-inference mismatch. Flow matching provides an efficient non-autoregressive alternative for latent forecasting, but existing formulations usually initialize transport from a generic Gaussian prior. We instead observe that the trained VQ codebook already captures representative latent prototypes and can thus serve as a more informative prior for flow matching. Based on this insight, we propose ProtoFlow, a forecasting framework that combines vector-quantized autoencoding with Prototype-prior Flow matching. Our method first maps multivariate sequences into a discrete latent space, then constructs a structured prior from the learned codebook, and finally learns a DiT-based rectified flow to transport samples from this prior to future latent representations conditioned on historical observations. By replacing generic noise initialization with a learned prototype prior, ProtoFlow avoids the rollout mismatch of AR token prediction and promotes faster training convergence. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets show that it consistently achieves superior forecasting performance with efficient inference.

</details>


### [93] [TRACE: Trajectory Return Attribution and Contrastive Erasure for Multi-Turn Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01323)
*Fengpeng Li,Kemou Li,Qizhou Wang,Haiwei Wu,Jiantao Zhou,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出理论化单回合压制到多回合风险的上界，并设计代币级TRACE目标，通过对比擦除和折扣优势将拒绝信号传播到早期代币，从而显著降低多回合绕过成功率且几乎不损失能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好目标基于对单次提示的整体响应打分，不能保证在未见历史下的多回合风险控制；因此需要将监督性的单回合压制效果形式化为多回合轨迹风险的保证，并在训练中实现代币级信用分配以传播‘拒绝’信号。

Method: 理论上给出充分条件与一个关于覆盖率、迁移松弛（transfer slack）和泄露（leakage）的风险上界，随后提出TRACE——一种代币级目标：为安全回复的每个代币按折扣后的‘拒绝可归因优势’加权，该优势通过比较冻结的参考模型与其‘拒绝消融’版本得到；在被拒绝响应的高差位点，TRACE 在擦除目标中结合观察到的代币与策略选择的替代项，并用梯度范数惩罚代替保留集。

Result: 在五个开源权重模型和七种多回合攻击上，TRACE 在所有35个模型-攻击对中实现了最低的攻击成功率（ASR），而在MMLU与HellaSwag上的性能下降最多仅为1.23点；源码随附。

Conclusion: TRACE 将单回合的拒绝信号以代币级目标传播到多回合轨迹上，有效降低多回合绕过（jailbreak）成功率，同时对模型能力影响极小。

Abstract: Safety-aligned large language models (LLMs) often refuse a harmful request but comply once the same goal is spread over several turns. Preference objectives score whole responses to single prompts, so their training loss alone cannot control risk on unseen histories. Our analysis gives sufficient conditions under which suppression at supervised single-turn contexts yields a bound on multi-turn trajectory risk. The bound accounts for coverage, transfer slack, and leakage, and characterizes contraction relative to a base-policy risk budget evaluated on the trained policy's contexts. TRACE (Trajectory Return Attribution and Contrastive Erasure) turns this principle into a token-level objective. On the safe response, each token is weighted by the discounted return of a refusal-attributable advantage. The advantage compares a frozen reference model with its refusal-ablated copy, allowing earlier response tokens to receive credit from later refusal-related evidence. At high-gap positions on rejected responses, TRACE combines the observed token with policy-selected alternatives in the erasure target. A gradient-norm penalty replaces the retain set. Across five open-weight models and seven multi-turn attacks, TRACE gives the lowest attack success rate (ASR) in all 35 model and attack pairs, while the model utility evaluated on MMLU and HellaSwag drop by at most 1\.23 points. Source code can be found in the supplemental material.

</details>


### [94] [PPO-HRAP: Proximal Policy Optimization with a Hybrid Regime-Aware Policy for Risk-Controlled Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01325)
*Duong Hien Chi Kien,Thanh Trung Huynh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 把PPO动作与一个VIX条件的可解释制度先验混合，能在SPY样本上提高风险调整收益并显著降低回撤，但需进一步检验换手率、交易成本和跨资产鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单纯追求利润的RL策略易在上升漂移资产上退化为被动多头，而强烈的风险惩罚又会在高波动期过度防御；为平衡收益参与与回撤控制，提出混合制度先验的方法。

Method: 使用PPO作为学习主体，观测市场特征与组合状态；设计复合奖励：组合对数收益、以VIX为条件的回撤增加惩罚、目标敞口偏差惩罚及换手成本；根据可解释的制度先验导出目标敞口，并将PPO actor输出与该目标按权重混合后执行交易。

Result: 在2020–2022年留出期的SPY测试上，PPO-HRAP实现总回报27.62%、年化8.48%、Sharpe 0.6447、Sortino 0.8588、Calmar 0.4592，最大回撤降至18.47%（相比买入并持有的34.10%）；5个随机种子平均总回报0.2725±0.0109，平均Sharpe 0.6219±0.0565；对QQQ和DIA的单次运行显示在三资产中总回报和Sharpe均为最高。局限包括高换手率和跨资产仅有单次证据。

Conclusion: 将PPO策略输出与一个基于波动率的可解释制度先验按比例混合，能在保留上涨参与的同时显著降低回撤并提升风险调整后收益，但仍存在高换手率和跨资产稳健性证据有限的问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for trading often struggles to balance upside participation with drawdown control. Profit-only policies can collapse toward passive long exposure on upward-drifting assets, while aggressively risk-penalized rewards can become too defensive during volatile periods. This paper proposes PPO-HRAP, a hybrid regime-aware policy that combines Proximal Policy Optimization with an interpretable regime prior. The agent observes both market features and portfolio-state variables, receives a reward combining portfolio log return, VIX-conditioned drawdown-increase penalty, target-exposure deviation, and turnover cost, and executes a blended action between the PPO actor output and a regime-derived target exposure. On the held-out 2020-2022 SPY test window, PPO-HRAP achieves 27.62% total return, 8.48% annualized return, 0.6447 Sharpe ratio, 0.8588 Sortino ratio, and 0.4592 Calmar ratio, while reducing maximum drawdown from 34.10% for Buy and Hold to 18.47%. Across five SPY seeds, PPO-HRAP remains stable with mean total return $0.2725 \pm 0.0109$ and mean Sharpe ratio $0.6219 \pm 0.0565$. Single-run cross-asset tests on QQQ and DIA further show that the proposed method ranks first on total return and Sharpe ratio for all three reported assets. These results suggest that blending learned actions with a volatility-aware regime prior is a practical way to improve risk-adjusted trading behavior, although the current policy still incurs high turnover and cross-asset robustness beyond SPY remains limited to single-run evidence.

</details>


### [95] [An ontology for cross-sectoral crisis management: core and public health modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01326)
*Aldo Gangemi,Rita T. Sousa,Luigi Asprino,Giorgia Lodi,Andrea G. Nuzzolese,Valentina Presutti,Johannes Gysen,Diana F. Sousa,Luigi Spagnolo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ECMO 提供了一个模块化、可扩展的跨部门危机管理 OWL 本体，已完成公共卫生对齐并在 EIOS 中演示了从新闻到知识图谱的端到端应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前灾害管理与响应涉及多学科、异构数据源和多种编码体系，缺乏一个统一、跨部门的参考本体以支持数据互操作、语义整合和自动化推理；ECMO 旨在填补这一空缺。

Method: 采用模块化设计（ECMO-CORE + 若干领域模块），在核心模块中建模 hazard、event、exposure、impact、response measure 等基本概念；使用本体设计模式和 OWL2 punning 处理危害类型与事件表现之间的歧义；为公共卫生领域编写对齐模块并映射到 SNOMED CT 与 ICD-11；实现从非结构化流行病学新闻到 ECMO 合规知识图谱的端到端管道（在 EIOS 系统中演示）。

Result: 产出一个可公开获取的模块化 OWL 本体集合（含核心与领域模块）、在公共卫生语义对齐上的具体实现以及一个将非结构化事件新闻转换为本体驱动知识图谱的原型管线；初步结果表明 ECMO 能为一致性表示和数据整合提供形式保障。

Conclusion: ECMO 是一个模块化的 OWL 本体，旨在作为跨部门的灾害风险降低与响应参考，能够为异构危机相关数据提供一致的语义框架；通过与流行的公共卫生编码（SNOMED CT、ICD-11）对齐并在 JRC 的 EIOS 系统上实现端到端知识图谱填充，证明了其实用性与可复用性，已公开发布并采用 CC BY 4.0 许可。

Abstract: This paper presents the European Crisis Management Ontology (ECMO), a modular OWL-based ontology intended as a cross-sectoral reference for disaster risk reduction and response. ECMO is designed to be organised as a network of ontological modules. Among the modules, ECMO-CORE captures fundamental crisis management concepts such as hazard, event, exposure, impact, and response measure and uses ontology design patterns and the OWL2 punning technique to resolve ambiguities between hazard types and event manifestations. In addition, domain-specific modules are defined as in the case of the public health module aligned with SNOMED CT and ICD-11. To demonstrate the resource's utility, we used ECMO to represent the data of the Epidemic Intelligence from Open Sources system of the Joint Research Centre to generate an end-to-end pipeline that populates an ECMO-compliant knowledge graph from unstructured epidemiological news.
