Initial results demonstrate that ECMO provides the formal guardrails necessary for consistent and unified knowledge representation and integration. The ontology is publicly available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20070268 and is released under the Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 International (CC BY 4.0) license.

</details>


### [96] [Verify Claims, Not Scores: Evidence-Based Verification of Modular Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01348)
*Ali Atiah Alzahrani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种对模块化智能体按结论打证据的验证审计方法，并用三种工具诊断改进可达性、价值丢失来源和验证器效用；在投资组合示例中显示若干实际误区，贡献为协议而非普适结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常用的整体任务分数无法说明是否存在可达改进、哪个组件丢失了价值或智能体自检证据的含义；需要一种将结论与对应证据和适用边界绑定的审计方法以便有针对性地改进和比较组件。

Method: 设计三类证据工具并组合成审计流程：1) 在显式动作集下用oracle策略量化可达改进；2) 逐个将组件替换为完美同僚以定位价值损失（若下游遮罩则判未决）；3) 单独测试运行时验证器的度量是否确实在被其读作上界的量上有识别能力。将该协议应用于受约束的投资组合分配智能体和带已知隐含情景的合成市场以示范。

Result: 审计结果表明：完美情景信息的价值依赖于用于测量它的动作集；场景生成器在多数情形下丢弃了大部分情景信号，而提高局部保真度并未改善决策；运行时验证器可被绕过而不改变可观察结果。总体贡献在于审计协议和证据-裁决的区分，且具体实证发现仅适用于所研究的智能体和环境。

Conclusion: 提出并验证了一种面向命题的验证审计协议：对模块化智能体不评分整体任务，而是对每条结论给出证据、四类裁决（支持/不支持/未决/未评估）及其适用边界，从而能区分可达改进、价值丢失源和验证器本身的有效性。

Abstract: When developers change one component of an agent, such as its controller, a learned model or its verifier, they usually judge the change by an aggregate task score. That score cannot tell whether improvement was attainable, which component lost value, or what the agent's own checks certify. We introduce a claim-specific verification audit for modular agents that plan, act, check and refine. Instead of scoring the agent, the audit scores the evidence: each conclusion is recorded with the evidence behind it, one of four verdicts (supported, unsupported, unresolved or not evaluated) and the boundary within which it holds. Three tools supply that evidence. Oracle policies measure attainable improvement under an explicitly stated action set, so that a low value can be traced to the evaluation rather than to the environment. Replacing one component at a time with a perfect counterpart locates lost value, with null results read as unresolved whenever a downstream component could mask them. A separate test asks whether the verifier's score identifies the quantity it is read as bounding. Applied to a constrained portfolio-allocation agent in a synthetic market with known hidden regimes, the audit shows that the value of perfect regime information depends on the action set used to measure it, that the scenario generator discards most of the regime signal while better local fidelity does not improve decisions, and that the runtime verifier can be bypassed with no visible change in outcomes. The contribution is the protocol and the evidential distinctions it enforces; the empirical findings are specific to the agent and environment studied.

</details>


### [97] [Generation Provenance Before Behavior Attribution: Auditing Synthetic Speech Research Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01378)
*Sidi Chang,Peiying Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并审计了一种合成数据生成溯源基底，证明溯源是构建可审计因果候选图的必要条件，但并不足以证明合成训练数据对模型行为的因果贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To accurately attribute model behavior to synthetic training data, one must know how each training item was produced; waveform-label pairs lack necessary provenance to support attribution.

Method: Design a generation-provenance substrate that binds source specification, generated content, waveform, target, fact requirements, quality signals, review lineage, and immutable manifest identity; audit the substrate on a private Japanese care-handoff TTS pipeline with 113 synthetic assets across six scenario families, inspecting linked audio, transcripts, candidate notes, and fact checklists.

Result: The substrate supports richer provenance and auditability; two faithful-only manifests were scenario-seed-disjoint and versioned, but exact upstream attribution was impeded by floating generator aliases, missing per-clip TTS/code stamps, and an unversioned checking prompt. The work yields a provenance contract, an audit protocol, and a bounded case study, without claiming causal attribution or clinical validity.

Conclusion: Generation provenance alone cannot prove causal attribution of model behavior; it defines candidate causal graphs and audit units, but causal claims require frozen training runs and intervention/influence evidence.

Abstract: Attributing model behavior to synthetic training data requires knowing what produced each training item before estimating what that item caused. A waveform-label pair does not preserve this knowledge. We propose a generation-provenance substrate in which a synthetic research object binds source specification, generated content, waveform, target, fact requirements, quality signals, review lineage, and immutable manifest identity. Producer and selection mechanism determine evidentiary meaning; storage location and variable name do not. We audit this substrate in a private Japanese care-handoff pipeline. A 113-asset review population contains 1.552 hours of synthetic speech across six scenario families; all items have linked audio, transcripts, candidate notes, and fact checklists, but human evidence is selective and source-specific. Two faithful-only manifests are scenario-seed-disjoint and immutably versioned, while exact upstream attribution remains blocked by floating generator aliases, missing per-clip TTS and code stamps, and an unversioned checking prompt. We argue that generation provenance is necessary but not sufficient for behavior attribution: it defines the candidate causal graph and audit units, whereas contributive attribution still requires frozen training runs and intervention or influence evidence. The paper contributes a compact provenance contract, an audit protocol, and a bounded case study for synthetic-data attribution; controlled research access may be offered, but we do not claim causal training-data attribution, clinical validity, or unrestricted public release.

</details>


### [98] [Gacha Decoding: Eliciting Diverse Generations Through Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01382)
*Scott Geng,Yufei Zhang,Joseph Lee,Jerry Li,Marjan Ghazvininejad,Pang Wei Koh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Gacha Decoding用指令+外部随机源（“计划与骰子”）替代token熵驱动的采样，能在更少样本下从强能力LM中高效挖掘更多高质量多样生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于token熵或标准采样的多样性方法随模型能力提高反而产生更低多样性，且需要大量样本去覆盖高质量模态。作者希望找到一种随模型能力提升而能获得更多高质量、互异模态的做法。

Method: 在推理阶段不依赖模型的token熵，而是用模型的指令跟从能力去执行带随机性的外部指令（“用骰子计划”），通过外部RNG和精心设计的指令空间可扩展地识别并实现不同响应模态。

Result: 在多领域（聊天、创造性写作、图像生成规划、蛋白质设计）上，Gacha Decoding在相同比质量下显著优于现有方法（Vendi指标最高2.4倍），以11倍更少样本达到相同高质量模态数量，并发现了其他方法未覆盖的新模态；且模型能力越强，多样性表现越好。

Conclusion: 作者提出的Gacha Decoding通过把多样性视为“按指令产生不同模态”的问题，并结合外部随机数生成器，在推理时高效地从语言模型中挖掘多样生成，从而逆转以往随模型能力提高而多样性下降的趋势。

Abstract: We introduce Gacha Decoding, an inference-time method for eliciting diverse language model generations that scales with model capability. Across open-ended domains (in-the-wild chat, creative writing, planning for image generation, and protein design), Gacha Decoding significantly outperforms existing generation diversity approaches at equal quality (up to 2.4x Vendi over the next-best prior approach), reaching the same number of high-quality modes with over an order of magnitude fewer samples (11.0x) and discovering novel modes that no other approach surfaces. Our key insight is to treat diversity as an instruction-following problem: rather than relying on the LM's token entropy, we combine its instruction-following capability with randomness from an external RNG tool to scalably identify and realize distinct modes of the response space. This approach of "planning with dice" enables Gacha to invert the long-observed tension between diversity and model capability. As the underlying LM becomes a better instruction follower, diversity under Gacha Decoding consistently improves--even as its token entropy and diversity under prior approaches decline. Together, our results highlight that instruction following, rather than token entropy alone, can drive generation diversity.

</details>


### [99] [PRISM: A Category-Theoretic Framework for Measuring and Refining Multimodal Analogies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01383)
*Mirella Zeisler,Ojas Shirekar,Mircea Licǎ,Chirag Raman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PRISM 提出可解释的 pullback 得分与基于该得分的迭代精修，能有效提升视觉隐喻生成的关系对齐度，但仍存在偏向视觉拥挤等局限，需更多评估与方法学改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态类比生成缺乏可解释的量化指标来判断生成物是否保持或理解原始关系结构，导致很难评估或改进模型在保持语义/关系一致性上的表现。

Method: 基于范畴论的显式关系映射，将类比表示为图结构；使用视觉语言模型（VLM）跨模态实现这些结构；定义 pullback 得分以衡量图间的关系对齐；并用该得分作为 in-context 反馈，在迭代循环中对生成的图像进行修正。

Result: 在 AnaloBench 上，仅用 pullback 得分选择正确类比的准确率为 82.5%；迭代精修在 VLM 判决与人工评估上均优于零样本生成，人类参与者在 57.65% 的两两对比中偏好精修输出；但质性分析指出精修可能偏向视觉元素密集的构图而非真实的关系深度。

Conclusion: PRISM 提供了一个可解释的、模态不可知的框架，通过“pullback 得分”量化并改进多模态类比中的关系对齐，在视觉隐喻生成上表现出可观的选择与改进效果，但精修可能偏向视觉拥挤而非真正的关系深度。

Abstract: Analogical reasoning involves identifying and preserving relational structures across domains. However, existing approaches to AI-driven multimodal analogy generation lack an interpretable measure of whether this structure is understood and maintained in the generated output. We address this gap with Pullback Refinement via Interpretable Structural Mapping (PRISM), a modality-agnostic framework for measuring and improving relational alignment in multimodal analogies, evaluated on visual metaphor generation. PRISM represents analogies as explicit relational mappings grounded in category theory and uses VLMs to instantiate these structures across modalities. Its first component, the pullback score, quantifies relational alignment from the resulting graph representation. On the AnaloBench benchmark, selecting the correct analogy purely by pullback score achieves 82.5% accuracy, demonstrating that the score captures meaningful relational information. PRISM's second component is an iterative refinement loop that uses the pullback score as an in-context feedback signal to iteratively revise the generated image towards greater relational depth. VLM-as-a-judge and human evaluations show that PRISM consistently improves metaphor consistency and analogy appropriateness over zero- shot generation, with human participants preferring the refined output in 57.65% of pairwise comparisons. However, a qualitative analysis reveals that refinement can favour visually crowded compositions rather than genuinely deeper relational correspondences.

</details>


### [100] [AiSearch: Interactive Multi-Modal Search with VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01389)
*Ali Koksal,Mei Chee Leong,Vicky Sintunata,Ching Ling Chin,Wee Teck Fong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AiSearch is a multimodal retrieval framework using VLM zero-shot capabilities for interactive natural-language search over images and videos, supporting user feedback and multi-model benchmarking; abstract lacks implementation and evaluation specifics.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Need for automated, interactive retrieval systems that support real-time search and refinement over images and videos and allow selecting suitable VLMs.

Method: Leverages Vision Language Models’ zero-shot natural language image/video search; supports interactive refinement via user feedback; provides visual benchmarking across multiple VLMs to compare models.

Result: Proposed system enables natural language search over images/videos with interactive feedback and model benchmarking; no quantitative results provided in abstract.

Conclusion: AiSearch shows potential as a flexible, multimodal retrieval framework that leverages VLM zero-shot capabilities for interactive image/video search and model benchmarking, but the abstract lacks methodological and evaluation details.

Abstract: Modern retrieval systems must both be automated and interactive, allowing users to search and refine results in real time. We present AiSearch, a flexible multimodal retrieval framework that leverages the zero shot capabilities of Vision Language Models (VLMs) for natural language search over images and videos. AiSearch supports interactive search refinement through user feedback to tailor results to the user's intent, and allows visual benchmarking across multiple VLMs, enabling users to select the most suitable model for their task.

</details>


### [101] [Contrastive Attention Mitigates Spectral Bias in Spiking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01403)
*Xiaoli Liu,Malu Zhang,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过频谱分析发现脉冲模块的低通效应削弱高频信息，提出SCA（全局对比聚合+局部差分精化）来恢复高频，从而在多任务上提升Spiking Transformer的性能和能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到Spiking Transformer在性能上落后于ANNs，虽有观点归因于二值激活，但作者通过频谱角度提出真正问题是脉冲组件与SSA的低通特性导致高频信息丢失；受生物视觉系统边缘检测与差分感知启发，提出增强高频的方案以缩小性能差距。

Method: 基于多尺度频谱分析证明脉冲神经元与脉冲自注意力均表现为低通滤波器（导致高频衰减）；设计SCA模块：1) 全局对比聚合（global contrastive aggregation）提取对比原型，强调边缘/差分信息；2) 局部差分精化（local differential refinement）在局部尺度恢复高频细节；将SCA作为通用替换模块插入Spiking Transformer并在多种任务上评估，同时对复杂度及能效进行对比分析。

Result: 实验显示SCA能在图像分类、语义分割与事件驱动跟踪中稳健提升性能，并在复杂度与能耗上优于原SSA，证明SCA作为构件具有通用性与实用性。

Conclusion: 提出的Spiking Contrastive Attention (SCA) 解决了脉冲Transformer中低通效应导致高频信息耗散的问题，通过全局对比聚合提取对比原型并结合局部微分精化来增强高频成分，从而显著提升模型在分类、语义分割和事件跟踪任务上的性能与能效，且复杂度低于原有的脉冲自注意力（SSA）。

Abstract: Spiking Transformers merge the energy-efficiency of spiking neural networks (SNNs) with the representational power of self-attention, creating a promising architecture for high-performance, energy-efficient computation. However, a performance gap persists versus its counterparts in artificial neural networks (ANNs). Unlike prior works attributing this to binary activations, we reveal that both spiking neurons and spiking self-attention (SSA) act as low-pass filters through multiscale spectral analysis. This characteristic leads to the dissipation of high-frequency components. To address this issue, we propose the Spiking Contrastive Attention (SCA) paradigm, which draw inspiration from the edge-detection and differential sensing properties of biological visual system. By extracting contrast prototypes via global contrastive aggregation and applying local differential refinement, SCA effectively enhances high-frequency information. Extensive experiments show that SCA is a general module that consistently boosts Spiking Transformers across image classification, semantic segmentation, and event-based tracking. Furthermore, it achieves lower complexity, offering superior efficiency over original SSA. These results establish its potential as a fundamental building block for energy-efficient Spiking Transformers.

</details>


### [102] [Beyond Memory: Harnessing Long-Horizon Agents with Explicit Belief States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01415)
*Yu Luo,Jiamin Jiang,Yimin Zuo,Xidao Wen,Rongchen Gao,Yongqian Sun,Shenglin Zhang,Guiyang Liu,Cheng Zhang,Fang Situ,Qi Zhou,Dan Pei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PoS通过显式信念构建、一致性验证与定制恢复，有效避免代理的停滞行为并提升长程任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理仅以交互历史或压缩记忆组织上下文，难以保证对当前世界的连贯理解与对未完成任务的显式追踪，从而导致无效或循环的行为。

Method: 在推理阶段为代理维护显式的信念状态（包含当前世界估计与未解决的任务需求），对信念进行一致性校验与进度监控，以检测并基于陷阱模式与需求类型执行定制化恢复策略。

Result: 在四个覆盖执行与诊断任务的基准上，PoS在所有三个LLM骨干模型上均取得最高总体性能。消融研究表明一致性校验与恢复模块至关重要；上下文规模扩展试验显示对上下文增长具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PoS证明在推理时构建并维护显式信念状态，是提升长程任务管理与上下文利用的有效方法，能减轻“信念陷阱”并提高多基座LLM上的任务完成率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can now undertake increasingly complex tasks, but the way they organize interaction history into memory does not ensure a coherent understanding of the current world. We introduce PoS, an inference-time framework that constructs and continually maintains explicit belief states as the agent's decision context. Each belief combines an estimate of the current world state with unresolved task requirements, making explicit what the agent still needs to learn and accomplish. To keep this belief reliable and actionable, PoS validates its consistency and monitors task progress to detect Belief Trapping, where the agent continues to act without making meaningful progress toward the goal. Recovery is then tailored to both the trapping pattern and the type of unresolved task requirement. Experiments on four benchmarks spanning execution and diagnosis show that PoS achieves the highest overall performance on every benchmark with all three LLM backbones. Ablations demonstrate the importance of consistency validation and recovery, while context-scaling experiments show resilience to context growth. Together, these results support belief construction and continual maintenance as a foundation for long-horizon context management beyond history retention and compression.

</details>


### [103] [SpikeMoE: Brain-Inspired Competitive Routing for Flexible Spiking Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01418)
*Xiaoli Liu,Yujie Liang,Jialin Li,Malu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将生物学启发的尖峰路由（k-WTA）与 MoE 结合，提出 SpikeMoE 并用两阶段缺模建模应对不完整多模态输入，实验表明其在精度与能效上具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结合 SNN 的事件驱动能效优势与 MoE 的条件计算稀疏性，设计在尖峰层面实现专家选择的机制以构建灵活且能效友好的神经架构，同时应对多模态输入缺失问题。

Method: 提出基于生物启发的 spike-based k-WTA 路由器（含侧向抑制和不应期）按离散尖峰计数选择 top-K 专家；在此基础上构建 SpikeMoE 框架并为缺失模态场景设计两阶段缺模建模：从观测模态池抽取经验原型并与模态专属可学习嵌入组合以构造缺失模态表示。

Result: 在视觉、语言和多模态基准上，SpikeMoE 在 SNN 基线中达到或接近最优，并能与部分 ANN 对手匹配或优于其性能；在不同缺模条件下保持稳健；展示了性能与能效间良好折衷。

Conclusion: SpikeMoE successfully integrates spiking neuronal dynamics with sparse Mixture-of-Experts routing, showing competitive or superior accuracy to ANN baselines while maintaining improved energy-efficiency trade-offs among SNN methods.

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) enable event-driven computation through biologically inspired dynamics at the neuronal scale, while Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) perform conditional computation through expert selection at the model scale. Integrating their strengths offers potential for flexible neural architectures. A key challenge, however, lies in designing an expert selection mechanism based on spiking activity. To address this, we introduce a spike-based k-WTA Router inspired by competition-inhibition observed in the hippocampal CA1 region. The router incorporates lateral inhibition and refractory period to select Top-K experts according to discrete spike counts. Building on this, we present SpikeMoE, a framework that integrates neuronal-scale spiking dynamics with model-scale expert selection. To address incomplete multisensory inputs in multimodal tasks, we further equip SpikeMoE with a two-stage missing-modality modeling module that combines empirical prototypes from an observed-modality pool with modality-specific learnable embeddings to construct missing-modality representations. Experiments on vision, language, and multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that SpikeMoE achieves state-of-the-art performance among the SNN baselines, matches or exceeds the performance of ANN counterparts, and maintains robustness across diverse missing-modality conditions. These results demonstrate a favorable trade-off between performance and energy efficiency, validating the integration of spiking dynamics with sparse expert computation and highlighting SpikeMoE as a promising approach to energy-efficient brain-inspired computing.

</details>


### [104] [A Deterministic and Auditable AI Security Risk Assessment Framework with ATLAS Aligned Executable Rules and Formal Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01436)
*Yixuan Huang,Basel Halak,Boojoong Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并形式化验证了一个基于证据的、确定性AI安全评估框架，能把工程证据映射到技术级可行性/影响评分，实验显示加固可降低可行性，但关键控件缺失会留下残余风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI安全评估多依赖叙述性清单或评估者打分，缺乏从可观测工程产物到稳定技术级结论的显式、可机器评估的映射，导致难以在审计中重现与辩护。需要一个确定性、可追溯且可重评的评估流程。

Method: （1）定义项目无关的Control ID分类并在四级序数尺度上对异构证据归一化；（2）从固定的MITRE ATLAS快照编译技术级谓词，通过显式的缓解→控件映射生成可评估的条件；（3）把所有规范性选择封装为版本化的评估策略对象；（4）对编译器/评估器在声明分数域上进行形式化证明（有界、完备、有序一致、单调）；（5）在五个开源仓库快照上评估，应用统一的加固干预并通过SBOM与CI门禁验证对工程变化的响应。

Result: 实现了一个可版本化、可验证的评估器，能把异构证据映射到技术可行性/影响等级并保留追溯链；实证上在控件加强后总体可行性下降，验证了敏感性；但当关键技术控件超出证据采集范围或未被观测到时，仍会出现残余高可行性的最坏情况。

Conclusion: 提出了一个证据驱动的AI安全评估框架，将评估操作化为确定性决策函数，能把异构工程证据归一化到独立于项目的Control ID分类并在四级序数尺度上评分，基于固定的MITRE ATLAS快照把缓解措施映射为技术级谓词，输出可追溯到触发证据的技术可行性与影响等级。通过版本化评估策略对象支持可重复评估，并对评估器在声明的分数域上形式化验证有界性、完备性、有序语义一致性和单调性。基于五个开源项目快照的实验表明：在可观测控件强化后，可行性概况普遍向下移动，但若关键技术控件缺失仍存在最坏情形的残余可行性。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems are increasingly deployed in high impact and safety critical settings, yet security assessment remains difficult to reproduce and defend under audit. Existing approaches often rely on narrative checklists or assessor driven scoring, and they lack an explicit, machine evaluable mapping from observable engineering artefacts to stable technique level outcomes. We present an evidence driven AI security assessment framework that operationalises assessment as a deterministic decision function. The framework normalises heterogeneous artefacts into a project independent Control ID taxonomy scored on a bounded four level ordinal scale, compiles technique level predicates from a pinned MITRE ATLAS snapshot via an explicit mitigation to control mapping, and outputs technique indexed feasibility and impact levels with traceable links back to the triggering evidence. We package all normative choices as a versioned assessment policy object to support repeatable reassessment across snapshots. To ensure semantic correctness, we formally verify boundedness, totality, ordered semantic consistency, and monotonicity of the compiled evaluator over the full declared score domain. We evaluate the framework on five public open source AI projects pinned to explicit repository snapshots, quantify before and after changes under a unified hardening intervention, and validate responsiveness to real engineering changes through fork based implementations of Software Bill of Materials (SBOM) generation and Continuous integration (CI) security scanning gates. Results show consistent downward shifts in feasibility profiles under strengthened observable controls, while worst case residual feasibility persists when technique specific core controls remain absent from the evidence scope.

</details>


### [105] [DRelay: Global Draft Context for Prefix-Aware Parallel Speculative Decoding Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01439)
*Zhuoyu Wang,Junnan Huang,Xinyu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DRelay 用全局阅读与因果选择的前缀感知修复，在验证前修正草拟块中的早期选择错误，通过联合训练显著提高接受前缀长度与端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行草拟能减轻投机解码的开销，但受限于“已接受前缀”的长度：早期单点选择错误会阻断后续可用预测。需要在模型验证前利用整个草拟块的全局信息，对候选选择进行前缀感知的修复，从而扩展有效前缀并提升速度与准确性。

Method: DRelay 在草拟块（draft block）上使用两部分：一个“全局阅读器”（global reader）跨位置提取每个候选项的预测信息，和一个“因果选择器”（causal selector）将候选级信息与先前已选 token 的路径结合，用于判断当前原生选择(token)是否与全局证据和已选前缀一致，从而决定保留或替换。训练上对草拟主干和选择器进行联合训练，结合候选支持学习与按位置加权的修复目标。

Result: 在 H800 GPU 上 8 个不同基准上，相较于 DFlash、Domino、DSpark，DRelay 在平均接受长度和端到端解码性能上持续改进；在 SGLang 部署下，平均端到端加速分别优于 DFlash 14.7%–16.8%、Domino 8.7%–9.3%、DSpark 8.1%–9.3%。

Conclusion: DRelay 提出了一种基于全局信息的前缀感知选择性修复机制，通过在验证前修正并扩展已接受的前缀，从而缓解并行草拟（parallel drafting）受早期选择错误限制的问题。实验证明它在多个基准和部署场景下显著提升了平均接受前缀长度和端到端加速。

Abstract: Parallel drafting reduces the drafting overhead of speculative decoding for large language models (LLMs), but its gains remain limited by the accepted prefix length. Even when the correct token is present in the candidate pool, a single early selection error prevents subsequent predictions from being used. We propose DRelay, which uses global information from the entire draft block to perform prefix-aware selective repair of candidate selections before target-model verification. DRelay bases its decisions on candidate correlations and the selected path: a global reader extracts predictive information across positions for each candidate. While a causal selector combines candidate-level information extracted by the global read with the tokens selected at preceding positions to determine whether the native choice at the current position is consistent with the global evidence and the selected prefix. It then decides whether to retain or replace the token, thereby repairing early errors and extending the accepted prefix. We further jointly train the draft backbone and the selector, combining candidate-support learning with a repair objective, while weighting the repair loss according to each block position's potential contribution to the consecutive accepted prefix. Across eight diverse benchmarks on an H800 GPU, DRelay consistently improves both average acceptance length and end-to-end decoding performance over DFlash, Domino, and DSpark. Under SGLang serving, DRelay improves average end-to-end speedup over DFlash, Domino, and DSpark by 14.7%-16.8%, 8.7%-9.3%, and 8.1%-9.3%, respectively.

</details>


### [106] [A Multi-Agent LLM Framework for Personalized Health Checkup Interpretation and Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01451)
*HyungJun Kim,Taehan Lee,Soojin Cheon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多代理体系能显著提升复合体检解读的实用性与一致性，尤其在需个人记录检索的任务上，但未显著改进医疗安全与数值可靠性，且带来更高延迟和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人化体检结果解读需要跨越纵向病历、医学知识、生活方式建议与就医导航等多种任务，单一通用模型可能难以同时兼顾所有子任务的专业性与一致性。

Method: 构建一个多代理框架：对输入识别多个意图，将每个意图映射到任务专门代理并行执行，最后合成输出。使用合成的健康体检记录生成120个包含2–4项需求的韩文复合查询，对比单代理与多代理生成答案。通过一个加权的LLM评判器（主评判）和另外三个LLM评判器、以及两名人工评估者进行打分与偏好选择，并记录延迟、成本与关键失败率。

Result: 多代理在主评判的加权得分从1.695提高到1.797（p=0.027）；另外三位LLM评判器也一致改善（Δ=+0.111至+0.186，均p<0.05）。改进主要来自有用性、一致性及覆盖所有需求的能力；数值准确性与溯源仅在一位评判器下显著改善；医疗安全性无差异；关键失败率接近（单：15.0% vs 多：13.3%）。两名人工评估者在66.7%和68.3%的配对比较中更倾向多代理。多代理增加延迟1.31×，成本2.02×；亚组分析显示改进集中在涉及个人记录查找的查询。

Conclusion: 多代理LLM系统在处理复合健康体检解读查询上优于单一代理，尤其在有个人记录查找需求的场景中表现更好，但在数值准确性、信息落地性与医疗安全性上并未有一致大幅提升，且延迟和成本显著上升。

Abstract: Personalized interpretation of health checkup results requires reasoning across longitudinal records, medical knowledge, lifestyle guidance, and healthcare navigation. We present a multi-agent large language model (LLM) system that identifies multiple intents, maps each to a task-specific agent, executes them in parallel, and synthesizes their outputs. We compared answers generated in Single Agent and Multi Agent settings on 120 Korean compound queries combining two to four requirements, using synthetic health checkup records. The Multi Agent improved the weighted LLM-judge score from 1.695 to 1.797 (p = 0.027), and three additional LLM judges showed consistent improvements ($Δ$ = +0.111 to +0.186, all p < 0.05). The gains came from usefulness, consistency, and the handling of every requirement in compound queries, whereas numerical accuracy and grounding improved significantly under only one of the four judges and medical safety did not differ, and critical failures occurred at similar rates (Single Agent 15.0% vs. Multi Agent 13.3%). Two human evaluators preferred Multi Agent in 66.7% and 68.3% of pairwise comparisons. Multi Agent execution increased latency and cost by 1.31$\times$ and 2.02$\times$, respectively. In exploratory subgroup analyses, the improvement was concentrated in queries involving personal-record lookup.

</details>


### [107] [Rethinking Probability-Based Reinforcement Learning From Posterior Concentration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01458)
*Shiu-Hong Kao,Yubo Zhao,Zhenyu Tian,Pengzhan Sun,Yicong Li,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 发现并定义‘后验集中现象（PCP）’，提出RLCPR通过不确定感知采样与集中感知正则化缓解PCP，提高 verifier-free RL 在长轨迹推理上的稳定性与效率，实验验证优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏外部判别器的通用推理任务中，常用基于模型概率的奖励，但其可靠性在长时序推理中未充分研究。本工作动机是识别并修复概率奖励在长轨迹下失去判别性的缺陷，以改进 verifier-free RL 对 LLM 推理能力的训练。

Method: 分析概率奖励随推理长度的行为并形式化PCP；在GRPO设定下研究其对优化的影响；提出RLCPR：1) 不确定感知数据采样——在生成前减少易出现后验集中的rollout；2) 集中感知正则化——在后验奖励塌缩时惩罚不必要的冗长轨迹，实现训练稳定性和token效率提升。

Result: 理论与实证表明存在PCP，导致奖励近似恒定，降低GRPO优化效果。RLCPR在多项通用与数学推理基准上实现更高的token效率，并在六/七个基准上平均提升最高达4.0%，同时改善训练稳定性。

Conclusion: 本文指出概率奖励在长轨迹推理中会出现后验集中现象（PCP），导致奖励难以区分，从而使基于概率的策略优化不稳定。提出RLCPR框架以感知并缓解PCP，包含不确定感知采样与集中感知正则化，两者提高优化稳定性与token效率，在七个基准上六个任务上优于SOTA verifier-free基线，最高提升4.0%。

Abstract: Verifier-free reinforcement learning with probability-based rewards offers a promising way to train LLMs on general reasoning tasks where external verifiers are unavailable. Yet the reliability of these rewards, especially in long-horizon reasoning, remains underexplored. This work identifies a length-dependent failure mode of probability rewards, which we call the Posterior Concentration Phenomenon (PCP). We show that the probability of a reference answer conditioned on a reasoning trace often collapses to a low-variance interval as the trace becomes lengthy. This phenomenon results in nearly indistinguishable rewards, which, under GRPO-based settings, makes probability-based policy optimization unstable and inefficient. Motivated by this, we propose Reinforcement Learning with Concentration-aware Posterior Rewards (RLCPR), a verifier-free RL framework to explicitly account for PCP for better optimization stability and token efficiency. It has two components: uncertainty-aware data sampling, which reduces concentration-prone rollouts before generation, and concentration-aware regularization, which penalizes unnecessarily long traces when posterior rewards collapse. Extensive experiments show that, alongside higher token efficiency, RLCPR outperforms the state-of-the-art verifier-free RL baseline by up to 4.0% on six of seven benchmarks, including general-domain and mathematical reasoning challenges.

</details>


### [108] [NextMe-800: Anticipating Personal Behavior from Months of Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01461)
*Zhaoxu Meng,Yiming Sun,Mingyuan Gao,Jiachang Zhang,Zhuhan Dai,Yipeng Du,Zheng Lian,Jian-Qiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提供了长期第一视角多模态个体数据与基准，提出开放词汇 K 步序列预测任务与软编辑距离评估，用以研究在不同抽象与时间尺度上对个人行为的可预见性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们常常制定远大计划但实际依赖习惯行为，若能在决策时得到基于个人历史的“事后视角”预测（可行轨迹预览），或可促成更现实的计划与更好的决策。

Method: 长时段（约800小时、126天、1 Hz）第一视角多模态数据采集（图像、注视点、音频），在五个抽象层级上人工标注动作/活动；将个体化动作预测定义为开放词汇的 K 步序列预测；将 NextMe-800 与多人 EgoLife 数据集合并形成 NextAct 基准；使用基于嵌入的软编辑距离评估模型在不同抽象层级和预测时域下的表现。

Result: 发布了一个月级别的长期个人视角数据集（NextMe-800）及一个1500点的评测基准（NextAct）；提出并采用了嵌入式软编辑距离作为衡量开放词汇序列预测的指标；并通过基线实验分析了模型随预测时域和语义抽象层级的性能变化。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一个面向个体行为预测的长期第一视角数据集 NextMe-800，并构建了结合多人的 NextAct 基准，展示如何以开放词汇的 K 步序列预测形式评估个体化动作前瞻能力，使用嵌入式软编辑距离作为评价度量。

Abstract: We often plan ambitiously yet act habitually and wonder, in retrospect, whether we would have planned differently had we known what we would actually do. Hindsight offers a valuable perspective on past decisions, although we often wish we could have simulated hindsight at the moment of choosing. If a system could generate plausible trajectories from one's personal history, such previews might help people formulate more realistic plans and make better informed decisions. We introduce NextMe-800, an approximately 800-hour first-person dataset from one volunteer over 126 days with 1 Hz images, gaze, and audio, captioned at five hierarchical abstraction levels from atomic actions to major activities. We formulate personalized action anticipation as open-vocabulary K-step sequence prediction and construct NextAct, a 1,500-point benchmark combining NextMe-800 with the multi-person EgoLife dataset. Using an embedding-based soft edit distance as the metric, we evaluate how well different models can anticipate personal behavior across abstraction levels and prediction horizons. NextMe-800 and NextAct provide a months-long resource and evaluation framework for studying how far ahead personal behavior can be anticipated from egocentric observation.

</details>


### [109] [Auditing Routing Entropy as an Uncertainty Signal in Attention-Residual Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01495)
*Wenhao Liang,Lin Yue,Wei Emma Zhang,Mingyu Guo,Olaf Maennel,Weitong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Routing traces give a signal over shuffled controls but not robust, replicable added information beyond model confidence in these experiments; probe recovery is incomplete but measurable under injected effects.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Assess whether per-example routing traces (routing entropy) in dynamic architectures carry information about correctness beyond the model's own confidence.

Method: Audit of Attention-Residual (AR) variants of Swin-Tiny and DeiT-Small trained from scratch on CIFAR-10/100 with a soft-binned calibration auxiliary loss; three empirical checks (fixed-confidence stratification, seed replication, held-out predictor vs output-only and shuffled-trace controls); sensitivity injection to quantify probe recovery and multiplicity correction.

Result: No test in a 30-test binned family survived multiplicity correction; results did not replicate across seeds; scalar routing probe yielded no pooled improvement; entropy-profile probe outperformed shuffled traces but underperformed confidence-only predictors; conditioning on full logit vector unresolved; sensitivity injections show partial recovery (profile probe 24–59%, correction probe 8–23% at 0.010 nats).

Conclusion: Control-dependent gains and incomplete estimator recovery; no robust evidence that routing entropy adds predictive information beyond model confidence under the tested settings.

Abstract: Dynamic architectures leave a per-example routing trace beside each prediction, and diffuse routing is easy to read as a sign that the prediction is unreliable. We audit that reading for routing entropy in Attention-Residual (AR) variants of Swin-Tiny and DeiT-Small, trained from scratch on CIFAR-10/100 with a soft-binned calibration auxiliary loss, asking whether the trace carries information about correctness beyond what the model's own confidence already reveals. Three checks probe this increment: does a routing signal appear at fixed confidence, does it replicate across training seeds, and can a held-out predictor exploit it against output-only and shuffled-trace controls? A sensitivity audit then injects effects of known size and measures the fraction of each that the probes recover. No test in the fixed 30-test binned family survives multiplicity correction, and neither the nominal hit nor a borderline result recurs in its sibling seeds. Across 24 paired runs a scalar routing probe yields no pooled improvement in routing-stratified calibration, and an entropy-profile probe predicts correctness better than the same probe given shuffled profiles yet worse than a confidence-only predictor in both binary log-loss and Brier score: a gain over shuffled traces does not become a gain over the output. Conditioning on the complete logit vector leaves the corresponding comparison unresolved. The audit bounds how far these non-detections can be read: at an injected effect of 0.010 nats the profile probe recovers 24-59% of the oracle gain, and a reference-preserving correction probe recovers 8% and 23% in the two CIFAR-100 settings, below the threshold we fixed for applying it to real labels. The results establish control-dependent gains and incomplete estimator recovery, not the absence of conditional routing information.

</details>


### [110] [OpenMTB-Audit: Exposing Over-Refusal and Clinical Expert Perspectives in LLM-Based Molecular Tumor Board Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01497)
*Negin Ashrafi,Jia Luo,Stacey M. Frumm,Roxana Daneshjou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了OpenMTB-Audit基准揭示LLM在MTB情境中普遍过度拒绝问题；提出确定性七模块MTB-AuditAgent，有效恢复‘Partially Supported’等临床重要区分并显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI介入分子肿瘤委员会(MTB)工作流，一个关键的安全挑战是区分真正不被证据支持的建议与那些有证据但因信息不全或临床注意事项仍需医生审查的选项（即“部分支持”）。需要一个基准和方法来评估并减少AI在该情境下的错误拒绝或错误建议。

Method: 构建了500例合成非小细胞肺癌病例，覆盖五类对抗性错误；为每例标注四类安全标签（Supported, Partially Supported, Unsupported, Insufficient Information）；在8种LLM配置上评估性能并测量过度拒绝率；设计并实现了一个确定性七模块框架（MTB-AuditAgent）用于证据核验、缺失信息检测、安全分类与弃权决策；并进行了两位肿瘤医师的注释一致性研究。

Result: 发现所有评估的LLM在真实为‘Partially Supported’的病例中以83.3–100%的比例未能保留该标签（过度拒绝/标签坍塌）；MTB-AuditAgent 将过度拒绝率降至6.7%，总体准确率达91.2%（95% CI: 88.6–93.6%）；注释者分歧集中在信息是否充分与治疗优化的边界。

Conclusion: OpenMTB-Audit 显示大型语言模型倾向于过度拒绝（label collapse），不能恰当地保留临床上有部分证据但仍需肿瘤医师复核的“Partially Supported”标签；所提出的 MTB-AuditAgent 通过分离证据验证、缺失信息检测、安全分类与回避策略，显著降低了过度拒绝并提高了分类准确率。

Abstract: Molecular tumor boards integrate genomic findings, clinical context, and therapeutic evidence to support precision oncology. As AI enters this workflow, a key safety challenge is distinguishing truly unsupported recommendations from evidence-supported options that still require oncologist review because of incomplete information, poor ECOG performance status, or other clinical caveats. We introduce OpenMTB-Audit, an open-source benchmark of 500 synthetic non-small cell lung cancer cases spanning five adversarial error categories and four safety labels: Supported, Partially Supported, Unsupported, and Insufficient Information. Across eight large language model configurations, we identify pervasive over-refusal: all LLM configurations failed to retain the Partially Supported label in 83.3-100% of true Partially Supported cases, achieving high aggregate safety scores through label collapse rather than clinically calibrated reasoning. To address this limitation, we developed MTB-AuditAgent, a deterministic seven-module framework separating evidence verification, missing-information detection, safety classification, and abstention. It reduces over-refusal to 6.7% and achieves 91.2% accuracy (95% CI: 88.6-93.6%). A two-oncologist annotation study found disagreement concentrated at the boundary between information sufficiency and treatment optimization, underscoring the need to preserve clinically meaningful distinctions.

</details>


### [111] [Sharpening Tax in Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01509)
*Changdae Oh,Qi Zeng,Qi Qi,Andrey Zhmoginov,Deren Lei,Yun He,Hoang Phan,Hangoo Kang,Azalia Mirhoseini,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 后训练提高了一次性准确率与一致性，但常以牺牲多样性/覆盖率为代价（Sharpening Tax）。预训练模型配合轻量推理在多次采样预算下常能取得更高覆盖率；PTGS是一种有效缓解方案，按提示难度自适应温度，能同时改善覆盖率与单次准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑并精细化现有对“后训练只是‘锐化’基础模型行为”的理解，尤其在agentic任务中：这些任务可能需要后训练获得的新能力，而不是仅仅把已有行为做窄幅放大。进一步，研究后训练是否不可避免地以牺牲解决方案覆盖率换取一次性准确率，及如何度量与缓解这一代价。

Method: 1) 设计并分析基准：选取14对基/后训练模型、3个agentic基准，比较pass@1与pass@K在不同采样预算下的表现；2) 提出并定义Sharpening Tax，作为度量后训练后测试时可扩展性（solution coverage）损失的诊断指标；3) 机理分析：统计学和实验分析表明后训练使任务成功概率分布两极化，提升采样效率但降低多样性；4) 提出PTGS（posterior-tempered group sampling），一种按样本难度自适应温度的贝叶斯采样器，并在两个agentic环境中于训练/推理中验证其效果。

Result: 实证发现：在多数（但非全部）模型/任务组合中存在显著的Sharpening Tax——后训练提高pass@1但降低pass@K的可扩展性；该税可以通过少量rollout估计且与其他一致性与多样性度量相关。PTGS在训练中比固定温度策略产生更小的税，能在重复采样下解决更多任务，同时还提升单次准确率。

Conclusion: 后训练（如RL微调）确实提高了模型的一次性准确性（pass@1）和采样一致性，但往往以牺牲解的覆盖率（pass@K）为代价——即把任务推向“总是解出”或“从不解出”的极端。对于需要多回合工具调用和交互的agentic任务，未经后训练的预训练模型配合轻量推理机制在给定足够测试时预算时，常常能在解的覆盖率上超过后训练模型。提出的PTGS方法能在训练和推理阶段缓解该矛盾，既提升一次性准确率又保留更高的覆盖率。

Abstract: An emerging hypothesis about reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of large language models (LLMs) is that it merely sharpens existing behaviors of a base model, improving single-shot accuracy at the cost of solution coverage. Although this trade-off has been observed in math and coding tasks, it need not extend to agentic tasks, where multi-turn tool use and interaction may require capabilities newly acquired during post-training. Our surprising finding is that pre-trained LLMs, equipped with a light inference harness, can serve as capable agents. Despite far lower accuracy (pass@1), they often surpass their post-trained counterparts in solution coverage (pass@K) given a sufficient test-time budget. We further analyze the underlying mechanism and show that post-training pushes tasks toward two extremes, always solved or never solved, and thereby improves sampling efficiency and consistency at the cost of solution coverage. To measure this cost, we propose Sharpening Tax, a diagnostic metric that quantifies the loss in test-time scalability after post-training. Across 14 base/post-trained model pairs from four families and three agentic benchmarks (42 cases in total), the tax is prevalent in most settings, can be estimated from a few rollouts, and correlates well with other metrics. Finally, we present posterior-tempered group sampling (PTGS), a simple plug-and-play Bayesian sampler that adapts the sampling temperature per prompt to its estimated difficulty. Applied during RL training in two agentic environments, PTGS pays a smaller tax than the fixed-temperature baseline, solving more tasks under repeated sampling while also improving single-shot accuracy.

</details>


### [112] [Decision Titan: Test-Time Training for Long-Term Memory in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01513)
*Jude Waide,Robert Lieck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将Test-Time Training加到Decision Transformer上（Decision Titan）可在离线RL任务中获得显著超长时序记忆，但其泛化和性能高度依赖时间嵌入与信息编码，需更多消融与基线比较验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNN因梯度消失和向量隐藏态有限表达能力，Transformer因注意力的平方复杂度在处理极长序列上受限。TTT通过把记忆保存为模型参数的方式提供一种潜在解决方案，但尚未在强化学习领域系统研究。

Method: 在Decision Transformer架构中插入Test-Time Training层，训练时与测试时通过梯度下降将情节记忆写入参数；在扩展的T-Maze（X-Maze）环境上评估，分析门控值随时间变化以可视化记忆机制的工作方式。

Result: Decision Titan在X-Maze上能学习20倍于上下文窗的长期依赖，并能泛化到1.7倍训练长度；但时间泛化依赖时间嵌入的设计，且是否能学到长期依赖取决于相关信息的编码方式。可视化门控展示了记忆写入和召回的极简机制。

Conclusion: Decision Titan（在Decision Transformer中加入TTT层）能够在离线强化学习的X-Maze任务中通过参数化的测试时训练（TTT）学习远超上下文窗的长期依赖，但泛化性受时间嵌入与信息编码方式显著影响。

Abstract: Long-term dependencies remain a major challenge for sequential decision-making in the field of AI: RNNs suffer from vanishing gradients and the limited expressivity of vector-based hidden states, whilst Transformer-based models are limited by the quadratic scaling of attention. Recent work has proposed tackling this problem with the Test-Time Training (TTT) framework, which stores episodic memories in the parameters of a neural network through gradient descent at both train and test-time. This approach has seen success in the domain of Natural Language Processing, however, to the best of our knowledge it has not yet been applied to the domain of Reinforcement Learning (RL), nor has there been a study analysing how this memory practically functions. In this paper, we study the potential of the TTT framework for offline RL by augmenting a Decision Transformer with TTT layers, dubbed the Decision Titan. We analyse performance and properties of the model in the X-Maze environment, an extension of T-Maze designed to test sequential memory, and investigate how the memory mechanism learns by visualising gate values over time. Our key findings are that Decision Titan can learn long-term dependencies with ranges 20x longer than the context window, generalises to lengths 1.7x the training data, but crucially temporal generalisation depends on the time embeddings used, and the ability to learn long-term dependencies depends on how the relevant information is encoded.

</details>


### [113] [Towards Reliable Vision-Language Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01531)
*Manasa Mariam Mammen,Priyanka Mary Mammen,Zafer Kayatas,Stefan Wagner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在驾驶相关QA任务上，视觉退化对VLM的影响因模型与输入设置而异；Visual Evidence Augmentation可在若干配置下改善可靠性，但效果不稳定，需要更细致的诊断与更健壮的补救策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视觉-语言模型在自动驾驶中的应用增加，必须评估并提高其在真实世界中常见的传感器或环境退化下的鲁棒性与可信度，以保障安全关键决策。

Method: 在四种驾驶场景相关的问答数据集（单帧、单目、多帧、多视角）上，对五个视觉-语言模型（Qwen3.5-9B、Gemma4-E4B、LLaVA-OneVision-7B、DriveFusion/DriveFusionQA-4B、NVIDIA Alpamayo-1.5-10B）进行系统化评估；在不同的视觉退化条件下测量模型的准确性与置信度可靠性，并在推理阶段应用Visual Evidence Augmentation（V_EA）以评估其对鲁棒性与置信度校准的影响。

Result: 不同模型和输入设置对视觉退化的敏感性差异显著：准确率和置信度可靠性在多种退化下呈现非一致性变化；V_EA在部分模型/数据集上能提高性能和/或校准，但并非普适性方法，效果受模型架构、输入类型和退化种类影响。

Conclusion: 视觉输入退化对自动驾驶领域的视觉-语言模型导致的影响存在显著异质性：不同模型、数据集和输入设置下，准确率与置信度可靠性的变化各不相同；视觉证据增强（V_EA）在推理时能对部分模型/数据集带来性能提升，但并不一致且效果依赖具体情形。

Abstract: Vision-Language models (VLMs) are increasingly being explored in autonomous driving for tasks such as scene understanding, driving reasoning, decision-making, and end-to-end driving. As their role becomes more prominent, ensuring their robustness and reliability is increasingly important. In real-world conditions, visual inputs may be degraded by sensor imperfections and environmental conditions, potentially affecting both model predictions and their associated confidence. Such degradation is especially concerning in autonomous driving, where safety-critical decisions require models to make accurate predictions and recognize when their predictions may be unreliable. In this work, we evaluate five VLMs (Qwen3.5-9B, Gemma4-E4B, LLaVA-OneVision-7B, DriveFusion/DriveFusionQA-4B, and NVIDIA Alpamayo-1.5-10B) across four driving-related QA datasets with different visual input settings, including single-frame, multi-view, multi-frame, and monocular inputs. Our results show that the effects of visual corruption vary across models, datasets, and input settings, with changes in accuracy and confidence reliability and also differing across conditions. We then apply Visual Evidence Augmentation ($\mathrm{V}{\scriptstyle \mathrm{EA}}$), a recent inference-time method to examine whether it can improve model reliability under degraded visual conditions. We find that $\mathrm{V}{\scriptstyle \mathrm{EA}}$ improves performance for some models and datasets, although the gains are not consistent across all settings.

</details>


### [114] [Neither Black nor White: Balancing Semantic and Collaborative Signals with Graph-Informed Semantic IDs (GrIS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01533)
*Aleksei Medvedev,Alejandro Ariza-Casabona,Steven Derby,Gonzalo Fiz Pontiveros,Xinyang Shao,Florian Spiess*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 把语义ID构造看作层次化图划分，提出 GrIS 框架及两个实例，兼容并扩展先前方法，在实证上带来显著推荐性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将SID视作表示量化的做法忽视了协作信号的结构性价值。将问题转为图上的递归聚类能自然融合内容与协作信息，并拓展设计空间（图构建与递归划分算法两轴）。

Method: 把物品视为带内容特征的图节点、用户交互或协作信息为图边，将SID指派定义为层次化图划分；证明内容-only量化（RQ-VAE、RQ-KMeans）为图为空时的特例；实现两条具体路径：RecDMoN（可微分图池化实现递归分配）与 RQ-GAE（在 RQ-VAE 基础上引入图感知表示与图重建目标）。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上，GrIS 系列方法系统性地优于基线（含CF-aware SOTA），并展示了图构建与分区策略可独立替换与组合以推动性能改善。

Conclusion: 将语义ID的构建重新表述为带语义节点与协作边的图上的递归聚类问题，提出统一框架GrIS并包含先前方法为特例；通过两种实例化（RecDMoN 与 RQ-GAE）验证了该思路，在多数据集上显著优于现有CF-aware SOTA，最高提升达+52% Hit@10。

Abstract: Existing work on Semantic IDs (SIDs) for generative recommendation treats SID construction as a representation learning problem: encode items into a quantised latent space and read off codes. We argue this view is incidental. SID construction is, at heart, a recursive clustering problem, and once stated this way the natural object to cluster is a graph whose nodes carry semantic content and whose edges carry collaborative signal; SID assignment becomes a hierarchical graph partition. This reframing yields a unified framework, Graph-Informed Semantic IDs (GrIS), that subsumes prior approaches rather than displacing them. RQ-VAE and RQ-KMeans are recovered as the special case where the graph is empty, exposing content-only quantisation as one corner of a larger design space along two so-far-collapsed axes: graph construction and recursive partition algorithm. We explore two contrasting instantiations: RecDMoN, which performs hierarchical assignment via differentiable graph pooling, and RQ-GAE, which extends RQ-VAE with graph-aware item representations and a graph reconstruction objective. On multiple real-world datasets, GrIS consistently improves over CF-aware SOTA, with gains of up to +52\% Hit@10. Because graph construction and partition are explicit, separately configurable components, improvements on either axis can be combined and evaluated systematically.

</details>


### [115] [The AI Assessment Sandbox Configurator: A Framework to Support Technical Assessment in AI Regulatory Sandboxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01539)
*Alessio Buscemi,German Castignani,Daniele Pagani,Maxime Cordy,Jordi Cabot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为回应AI法案对AIRS的要求，本文提出并实现了一个开源框架以协调分散的技术测试工具链，通过目录化测试、插件API与共享数据模型实现结果统一与可审计报告，并在真实AIRS试点中初步验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟AI法案要求成员国在2027年前建立AIRS，且当AIRS包含结构化技术测试时，需在规模上运行专用基础设施。但现有工具生态碎片化、输出异构、难以比较与复用，迫切需要一种统一的基础设施来支撑跨学科监管测试与可审计的报告。

Method: 从AIRS的程序条件与AI法案对高风险系统的规定出发，归纳出11项架构与治理需求；基于这些需求设计并实现一个开源框架，包含：经策划的测试与控制目录、稳定的插件API、共享数据模型、多角色仪表盘与面向受众的报告机制。完成了架构描述、当前发布版实现，并在真实AIRS参与中进行了早期试点评估。

Result: 交付了一个开源的配置器（Catalog + 插件API + 共享数据模型 + 仪表盘 + 报告生成），在实地试点中实现了异构测试结果的统一和分层报告，并为退出报告提供了输入；同时提出了后续路线图、目录治理问题及跨成员国开源生态的制度化路径。

Conclusion: 本文提出并实现了一个面向欧盟AI监管沙箱（AIRS）的开源基础设施框架——AI Assessment Sandbox Configurator，用以统一和规模化技术测试流程，满足11项从AIRS程序性要求和AI法案高风险系统义务推导出的架构与治理需求。该框架已完成架构设计并发布早期版本，在一次实地AIRS试点中验证了输出统一化和报告能力，并对目录分层贡献模型与治理路径提出讨论。

Abstract: The EU's Artificial Intelligence Act requires all Member States to establish AI Regulatory Sandboxes (AIRS) by August 2027: supervised environments bringing together national Competent Authorities, technical experts, and the organisations under assessment. When AIRS engagements include structured technical testing, running such testing at scale demands dedicated infrastructure, yet the tooling ecosystem remains structurally fragmented, with heterogeneous tools producing outputs that are difficult to compare, trace, and reuse. From the procedural conditions of AIRS engagements and the AI Act obligations for high-risk systems, we derive 11 architectural and governance requirements for the infrastructure that operationalises technical testing within an AIRS. In response to these requirements, we introduce the AI Assessment Sandbox Configurator, an open-source framework combining a curated Catalogue of tests and controls accessed through a stable plug-in API, a shared data model that harmonises heterogeneous outputs, role-specific dashboards for multi-disciplinary interpretation, and audience-segmented reporting. We describe the architecture and current release, and report an early-stage pilot that exercised the harmonisation and reporting layers within a live AIRS engagement and contributed to an official Exit Report. We discuss the roadmap, the governance questions raised by the Catalogue's tiered contribution model, and the institutional pathways through which an open-source assessment ecosystem could emerge across Member States.

</details>


### [116] [Evaluating Physical Consistency and Plausibility in Generative Scenario Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01581)
*Manasa Mariam Mammen,Zafer Kayatas,Stefan Wagner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 五层评估协议：从内部表征到动力学约束，补充传统指标，揭示生成场景在运动学一致性与可控性上的潜在问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI被用于自动驾驶场景生成，但现有评估多依赖视觉和单点统计指标，缺乏对内部表征的透明性与对真实车辆动力学一致性的形式化保证，限制其在安全关键验证与认证流程中的应用。

Method: 构建五层评估流程：1) 运动学对齐（kinematic alignment）检验潜在表示与车辆运动学特征的映射；2) 与统计基线比较，评估分布一致性；3) 潜在空间可控性（latent controllability）测试，通过潜在变量操作产生目标行为；4) 激活分析（activation analysis），检查网络内部表征和单元响应；5) 输出级动力学约束检查，例如横向jerk阈值。将该协议应用于VAE并推广到其它生成模型。

Result: 在VAE实验中，传统输出级指标和可视化显示场景“真实”，但分层协议揭示潜在空间与运动学特征仅部分对齐，且若干生成轨迹违反车辆动力学（如横向jerk超标）。协议同样成功应用于其他生成模型，表明方法具有架构无关性。

Conclusion: 提出的分层评估协议为生成式场景模型提供了比传统输出级指标更深的可解释性，能检测出即便视觉上逼真的轨迹也可能不满足车辆动力学约束；该协议在VAE及其他生成架构上均可应用，但需要更明确的量化阈值、统计检验和闭环可行性验证以用于认证。

Abstract: Generative AI models are increasingly used for scenario generation in autonomous driving. While they can generate realistic-looking scenarios, they often provide limited transparency into learned representations and consistency with real-world vehicle dynamics. This lack of formal assurance limits their use in safety-critical validation and certification workflows. To address this aspect, we introduce a layered evaluation protocol that complements existing methods by assessing models across five layers. The first four layers inspect internal representations and network layers through kinematic alignment, statistical baseline comparison, latent controllability, and activation analysis. The fifth layer evaluates model outputs against vehicle dynamics constraints such as lateral jerk thresholds. We demonstrate the protocol on a Variational Autoencoder (VAE)-based scenario generator. Although standard output-level metrics and visualizations suggest that the generated scenarios are realistic, our protocol provides deeper insight into the extent to which the model's latent space aligns with kinematic features and whether visually plausible trajectories satisfy vehicle-dynamics constraints. We further apply the protocol to additional generative models, demonstrating its applicability beyond the VAE architecture.

</details>


### [117] [Agents Are Systems, Not Models: Rethinking Agentic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01618)
*Luis Wiedmann,Leander Girrbach,Cordelia Schmid,Zeynep Akata*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 评估应把智能体视为可配置系统：信息量比时间和模型规模更重要；大部分变异来自随机运行而非配置改变；工具化实现比提示更能可靠地引导某些行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估通常只报告单一成功率并把智能体视为固定对象，但实际使用中用户会通过提示、运行时间和模型选择等配置改变智能体表现，因而需要研究配置对结果的影响与变异来源。

Method: 作者构建了一个包含四个科学任务的新基准，任务要求代码型智能体查找并调用已发表的专用模型。系统地研究了五种配置维度（任务信息、推理方式、自我验证、时间预算、骨干模型），收集并分析了超过18,000条智能体轨迹，量化各配置对性能与一致性的影响，并提出基于轨迹的行为分类法。

Result: 主要发现包括：约54%的结果方差来自重复相同配置的运行（运行内变异），提供给智能体的信息是影响性能最大的一项（超过时间预算与模型大小），且能降低成本并改善校准；配置之间存在交互，例如额外时间仅在信息充足或模型足够能利用时有用；提示要求验证对智能体的验证行为影响甚微，但引入专用验证工具会显著改变行为。作者同时公开了基准与数据集。

Conclusion: 论文结论是：智能体应作为可配置系统来评估，而不是把其视为固定实体；某些期望行为更有效地通过系统级实现（例如专用验证工具）而非仅靠提示促发。

Abstract: Agent evaluations increasingly go beyond a single success rate, reporting metrics such as cost, consistency, and robustness. Yet they typically treat the agent itself as fixed. In practice, an agent is a configurable system: users decide what to tell it, how long to let it run, and which model to use, and each of these choices can change how well and how consistently it performs. We study these choices on a new benchmark of four scientific tasks, where a coding agent must find and correctly operate a published specialist model. We investigate five parts of the agent's configuration: task information, reasoning, self-verification, time budget, and backbone model. We find substantial run-to-run variability, with approximately 54% of the outcome variance coming from repeating the same configuration rather than changing it. Across configurations, the information provided to the agent has the largest effect, exceeding both time budget and model size, while also reducing cost and improving calibration. Configuration choices also interact: additional time helps only when the agent has sufficient information or a capable enough model to use it. Finally, a trajectory-based taxonomy of agent behavior reveals that prompting an agent to verify its answer has little effect on its verification behavior, whereas providing a dedicated verification tool changes that behavior substantially. These results suggest that agents should be evaluated as configurable systems themselves, and that some desired behaviors are more effectively implemented in the system than requested through prompting. We release the benchmark and more than 18,000 agent trajectories.

</details>


### [118] [FedLore: Communication and Memory Efficient Federated Learning via Shared Gradient Low-Rank Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01620)
*Junkang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Federated training of foundation models is constrained by client memory and communication costs. LoRA-based methods reduce these costs through low-rank adapters, but their fixed rank budget can limit adaptation. Gradient low-rank optimization offers greater flexibility, yet independently chosen client subspaces create a problem we term \emph{subspace fragmentation}: local projections interact with data heterogeneity to bias aggregated directions, while aggregation can increase update rank and communication cost. Thus, accurate local gradient compression need not preserve global descent. We propose \texttt{FedLore}, which shares a low-rank optimization basis within each round and refreshes it across rounds. The shared basis enables exact aggregation in low-rank coordinates and eliminates the identified projection bias. Subspace refresh allows the accumulated model update to exceed the per-round rank budget. We characterize the aggregation bias and establish an $O(T^{-1/2})$ stationarity bound for the projected-SGD variant under a global-gradient coverage condition and standard smoothness and variance assumptions, with bounded gradient heterogeneity. Experiments on vision and language tasks, including federated pre-training, show that \texttt{FedLore} outperforms the evaluated low-rank adapter baselines and matches or exceeds full-parameter training, while reducing communication and optimizer-state memory.

</details>


### [119] [Measuring the Stability Assumption Behind Action Chunking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01626)
*Aryan Goyal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过注入微小动作扰动并比较开放环/闭环传播，作者发现误差放大常见且重规划很少能可靠扭转放大，说明应显式训练闭环恢复能力而不是单纯依赖动作分块减少误差复合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清动作分块为何能提升模仿学习策略性能：是因为被动动力学收敛、规划时限变短、表征改进，还是误差复合被减少？作者直接研究“误差进入系统后如何演化”以检验误差复合相关论断。

Method: 在每个状态注入小幅动作扰动，分别在开放环（重放后续动作，不重规划）和闭环（扰动后让策略重规划）两种执行模式下测量误差随时间的增长/衰减，拟合传播速率并据此将状态标记为收缩、扩张或未判定；在12个操作任务上重复实验，研究拟合窗口长度对结论的影响，并训练观测到的状态表征以预测这些标签。

Result: 确定性稳定状态稀少；在可判定状态中误差放大更常见；误差放大通常前置（即短窗口会高估长远放大）；开放环的传播行为可由相机和本体感知较好预测，而闭环传播依赖于策略对扰动后的反应，仅能部分由当前观测预测；建议通过显式的扰动覆盖与树状覆盖训练来培养闭环恢复能力，而非期待其在标准模仿学习中自然出现。

Conclusion: 仅靠误差复合（error-compounding）无法完整解释动作分块带来的性能提升：在大多数可判定的状态下，注入的小动作误差更倾向被放大而非被被动收敛，且闭环重规划很少将开放环节的放大转变为确定性的收敛。

Abstract: Action chunking improves the performance of policies learned by behavioural cloning, and several mechanisms have been proposed to explain why, including temporal consistency, horizon reduction, representation learning, and reduced error compounding. We instead study what happens to an action error once it enters the system. At each state, we inject a small action error and measure how fast it grows or shrinks under two execution regimes: open-loop, where the rest of the chunk is replayed without replanning, and closed-loop, where the policy replans after the perturbation. The fitted rate labels each state as contracting, expanding, or unresolved. Across twelve manipulation tasks from three benchmark suites, we find that confidently stable states are rare, while error amplification is common among states whose propagation rate can be resolved. We further find that the measured propagation rate depends strongly on the fitting horizon: amplification is typically front-loaded, so short windows can overestimate longer-horizon propagation. Finally, we train predictors on these labels and find that a state's open-loop regime can be recovered from camera frames and proprioception alone, while its closed-loop propagation is only partially recoverable because it also depends on how the policy acts after the perturbation. These results suggest that error-compounding arguments alone do not provide a complete account of action chunking: neither passive open-loop dynamics nor policy replanning consistently contracts an injected error, and replanning rarely turns open-loop amplification into confident contraction. This suggests that closed-loop reactivity should be trained explicitly, using perturbation- and tree-coverage-oriented training to expose policies to deviations they must recover from, rather than expected to emerge reliably from standard imitation learning.

</details>


### [120] [CoEvolve: Construct-to-Edit Visual Grounding with Bidirectional State Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01710)
*Dongwei Sun,Yujie Zhang,Bowen Yao,Pei Liu,Jing Yao,Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoEvolve 用“构建-编辑”双阶段流程（RER + BDR）把定位问题显式化并可编辑化，显著提高定位鲁棒性与可诊断性，在小于数十亿参数规模下达到大型模型水平，且在受损输入下有很强的纠错能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态定位把目标识别、空间推理与边界估计压缩为一次终端预测，导致中间错误难以检测与修复；需要可测量、可编辑的显式空间状态以改善诊断与纠错能力。

Method: 提出 Construct-to-Edit 框架：Region-Evolution Reinforcement (RER) 以逐步语义-空间轨迹生成候选区域，Bidirectional Denoising Refiner (BDR) 固定文本语义并通过双向同位重建细化坐标，结合几何与行为层目标来整合和纠正候选。

Result: 在自然图像和遥感数据集上验证；9B 参数骨干在定位精度上可比拟高达241B模型；在受控污染测试下，单次 BDR 迭代使平均框重叠率提升 27+ 百分点，证明强恢复能力；状态来源比较显示显式构建与源匹配编辑互补。

Conclusion: CoEvolve 将视觉定位分解为显式状态构建与状态编辑两步，能更好诊断与修正中间定位错误，显著提高框选精度，尤其在受损输入下恢复能力强。

Abstract: Visual grounding localizes an object described by language with a bounding box. Most multimodal grounding models compress target identification, spatial reasoning, and boundary estimation into one terminal prediction. Free-form rationales make reasoning linguistically explicit but do not necessarily expose measurable, editable spatial states. Intermediate localization errors are therefore difficult to diagnose and correct, allowing incorrect region choices and imprecise boundaries to persist in the final box. We introduce CoEvolve, a construct-to-edit framework that separates grounding into explicit state construction and state editing. Region-Evolution Reinforcement (RER) organizes grounding analysis into a progressive semantic--spatial trajectory, with each reasoning step committing to an explicit candidate region. Bidirectional Denoising Refiner (BDR) treats the reasoning text as fixed semantic context and refines the trajectory's coordinate fields through bidirectional same-position reconstruction. Geometry- and behavior-level objectives provide target geometry and edit-preference signals for consolidating reliable candidates, preserving accurate inputs, or correcting toward annotations. Evaluations cover natural-image and remote-sensing grounding. With a 9B backbone, CoEvolve rivals models up to 241B parameters in grounding accuracy. Under controlled corruption, a single BDR pass improves mean box overlap by over 27 percentage points, demonstrating strong recovery from substantial localization errors. State-source comparisons further support the complementarity of explicit state construction and source-matched editing. The project is at https://sundongwei.github.io/CoEvolve_Project/.

</details>


### [121] [vFedProtoQNAS: Prototype-Guided Personalized Quantum Neural Architecture Search for Virtual Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01718)
*Seok Bin Son,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen,Soohyun Park,Joongheon Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: vFedProtoQNAS: personalized QNAS per client + class-wise prototype exchange as semantic anchors → avoids parameter mixing, improves accuracy and representation alignment over FedAvg.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Device heterogeneity in quantum federated learning makes a single shared QNN architecture impractical; naïve averaging of parameters across structurally different circuits leads to semantically inconsistent mixing of operations. A prototype-based semantic exchange can enable collaboration without parameter aggregation.

Method: Each client independently runs a personalized quantum neural architecture search (QNAS) and trains its own QNN. Clients compute class-wise prototypes from their latent representations and upload these prototypes to the server. The server aggregates prototypes across clients to form global class prototypes, which are distributed back as semantic anchors to refine local representation learning. No model parameters are averaged across structurally heterogeneous QNNs.

Result: Empirical results reported show an accuracy improvement of 3.70% over FedAvg and better class-consistent representation alignment (as measured by representation analyses in the paper).

Conclusion: vFedProtoQNAS avoids cross-client parameter averaging and achieves personalized quantum neural architectures by exchanging only class-wise prototypes; this yields better class-consistent representation alignment and improves accuracy over FedAvg.

Abstract: Quantum federated learning (QFL) has emerged as a promising approach for collaboratively training compact quantum neural networks (QNNs) over distributed private data on resource-constrained devices. However, differences in device capabilities make a single shared QNN architecture unsuitable for all clients. While personalized quantum neural architecture search (QNAS) allows each client to select a device-specific QNN, averaging parameters across structurally different QNN architectures mixes semantically inconsistent circuit operations. To address this, prototype-guided personalized QNAS for virtual FL (vFedProtoQNAS) is proposed, where model parameters are never aggregated across clients and federated collaboration is achieved through class-wise prototype sharing. Each client independently searches and trains a client-specific QNN, computes class-wise local prototypes from latent representations, and refines them using global prototypes from the server as federated semantic anchors. Experiments demonstrate that vFedProtoQNAS improves accuracy by 3.70\% over FedAvg and enhances class-consistent representation alignment.

</details>


### [122] [TopK-Guided: Adaptive, Budget-Aware Activation Sparsity for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01763)
*Mukund Agarwalla,Chih-Jen Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TopK-Guided: training-free activation sparsity that uses bounded per-token TopK with sensitivity-aware block-wise budgets, improving Llama-2/3 perplexity and task accuracy vs TEAL and WINA, especially at high sparsity.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing training-free activation-sparsity methods either adapt per-token sparsity without tight sparsity control (threshold-based TEAL) or enforce fixed per-token budgets (TopK WINA) and both ignore block-level sensitivity differences. The motivation is to combine the benefits: adapt per-token while bounding realised sparsity and allocate budgets across blocks according to sensitivity to improve performance at high sparsity.

Method: TopK-Guided enforces a per-token upper-bound on the number of nonzero activations via TopK selection (so realised sparsity is controlled) and allocates different sparsity budgets to transformer blocks based on block sensitivity; it remains training-free and preserves the same sparsity-dependent projection compute as standard TopK.

Result: Across Llama-2 and Llama-3, TopK-Guided consistently improves perplexity and downstream accuracy over TEAL and WINA, especially at high sparsity; ablation shows both bounded token-level adaptation and block-aware allocation contribute complementary gains.

Conclusion: This paper proposes TopK-Guided, a training-free activation sparsity method that combines bounded token-level TopK selection with sensitivity-aware block-level budget allocation, achieving better perplexity and task accuracy than prior methods while keeping compute comparable to fixed-budget TopK approaches.

Abstract: Activation sparsity speeds up large language model (LLM) inference by setting unimportant activations to zero so that the corresponding computations can be skipped. Existing training-free methods, however, make different trade-offs: threshold-based methods such as TEAL adapt the sparsity level to each token but do not tightly control the realised sparsity, while TopK-based methods such as WINA enforce a fixed sparsity level but use the same sparsity budget for every token. Both also apply the same budget across transformer blocks, despite large differences in block sensitivity. We introduce TopK-Guided, a training-free method that addresses both limitations by combining bounded token-level sparsity adaptation with sensitivity-aware block-level budget allocation. Across Llama-2 and Llama-3 models, TopK-Guided consistently improves perplexity and downstream accuracy over TEAL and WINA while preserving essentially the same sparsitydependent projection compute as WINA, with the largest gains at high sparsity. Ablations show that both components provide complementary improvements.

</details>


### [123] [VideoEvolve: Evolving Agent Harnesses for Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01766)
*Bingjun Luo,Yuhuan Fan,Jialin Guo,Siqi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VideoEvolve通过结构化harness表示和基于分支的演化机制，自动优化视频时间定位agent的工作流与指令，在多个基准上带来可观改进，指令优化为最稳定的贡献来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动改进agent harness昂贵且繁琐，需要定位错误并同时修改工作流与指令；因此希望设计一个自动化框架，能在不破坏接口的前提下演化和优化harness以提升视频时间定位性能。

Method: 提出Cloze-Structured Harness Representation以保持阶段接口同时允许工作流和指令演化；采用Branch-Guided Harness Evolution，通过执行反馈指导局部代码编辑、保留有前景的分支并用验证集决定改进是否保留。

Result: 在多个基准上展示了性能提升；成分分析表明指令精炼是一致的增益来源，而演化代码的收益随评测设置而异；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 本文提出的VideoEvolve证明了通过自动演化agent harness可以有效提升基于冻结视频-语言模型的视频时间定位性能，尤其是通过指令精炼带来的稳定增益。

Abstract: Video temporal grounding aims to localize events in videos from natural-language queries. For agents built around frozen video-language models, the harness determines how queries guide temporal predictions and how those predictions are refined. Manually refining these harnesses requires diagnosing grounding failures and coordinating changes to both agent workflows and instructions. We introduce VideoEvolve, a framework that automatically evolves agent harnesses for video temporal grounding. VideoEvolve uses a Cloze-Structured Harness Representation that preserves stage interfaces while leaving agent workflows and instructions open to evolution. Branch-Guided Harness Evolution preserves promising code branches for continued refinement, using execution feedback to guide local edits and validation to determine which improvements are carried forward. Experiments demonstrate improved grounding performance across multiple benchmarks. Component analyses identify instruction refinement as a consistent source of gains, while the benefits of evolved code vary across evaluation settings. Together, these results support automated harness evolution as an effective approach to improving video temporal grounding. Code is available at https://github.com/bingjunluo/VideoEvolve .

</details>


### [124] [RealCompanion: Benchmarking Human Understanding from Reasoning over Longitudinal Real-World Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01780)
*Arman Behnam,Sunglyoung Kim,Liangwei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Released a dataset of 10 real person–AI companion conversations with provenance-backed labels and reasoning traces; found that memory needs are rare and often recent, detectors fail to identify memory necessity on real data, labeling influences memory use, and different agents match persona reconstruction performance at vastly different costs.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To create a realistic benchmark for long-term conversational memory grounded in real private human-AI interactions, addressing limitations of synthetic benchmarks that generate people, questions, and predefined relevant context. The goal is to measure whether companion AIs can remember, infer user identity, and determine when past conversation is relevant.

Method: Collected and released 10 real relationships between people and AI companions: full chat logs and four derived files (profile, persona, chat ground truth, question set), where each label cites messages it rests on and carries a staged, checked reasoning trace. Evaluated: (a) how often past messages are needed (recency window and memory probes), (b) detector models for when memory is needed, (c) labeling effects, and (d) agent systems' persona reconstruction and cost-efficiency. Empirical analyses used pooled measures, recency-window retrieval, detection models, comparisons between real and authored questions, and cost-F1 comparisons across agent systems.

Result: Released the bench dataset (27,218 messages, 10 relationships) with provenance-backed labels and reasoning traces. Key empirical findings: (1) The past is infrequently needed; recency heuristics retrieve necessary context for most probes, and supplying recorded evidence helps mainly on non-memory messages. (2) Memory-need detectors cannot reliably predict when memory is required on real messages; authored questions differ by leaking cues, and manual labeling increases memory usage. (3) Three agent systems achieve similar persona-reconstruction F1 but differ dramatically in computational cost (up to 31x). These results indicate evaluation pitfalls, detector limitations, and cost-accuracy tradeoffs for long-term conversational memory.

Conclusion: This paper introduces bench, a dataset of ten real human-AI companion conversations (27,218 messages over up to 120 days), with derived labels (profile, persona, ground-truth chat, question set) and per-label reasoning traces. It assesses memory usage and detection: (1) Most queries don't need past messages; gains from supplying evidence mostly come from non-memory messages. (2) Memory-need detectors fail on real messages; authored questions leak cues; labeling messages as memories increases their usage. (3) Different agent systems can reconstruct persona equally well (same F1) at widely varying costs. Overall, the benchmark reveals practical challenges in evaluating and using long-term memory in conversational agents.

Abstract: A companion that talks with a person for months should come to understand them. It should remember what they said, infer who they are, and know when the past bears on the message in front of it. Testing this requires a real person's record, and such records are private, so benchmarks generate the person and the questions and settle in advance what matters. We release \bench, ten real relationships with an AI companion: 27,218 messages over up to 120 days, released as the conversation and four files derived from it, a profile, a persona, a chat ground truth and a question set, each citing the messages it rests on. Every chat label carries the reasoning trace that produced it, checked stage by stage against the conversation. Three findings follow. First, the past is rarely needed and far away. Pooled measures mislead: a recency window finds the required message for 95.9\% of probes and 2.2\% of those that need memory, and at the natural rate 96\% of the gain from supplying recorded evidence comes from messages that need none. Second, no detector we tried can tell when memory is needed on real messages, authored questions over the same histories leak the cue, and labeling the same messages as memories raises their use by ten to fourteen points. Third, three agent systems reconstruct the persona with the same F1 at a 31-fold difference in cost.

</details>


### [125] [Q-Learning for Reachability in MEC-Free MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01781)
*Lu-Chin Chang,Suguman Bansal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Q-learning的首个无模型可达性学习算法Quasar，理论保证收敛且在内存与样本效率上优于现有模型方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于可达性规范的RL存在渐进收敛结果但仅限于需显式估计转移概率的模型方法；目标是设计能实际部署、内存和样本效率更高的规范引导（specification-guided）强化学习算法。

Method: 基于Q-learning的无模型方法，使用时序差分（TD）更新直接学习动作值，不显式估计转移概率；算法天然适配通过标准MEC商简化后的MDP片段，从而只需O(|S||A|)存储而非模型方法的O(|S|^2|A|)。

Result: 在无非终结MECs的MDP片段上给出渐近收敛证明；在Quantitative Verification Benchmark Set上，Quasar以比现有模型方法少几个量级的样本收敛到最优策略，并显著降低内存占用。

Conclusion: Quasar是首个对无非终结最大环（non-terminal MECs）片段的MDP在可达性规范下具有渐进最优性保证的无模型算法，降低了内存复杂度并在基准测试上显著减少样本需求。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for reachability specifications is fundamental to sequential decision-making. Prior work establishes asymptotic convergence to optimal policies, but only through model-based methods that must explicitly estimate the transition probabilities of the underlying Markov Decision Process (MDP). We present Quasar, the first model-free algorithm with asymptotic guarantees for reachability on the fragment of MDPs free of non-terminal maximal end components (MECs), a building block to which every MDP reduces by the standard MEC quotient. Our algorithm follows the classical Q-learning approach, using temporal-difference updates to converge to an optimal policy without ever learning the transition probabilities. The resulting learner reduces the memory footprint from the O(|S|^2|A|) that model-based methods require to O(|S||A|). On the standardized Quantitative Verification Benchmark Set, our algorithm converges to the optimal policy with orders of magnitude fewer samples than the previous model-based state-of-the-art. Together these results are a concrete step toward the practical deployment of reachability learning and, with it, of specification-guided RL.

</details>


### [126] [Not All Experience Belongs in the Weights: Component Routing for Self-Improving GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01787)
*Beining Wu,Zihao Ding,Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将轨迹按 locators/procedures/state-facts/lessons 分解并按组件路由到权重或上下文，能通过一条基于重现性与状态条件性的简单规则稳健决定目的地，并显著优于把整轨迹放到某一处的做法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是解释文献中对“把经验写入权重 vs 放入上下文”冲突结论的来源：轨迹是将多种性质不同的条目捆绑在一起的混合体，因此把整条轨迹作为单位会掩盖不同子项对两种记忆目的地的不同需求。通过按组件分离，可以在可比单元上决定最佳去向并得出可普适的规则。

Method: 方法是提出“组件路由（component routing）”：将每条轨迹分解为 locators、procedures、state facts、lessons 四类组件，然后在相同的样本上比较把每类组件放入两种目的地（权重 vs 上下文）的效果。实验覆盖三类 backbone 家族、两个环境、三次随机种子，并对比整轨迹写入权重/上下文以及基于规则的路由。还进行了两种干预来验证规则边界效应，并量化了训练与 producer-consumer 差异对两类目的地价值的影响。

Result: 主要结果包括：1) 在相同样本上比较，locators 和 lessons 在写入权重时获益最大；procedures 和 state facts 在放入上下文时获益最大。2) 一个仅基于重现性和状态条件性的规则能在未见 backbone 家族上 24/24 正确预测组件目的地；两种人为干预能将组件推动到规则边界附近。3) 路由规则优于所有整轨迹基线，并平均比每个 backbone 的最佳单一目的地高出约 3.5 个点。4) 进一步分析表明：写入权重会降低对同一信息的 note readout（尤其是高重现项）；上下文带来的增益随信息差距增长而上升；写入权重带来的收益随策略差距（policy gap）增大而下降。

Conclusion: 本文结论是：将 GUI 代理生成的轨迹按组件拆分并分别路由到模型权重（fine-tuning）或上下文（prompt retrieval）比把整条轨迹作为单一单位放到某一目的地更优。具体地，定位器（locators）和经验教训（lessons）更适合写入权重，而过程（procedures）和状态事实（state facts）更适合放入上下文；基于“重现性（recurrence）”和“状态条件性（state-conditionality）”的规则可以在未见的 backbone 家族上完美预测目的地，并显著优于任何整轨迹基线。

Abstract: Self-improving GUI agents keep the trajectories they produce and return them to the agent, by fine-tuning or by retrieval into the prompt, and studies that compare the two destinations disagree. We attribute this to the unit of experience: a trajectory bundles items with different properties, so a conclusion about the bundle depends on its mix. To address this, (i) we introduce component routing, which splits the experience into locators, procedures, state facts and lessons and sends each component to the context or to the weights, compared on the same items across three backbone families, two environments and three seeds. One pool has two destinations: locators and lessons win in the weights, procedures and state facts in the context. (ii) We fit a rule in two properties measured before any training, recurrence and state-conditionality; it recovers the destination of a held-out backbone family in 24 of 24 cells, two interventions move a component toward the boundary, and routing by the rule beats every whole-trajectory baseline and, by +3.5 points on average, the better single destination of each backbone. (iii) We identify how training and producer-consumer differences change the value of the two destinations: note readout decreases after the same component is written into the weights, most for the items that recur most, context gains increase with the information gap, and weights gains decrease with the policy gap. Code and data will be released.

</details>


### [127] [LineupRL: Verifiable Reinforcement Learning for Time Series Captioning via Caption-to-Series Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01800)
*Haochen Zhang,Laura Yao,Zachary Plotkin,Gengwei Zhang,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用“标题到序列识别”作为可验证奖励，通过冻结 LLM 作为验证器的 RL 流水线（LineupRL）能让小模型在时间序列字幕生成和相关下游任务上超越 SFT 与现有 RL 基线，并具备抗作弊性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调受限于提供标签（大模型）本身的质量，无法超越它；而现有强化学习奖励多为其它模态/任务设计，难以直接迁移到开放式时间序列生成，导致奖励不可解释或易被规避。需要一种既可验证又适配时序开放生成的奖励机制。

Method: 提出了 RLVR（reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards）流水线——LineupRL：用一个冻结的大型语言模型作为验证器，验证器只读取生成的文本描述与原始时间序列数值（不看图表），在若干干扰序列中选出被描述的那个；模型通过该选择结果作为奖励信号进行强化学习训练。匹配（识别）比生成或评分更简单，因此可用现成 LLM 提供可靠奖励；训练在两个 caption 基准以及预测/重构任务上进行，并与 SFT 和其他 RL 基线比较。
