Result: 在两个时间序列 caption 基准以及预测和重构任务上，LineupRL 在所有评测指标上均优于 SFT 与 RL 基线；用 LineupRL 训练的 3B 视觉语言模型在生成质量与下游任务上以 1/24 的参数量超越了作为 SFT 源的 72B VLM；案例研究显示方法抵抗奖励作弊，生成的标题既能描述趋势也能在关键点给出数值。

Conclusion: LineupRL 通过将奖励定义为“生成的标题能否让验证器从多候选时间序列中识别出被描述的序列”，实现了对时间序列自动生成文本的可验证强化学习优化。该方法使小模型在生成质量与下游任务（预测、重构）上超过基于大模型蒸馏的监督微调和现有 RL 基线，且对奖励作弊具有抗性。

Abstract: Time series captioning is a fundamental step in time series understanding and can also serve as the bridge between signal and natural language. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) relies on a larger model's captions and cannot exceed their quality. Reinforcement learning (RL) can, but its rewards were designed for other modalities and other tasks, and they transfer poorly to open-ended generation in the time series domain. We address this by proposing LineupRL, a reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) pipeline whose reward is caption-to-series identification. The reward model is a frozen large language model (LLM) verifier that reads the generated caption and the candidate time series as raw values, never the chart, and must pick the described time series from multiple distractors. Matching is a far lighter demand on the verifier than writing questions or judging a caption, so an off-the-shelf LLM can supply the reward. Across two captioning benchmarks, and on forecasting and reconstruction where the predictor sees only the caption, LineupRL outperforms SFT and RL baselines on every metric. The 3B vision language model (VLM) trained by LineupRL also outperforms, at 1/24 of the parameters, the 72B VLM whose captions the SFT baseline is distilled from. Our case study shows that LineupRL resists reward hacking, and that the captioner it trains both traces the trend and names the values at key points.

</details>


### [128] [AI-assisted mitotic counting improves reproducibility and efficiency across multiple tumour types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01813)
*Simon Graham,Mostafa Jahanifar,Quoc Dang Vu,Vygante Maskoliunaite,Donatas Petroska,Ruta Barbora Valkiuniene,Ayat Gamal Lashen,Jen Hong Ong,Amede Ogechi Nnorom,Sinclair Couper,Natasha Kardasz,Reshma Agrawal,Brinder Singh Chohan,Jose Luis Solorzano Rendon,Shonali Natu,Arvydas Laurinavicius,Nasir Rajpoot,David Snead*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MitPro作为可控的辅助AI可显著提升有丝分裂计数的一致性并降低评估时间，且在多中心多设备环境中表现稳健，但需前瞻性验证临床结局与广泛推广的外部验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工有丝分裂计数耗时且存在病理学家间变异，亟需提高一致性与效率的辅助工具。

Method: 回顾性、非干预、配对阅片研究；385张全切片图像，3个中心，3国，7种肿瘤类型，3种扫描仪；13名病理学家参与，每张切片由3名病理学家独立评估，先无AI后有AI（至少两周洗脱期）；主要评估一致性（ICC）和评估时间，并做支持性分析和额外肿瘤类型验证。

Result: AI辅助将ICC从0.589提高到0.949；平均评估时间从286.4s降低到127.8s（每次节省约151.8s）；AI辅助导致计数及评分略微增高（更好地识别活跃热点和减少漏检）；评分改变频率与常规病理学家间变异相当；在不同影像管理系统和额外肿瘤类型中亦有类似改善。

Conclusion: MitPro能显著提高有丝分裂计数的一致性并节省评估时间，作为辅助工具在常规病理工作中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Mitotic counting is an important component of tumour grading, diagnosis and prognostic assessment across several tumour types, but manual assessment is time-consuming and subject to inter-pathologist variability. To help address these challenges, we developed MitPro, an AI tool designed to improve consistency and efficiency by directing pathologists towards regions with the highest predicted mitotic activity and highlighting mitotic figures for review, while retaining pathologist control over region selection and the final count. We evaluated its effect on the reproducibility and efficiency of mitotic counting in a retrospective, non-interventional, paired reader study comprising 385 whole-slide images from 3 centres in 3 countries and 7 tumour types using 3 different scanners. 13 pathologists participated, with each slide assessed independently by 3 pathologists without AI assistance and again with AI assistance after a minimum 2 week washout period. Across all slides, AI-assisted counting increased the intraclass correlation coefficient from 0.589 to 0.949. Mean pathologist-level median assessment time decreased from 286.4 to 127.8 seconds, corresponding to an average saving of 151.8 seconds per assessment. Improvements in agreement and efficiency were also observed in supporting analyses using HALO AP and Sectra image management systems and in 2 additional tumour types outside the main study population. AI-assisted assessment was associated with a subtle shift towards higher mitotic counts and scores, consistent with identification of more active mitotic hotspots and fewer missed mitotic figures. The frequency of score change between unassisted and AI-assisted assessment was comparable with inter-pathologist variation during routine counting. These findings support the use of MitPro as an assistive tool for more consistent and efficient mitotic assessment in routine practice.

</details>


### [129] [Code Owns the Simulation, Jev Owns the Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01834)
*Yaodong Yang,Hongyao Tang,Yi Ma,Xingyu Fan,Weixun Wang,Jinpeng Li,Tianpei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Judgment models excel at evaluating choices visible in the input but break when required to simulate unseen dynamics; combine them with explicit prediction/simulation modules (code) for robust agent control.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Determine which decision types judgment models can be trusted with as an agent’s action-selection layer — specifically to distinguish decisions that require pure evaluation from those needing simulation/prediction.

Method: Empirical evaluation across tasks: Cognitive Reflection Test, one-shot matrix games, ALFWorld text-game, and robot control. Measured model decisions in single-call probability format; probed model separately about simulated variables; compared performance when external simulation or code supplied predictions (lookahead, physics).

Result: JEv solves evaluation tasks very well (e.g., 99% on counterintuitive CRT items) but fails systematically on tasks needing simulation (opponent moves, subgoal ordering). Failures are often not due to lack of knowledge: the model can predict correctly when asked separately but cannot both simulate and evaluate in one call. When external code provides the simulation (lookahead, physics), JEv performs excellently as an evaluator and operates as an expert controller.

Conclusion: Judgment models (JEV) are reliable when the correct choice can be determined from the input (evaluation) but fail when the choice requires predicting or simulating information not present in the input (simulation).

Abstract: Judgment models such as \jev{} return, in a single call and without reasoning text, a probability for each described option. This makes them attractive as an agent's action-selection layer, but it is unclear which decisions they can be trusted with. We test \jev{} on reflection tests, one-shot matrix games, the text game ALFWorld and robot control, and find a sharp boundary. \jev{} succeeds when the right option can be judged from what the input describes, which we call \emph{evaluation}. Specifically, it solves 99\% of the counterintuitive Cognitive Reflection Test questions. However, it fails when the right option depends on \emph{simulation} (i.e., predicting something not in the input), such as the opponent's action or the subgoal that must come first. In games, \jev{} plays suboptimally as if its rational opponent acted at random, because the opponent's action is not given. In ALFWorld, \jev{} favors commands that mention an object or place named in the task description. For example, given the task ``put a clean knife in the drawer'', \jev{} carries an unwashed knife straight to the drawer instead of first washing it at the sink. Surprisingly, many of these failures are not due to a lack of knowledge. Asked separately what the opponent will do, \jev{} usually answers correctly, and it responds well given the opponent's action. It fails when one call must both perform the simulation and evaluate based on it. This suggests letting code make the prediction or simulation. When code supplies it, such as a lookahead in ALFWorld and physics simulation in robot control, \jev{} becomes an expert controller through its general evaluation ability.

</details>


### [130] [On the Divergence of Accuracy and Mechanism Consistency in Time Series World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01842)
*Haochen Zhang,Jiaheng Guo,Zhen Xu,Zachary Plotkin,Nicholas Konz,Zhen Tan,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者提出了mechanism consistency并在8数据集上系统比较TSWM设计，发现冻结潜在空间与输出侧融合有利于准确性，但准确度不保证机制一致性；加入方向性监督可提升响应方向正确性而不损失MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前世界模型通常仅以已执行计划下的预测误差训练/评估，但用于比较未执行计划时其对计划变化的响应未被检验。需明确哪些设计影响响应一致性，并让高精度预测器在改动计划时模拟真实系统行为。

Method: 形式化状态、动作与外生输入，区分连续/模式/事件动作，引入mechanism consistency度量；构建包含8个公开数据集的基准，比较7个模型骨干与5个随机种子，评估预测空间、计划融合与计划编码的设计选择；提出并测试方向性监督损失。

Result: 冻结潜在预测空间比直接观测空间平均降MAE 9.9%；输出侧门控融合比输入拼接平均降MAE 12.7%；时间上对计划的编码影响较小（MAE变化≤2.2%）。预测误差与mechanism consistency存在分歧：最低误差配置在四/五个有声明机制的数据集中达不到一致性（偶然或更差）。方向性监督显著提升被惩罚机制的一致性，而MAE保持不变。

Conclusion: TSWM设计应兼顾预测误差与响应机制一致性：冻结潜在预测空间与输出侧融合提升MAE表现，方向性监督能在不损MAE的前提下显著提升机制一致性。

Abstract: A time series world model (TSWM) predicts a controlled system's state from its observed history and planned actions and exogenous inputs. Current approaches build forecasters with actions as covariates, trained and evaluated on prediction error under the executed plan. Yet world models compare unexecuted plans, but their responses to changed plans remain untested. We ask which design choices matter and whether accurate forecasters respond to changed plans as real systems do. We address both with a formalization and benchmark. The formalization separates state, actions and exogenous inputs, distinguishes continuous, mode and event actions, and introduces mechanism consistency, a metric built on declared action-state relations with known directions, such as a vasopressor raising blood pressure: it checks whether shifting an action moves the forecast in the declared direction. The benchmark consolidates eight public datasets with real actions from engineered infrastructure and clinical care, varying prediction space, plan fusion and plan encoding across seven backbones and five seeds. First, a frozen latent prediction space lowers MAE by 9.9% over observation space and gated output fusion lowers it by 12.7% over input concatenation on average, with both improving all eight datasets; temporal plan encoding changes average MAE by at most 2.2%. Second, prediction error and mechanism consistency diverge: the lowest-error configuration is at or below chance in consistency on four of five datasets with declared mechanisms, and no design choice avoids this. Finally, directional supervision, a loss penalizing the wrong-signed part of the response to a shifted action, significantly raises consistency on penalized mechanisms with no change in MAE. Together they give TSWMs a recipe: a frozen latent space and output-side fusion for accuracy, and a training objective for mechanism consistency.

</details>


### [131] [Temporal-Difference Learning for Dragonchess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01845)
*Jim O'Connor,Annika Hoag,Sarah Goyette,Gary B. Parker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: C++ reimplementation enabled large, statistically rigorous experiments showing both evolutionary transfer learning and TD(lambda) outperform baselines in Dragonchess, with no clear winner between them.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To evaluate whether adaptive AI methods can update evaluation heuristics effectively in a novel, structurally complex 3D game domain (Dragonchess) where computational load and unique board structure present challenges.

Method: Re-implemented Dragonchess engine in C++; conducted large-scale experiments (10,000 games) with confidence intervals and significance tests; compared evolutionary transfer learning and TD(lambda) against other agents in a round-robin tournament.

Result: Both adaptive methods outperformed all other agents; statistical testing showed no significant performance difference between the evolved and learned evaluation functions; C++ implementation enabled sufficient throughput for robust statistical analysis.

Conclusion: Adaptive methods (evolutionary transfer learning and TD(lambda)) are effective in Dragonchess; they outperform other agents and show no statistically significant difference from each other.

Abstract: Our research investigates how two adaptive AI methods, evolutionary transfer learning and TD(lambda), perform in the three-dimensional chess environment Dragonchess. The game challenges players with its unique board structure and computational load, making it an ideal setting to study how adaptive methods can update evaluation heuristics in novel environments. In this work we re-implement the Dragonchess engine, changing it from a PyGame engine to C++. This enables faster gameplay, allowing us to run 10,000 games with confidence intervals and significance tests, rather than a single small tournament. Both adaptive methods outperform all other agents in the round-robin tournament. Our results showed that there is no significant difference in the performance between the evolved and learned evaluations. This research establishes the efficacy of adaptive methods in structurally complex, novel game domains.

</details>


### [132] [AVSD-Scenes: A Dataset for Audio-Visual Description of Urban Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01861)
*Dhanunjaya Varma Devalraju,Arshdeep Singh,Mark D. Plumbley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AVSD-Scenes: a synthesized audio-visual urban scene description dataset (12,291 samples) created via modality-specific LLMs and multimodal fusion; demonstrates that multimodal descriptions boost alignment, retrieval, and classification, with strong scene-discriminative properties.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lack of publicly available datasets that jointly describe both audio and visual urban scenes; need semantic multimodal descriptions to improve retrieval, alignment, and scene understanding tasks.

Method: Generate audio-only descriptions with Qwen2-Audio-7B and visual-only descriptions with Qwen2.5-VL-7B; fuse modality-specific outputs into multimodal scene descriptions using Qwen3-14B, Mistral-Small-3.2-24B-Instruct-2506, and Gemma-3-27B-it. Construct dataset of 12,291 descriptions, then benchmark on semantic alignment, cross-modal retrieval, scene classification, LLM-as-judge evaluation, and human subjective assessment.

Result: Multimodal descriptions outperform modality-specific ones in semantic alignment and retrieval; generated descriptions achieve up to 94.5% accuracy for scene classification, and combining audio, visual, and description embeddings raises accuracy to 95.4%. Descriptions remain scene-discriminative even when scene labels are omitted from prompts, suggesting content-driven semantics rather than label memorization.

Conclusion: Paper introduces AVSD-Scenes, a 12,291-entry paired audio-visual urban scene description dataset synthesized from TAU Urban Audio‑Visual Scenes using modality-specific LLMs and multimodal LLM fusion. Multimodal descriptions improve semantic alignment, retrieval, and scene classification versus unimodal descriptions and remain discriminative without explicit label prompts.

Abstract: Natural language descriptions can provide rich semantic representations of audio-visual urban scenes, yet datasets that jointly describe both auditory and visual information remain limited. In this paper, we introduce AVSD-Scenes, a paired audio-visual scene description dataset for urban environments. The dataset contains 12,291 audio-visual scene descriptions generated from the TAU Urban Audio-Visual Scenes dataset. To construct the dataset, we first generate audio- and visual-based descriptions using Qwen2-Audio-7B and Qwen2.5-VL-7B, respectively. These modality-specific descriptions are then combined using large language models, namely Qwen3-14B, Mistral-Small-3.2-24B-Instruct-2506, and Gemma-3-27B-it, to produce multimodal descriptions that capture complementary information from both modalities. We benchmark AVSD-Scenes using semantic alignment, cross-modal retrieval, scene classification, LLM-as-a-judge evaluation, and human subjective assessment. Results show that multimodal descriptions improve semantic alignment and cross-modal retrieval performance compared with modality-specific descriptions while preserving strong scene-discriminative information. The generated descriptions achieve up to 94.5% accuracy in urban scene classification, while combining audio, visual, and description embeddings further improves accuracy to 95.4%. Furthermore, the descriptions remain highly scene-discriminative even when scene labels are removed from the prompting instructions, indicating that they capture semantic information derived from the audio-visual content rather than merely reflecting label information.

</details>


### [133] [Mapping the RAG Landscape: A Four Axis Taxonomy of Efficiency, Defense, Interactivity, and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01936)
*Meghana Sunil,Shravya V,Shravan Venkatraman,Joe Dhanith PR*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对RAG研究进行四轴化整理与全面综述，涵盖方法、评测与挑战，指出构建可靠可扩展检索-生成系统的关键方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM尽管生成流利，但知识是静态的且易幻觉；RAG通过外部检索将生成结果落到可验证、可更新的来源上，从而缓解这些问题。

Method: 对RAG框架做形式化分解，系统回顾了稠密与稀疏检索、融合策略、嵌入优化、基于强化学习的检索策略等方法，比较Naive/Advanced/Modular等架构变体，并综述评测实践与领域应用。

Result: 给出一份结构化的综述，汇总技术路线与评测方法，总结应用场景并提出需继续攻关的问题与研究方向，旨在推动更可靠、可适配、可解释的RAG系统设计。

Conclusion: 本文归纳了检索增强生成（RAG）领域的近期进展，提出了四轴分类（检索效率、鲁棒性与安全性、用户交互与工作流、多步/复杂推理），并指出检索质量、可靠性、域适应、可扩展性与可解释性等长期挑战与未来机会。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable fluency across many tasks but remain limited by their static, parameter bound knowledge and their susceptibility to hallucinating information. Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) addresses these issues by incorporating external retrieval into the generation process, grounding model outputs in verifiable and up to date sources. While prior surveys primarily focus on core RAG architectures and standard pipelines, recent research explores broader challenges and capabilities that extend beyond these foundational designs. This survey provides a consolidated and structured examination of contemporary RAG developments, organizing the field into a four axis taxonomy: improving retrieval efficiency, strengthening robustness and security, supporting user driven and interactive workflows, and enabling multi step or complex reasoning. We formalize key components of the RAG framework and review methods spanning dense and sparse retrieval, fusion strategies, embedding optimizations, and reinforcement learning based retrieval policies, highlighting how these advances influence practical deployment and system design. We also synthesize evaluation practices, domain specific applications, and architectural variants such as Naive, Advanced, and Modular RAG. Finally, we outline persistent challenges related to retrieval quality, reliability, domain adaptation, scalability, and explainability, and identify opportunities for building RAG systems that are more reliable, adaptable, and transparent.

</details>


### [134] [Counterfactual Auditing of Bias in Open-Source Large Language Models for Clinical Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01963)
*Manar Aljohani,Brandon Ho,Kenneth McKinley,Dennis Ren,Xuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A lightweight counterfactual audit using paired vignettes shows open-source LLMs differ substantially in fairness for pediatric ESI triage; fine-tuning can reduce bias but model size or medical pretraining do not guarantee fairness—auditing is recommended before clinical deployment.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ED triage may be improperly influenced by non-clinical information; as open-source LLMs are considered for local clinical decision support, it is critical to compare fairness risks (counterfactual bias) across model families, sizes, and domain adaptations before deployment.

Method: Construct paired counterfactual clinical vignettes (real and handbook-style) that differ only in a single demographic/socioeconomic/behavioral/system-context variable; evaluate ten open-source LLMs on predicted Emergency Severity Index (ESI) and compute any-shift, undertriage, overtriage, shifts >1 level, mean shift, and mean absolute shift; perform stratified and correlation analyses to examine directionality and shared failure patterns.

Result: Counterfactual sensitivity varied widely and did not monotonically decrease with model size or medical pretraining. A QLoRA fine-tuned Qwen2.5-7B had the lowest sensitivity (5.27% any-shift; mean absolute shift 0.0534) compared to its base model (16.02% any-shift; 0.1706). Some larger or medical-domain models showed larger shifts. Stratified analyses exposed clinically relevant directional biases and shared failure modes not visible in aggregate metrics.

Conclusion: Counterfactual auditing reveals substantial and inconsistent demographic and contextual sensitivity across open-source LLMs for pediatric ESI prediction; fine-tuning can reduce sensitivity but larger or medically pretrained models are not guaranteed to be fairer.

Abstract: Emergency department (ED) triage is a high-stakes prioritization task in which demographic, socioeconomic, and system-context information may improperly influence acuity assignment. Although open-source large language models (LLMs) are increasingly considered for local and privacy-preserving clinical decision support, it remains unclear how counterfactual bias varies across model families, sizes, medical-domain models, and domain-adapted models. We present a comparative counterfactual audit of ten open-source LLMs for pediatric Emergency Severity Index (ESI) prediction. Starting from real and handbook-style clinical vignettes, we construct paired counterfactual variants that change only one injected demographic, socioeconomic, healthcare-access, behavioral, social, or system-context variable while holding the clinical presentation fixed. Models include Qwen2.5-7B, Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct, a QLoRA fine-tuned Qwen2.5-7B, MedGemma variants, MedLLaMA2-7B, GPT-OSS-20B, and GPT-OSS-120B. We measure any counterfactual shift, undertriage, overtriage, shifts greater than one ESI level, mean shift, and mean absolute shift. Counterfactual sensitivity varied substantially and did not consistently decrease with larger model size or medical-domain pretraining. The fine-tuned Qwen2.5-7B showed the lowest overall sensitivity, with a 5.27% any-shift rate and mean absolute shift of 0.0534, versus 16.02% and 0.1706 for the base model. Several larger or medical-domain models showed more significant shifts. Stratified and correlation analyses further revealed clinically important directionality and shared failure patterns hidden by aggregate rates. These findings support counterfactual auditing as a lightweight, clinically interpretable framework for comparing fairness risks in open-source LLMs before clinical deployment.

</details>


### [135] [Can AI Oversight Be Zero Knowledge?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01995)
*Alessandro Chiesa,Ziyi Guan,Burcu Yildiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 要点：零知识验证所有oracle辅助计算在随机预言机下不可行；但若oracle对答案加签名，则可在抗碰撞哈希假设下实现零知识、高效验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当AI基于机密数据输出结果时，需在不泄露机密数据的前提下验证输出正确性。既有基于交互证明和辩论的工作假设验证者远比计算更高效，但对一般oracle辅助计算这不可能；因此改为研究在验证者可运行与计算同量级时间的前提下，能否实现零知识，从而保护隐私。

Method: 在负结果上，构建不可区分函数或语言，利用随机预言机模型证明不存在对所有oracle辅助计算的零知识交互证明，并将不可行性扩展到辩论模型。在正结果上，设计协议要求oracle为其每个回答附带签名，并基于抗碰撞哈希函数构造零知识证明，使验证者在多项式时间内验证，同时保持零知识性质。

Result: 不可能性：在随机预言机模型下，针对所有oracle辅助计算，不存在零知识证明（即使参与方运行时间大幅超出计算）。该不可行性也适用于辩论协议。可能性：若oracle为每个答复附签名，则在仅假设抗碰撞哈希存在下，可构建对所有oracle辅助计算的零知识验证协议，且证明者、验证者均高效。

Conclusion: 在随机预言机模型下，针对带有oracle的计算，无法实现对所有此类计算的零知识交互证明；即使证明者与验证者能比计算本身运行更长时间也不行。辩论模型同样存在不可行性。但若oracle对其每个答案附上加密签名，则在仅假设抗碰撞哈希函数存在的条件下，可构造对所有oracle辅助计算的零知识验证协议，且证明者和验证者高效。这不仅实现隐私保护，也提供了一种替代的可扩展监督方法，无需依赖诚实对手或计算的鲁棒性。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly produce outputs from confidential data, such as a fitness-for-duty assessment from medical records or the predicted properties of a drug candidate from its secret structure. It is important to verify that such outputs are correct without revealing the underlying data. A recent line of work studies verification of AI outputs via interactive proofs and debate for oracle-aided computation, where correctness may depend on an oracle such as human judgment, a physical experiment, or the web. These works focus on verification by a verifier that runs much faster than the computation. However, such efficient verification is impossible for general oracle-aided computation, and these works therefore rely on additional assumptions. We focus instead on privacy: allowing the verifier to run in time polynomial in the computation, we ask whether interactive arguments for oracle-aided computation can be zero knowledge, so that the verifier learns nothing about the confidential data beyond the correctness of the output.
  We prove that, in general, they cannot. In the random oracle model, there are no zero-knowledge proofs for all oracle-aided computations, even if both the prover and the verifier are allowed to run much longer than the computation itself. The impossibility extends to debate, a canonical model for scalable oversight.
  On the positive side, we show that if the oracle attaches a cryptographic signature to each of its answers, then every oracle-aided computation can be verified in zero knowledge with an efficient prover and verifier, assuming only collision-resistant hash functions. Beyond privacy, this also gives an alternative approach to scalable oversight that relies neither on an honest opponent, as in debate, nor on the robustness of the computation, as in prior single-prover protocols.

</details>


### [136] [Mingbird: A Local-First Agent Harness Enabling Small Open Models to Complete Real Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02001)
*Hao Wang,Ting Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者证明许多小模型的失败源于harness设计，提出Mingbird——一个针对小模型失效模式设计的本地优先harness，包含十项机制，在受控比较和第三方基准上显著提升任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是观察到小型开源模型在本地可运行但在实际由复杂harness驱动的任务中经常失败，怀疑许多失败源于harness设计缺陷而非模型能力，因此设计Mingbird来验证并缓解这些问题。

Method: 作者通过一台机器上的受控对比实验（固定机器、模型、预算、评分）以及第三方基准测试，对比四个harness（Mingbird、goose、opencode、agent-mini）在多种开源模型和任务上的性能；引入并实现十种补偿小模型故障的机制，并进行留一机制消融实验和跨基准验证。

Result: 在LRAB基准（4 harness × 4 模型 × 18 任务）中，Mingbird总体得分0.886，显著高于其他三个harness（0.631、0.479、0.405）；在τ^2-bench（278任务）中，Mingbird得0.856，也优于对手。前沿模型探针显示在相同任务上各harness差异范围为0.997到0.478。留一机制消融结果受噪声影响但显示部分机制（例如可执行完成守卫）带来显著提升。

Conclusion: 本文结论是：很多小型开源大模型在云级智能体(harness)下的失败，主要归因于harness本身而非模型，通过设计专门的本地优先harness（Mingbird）并引入若干机制，可以显著提升这些模型在真实任务上的完成率和可靠性。

Abstract: Small open-weight models (2-9B) run on ordinary laptops, but under cloud-scale agent harnesses they rarely complete real tasks: tool prefill overflows the context, self-correction diverges, tool demonstrations loop, and tasks are silently abandoned. We present evidence, from a controlled single-machine comparison and one third-party benchmark, that a substantial share of these failures is attributable to the harness rather than the model. We introduce Mingbird, a local-first agent harness for Windows and Ollama whose ten mechanisms compensate point-by-point for small-model failure forms, three of them representative: a byte-level net-zero prefill budget, a finish gate that re-reads the task before accepting completion, and signature-level loop detection. On LRAB, a controlled comparison holding machine, models, budgets, and scoring fixed (4 harnesses $\times$ 4 open models (2B-35B) $\times$ 18 real tasks, deterministic artifact scoring), Mingbird reaches 0.886 overall against 0.631 (goose), 0.479 (opencode), and 0.405 (agent-mini), with all 288 cells published; on $τ^2$-bench (278 tasks, three arms, one protocol) it totals 0.856 against 0.791 and 0.737; and a frontier-model probe on the same 18 tasks spans 0.997 to 0.478 across harnesses, with well-formed scaffolds staying within 0.072 of each other. A leave-one-mechanism-out ablation is reported as directional only: same-night replications of the same arm move its mean by up to 0.069, the size of every nominal single-trial delta, and the one batch-matched comparison (full mechanism stack versus text re-read alone) gives the executable completion guards a paired +0.10 across three replications. The evidence carries stated limits: a self-built benchmark, a single machine, and single-trial scoring.

</details>


### [137] [Counting Moves, Weighing Voices: Bayesian Dialectical Argumentation for Calibrated Multi-LLM Councils under Persistent Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02005)
*Ionel Eduard Stan,Paolo Napoletano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BDA models council deliberation as Bayesian reliability estimation from typed moves, yielding calibrated probabilities and robustness to persistent unreliable agents without extra LLM calls.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing council aggregation methods produce confidence that reflects decisiveness rather than true correctness and cannot detect or discount persistently unreliable (or adversarial) agents; need calibrated probability estimates and robustness to persistent unreliability.

Method: Treats typed deliberation moves (who proposed, challenged, conceded which answer) as observations in a Bayesian annotator model with per-agent reliability parameters; performs Bayesian inference to weight agents' signals and compute posterior probabilities over candidate answers without additional LLM calls.

Result: On binary and multi-class benchmarks, BDA achieves the best calibration among zero-cost aggregation methods, improves robustness under persistent adversarial coalitions, and remains competitive in non-adversarial (clean) settings.

Conclusion: BDA reinterprets multi-LLM deliberation traces as observations from an annotator model to infer per-agent reliabilities, producing calibrated posterior probabilities over answers and enabling robust treatment (including inversion) of persistently unreliable agents.

Abstract: A multi-LLM \emph{council} lets several large language models (LLMs) deliberate on a question and return an answer together with a confidence estimate. As these systems become increasingly used for reasoning, that confidence should represent a calibrated \emph{probability of being correct}, and the decision should remain robust when some agents are persistently unreliable. Existing \emph{council aggregation} methods fail on both fronts: their confidence estimates measure decisiveness rather than correctness, and they cannot identify or discount persistently unreliable agents. We introduce Bayesian Dialectical Argumentation (BDA), which treats the council's \emph{typed} moves---who proposed, challenged, or conceded which answer---as observations of a classical annotator model with \emph{per-agent} reliabilities. This formulation recasts multi-agent deliberation as a reliability estimation problem, using the deliberation trace to infer agent reliability under persistent adversarial behavior. By weighting evidence according to inferred agent reliability, BDA yields calibrated posterior probabilities over candidate answers while allowing persistently unreliable agents to be inverted rather than merely outvoted. Across binary and multi-class benchmarks, BDA achieves the best calibration among zero-cost council aggregation methods, requiring no additional LLM calls, and improves robustness under persistent adversarial coalitions while remaining competitive in clean settings.

</details>


### [138] [Atoms to Processes: The Role of Artificial Intelligence and Machine Learning in Chemical Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02014)
*Michael Baldea,Linda J. Broadbelt,Marianthi G. Ierapetritou,Akhilesh Jain,Ankur Kumar,Thomas A. Kwan,Fèlix Llovell,Andrew J. Medford,Ilias Mitrai,Joel Paulson,Junyi Qiao,Matthew P. Rivera,Kirti C. Sahu,Lev Sarkisov,Zachary P. Smith,Calvin Tsay,Ching-Mei Wen,Victor M. Zavala,Huacheng Zhang,Dan Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将 AI/ML 与第一性原理和领域专业知识结合的混合/物理知情方法，是解决化工领域数据稀缺、可解释性与外推性问题的关键路径，并将推动更可靠与自主的工程系统发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算能力、数据可得性与学习算法的快速提升，使得 AI/ML 能够影响从原子尺度模拟到工业运行的众多化工领域；同时存在预测精度不足、数据稀缺、外推能力差与可解释性不足等挑战，促使研究者将 AI/ML 与领域知识和第一性原理结合。

Method: 本文以观点文章形式综述近期方法学进展与示例性应用，重点介绍混合模型和物理约束（如守恒律与热力学一致性）嵌入的策略，包括物理信息神经网络、基于贝叶斯的不确定性量化、迁移/元学习、主动学习、可微分模拟与代理模型、符号回归及数据中心化方法，并讨论模型生命周期管理（模型监控、再训练与数字孪生）。

Result: 在原子尺度模拟、材料与催化剂发现、传递与热力学、分离过程、流程系统工程及工业运行等领域出现了代表性成功案例；混合与物理知情方法提高了模型的鲁棒性与可靠性，满足物理约束并增强可解释性，从而促进人机协同决策与更高层次的自动化与可持续性实践。

Conclusion: AI/ML 正在强化化学工程的核心原则，而非替代：通过与第一性原理模型的深度结合，AI/ML 有望在准确性、可解释性、鲁棒性与应用可扩展性方面取得突破，推动研究与工业走向更自主、自适应与可持续的系统。

Abstract: The rapid maturation of artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) has catalyzed a profound shift in how chemical engineering problems are formulated, analyzed, and solved. Advances in computing, data availability, and learning algorithms have enabled AI/ML methods to impact applications spanning atomic-scale simulations, materials and catalyst discovery, transport and thermodynamics, separations, process systems engineering, and industrial operations. This article provides a perspective on recent methodological developments and representative applications, emphasizing how AI/ML tools are being integrated with first-principles models to address challenges of predictive accuracy, data scarcity, extrapolation, interpretability, and model lifecycle management. Across domains, a unifying trend is the move away from purely black-box approaches toward hybrid and physics-informed frameworks that explicitly respect conservation laws, thermodynamic consistency, and known structural constraints. These approaches not only improve robustness and reliability, but also enable meaningful human-AI collaboration by providing information at an appropriate level of abstraction for the task and decision context. We conclude that AI and ML are not replacing the core principles of chemical engineering; rather, they are amplifying them. As the field advances toward increasingly autonomous, adaptive, and sustainable systems, the thoughtful integration of AI/ML with first-principles understanding and domain expertise will be essential to realizing their full potential across both research and industrial practice.

</details>


### [139] [SPHERE: Adaptive VR Indoor Scene Generation via LLM-Enhanced Spatial Preference Learning and Human-in-the-Loop RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02023)
*Hyeonmin Lee,Zheng Wei,Kyungmin Kwon,Jumin Seo,Jiwon Park,Hayoung Oh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SPHERE：一种在VR中通过多模态示范+分层约束+人机闭环RL实现跨会话、几何稳健且用户画像对齐的室内场景持续生成框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的室内场景合成不能跨会话保留用户偏好，使沉浸式创作重复、耗费体力且体验断裂；需要一种能持续学习并在几何上稳健适应用户空间逻辑的方法。

Method: 从语音和手柄编辑中采集多模态示范，抽象为分层约束（局部功能上下文与全局拓扑关系），并引入人机闭环的强化学习机制，用用户最终编辑场景动态更新检索/生成策略，整合进VR场景生成管线。

Result: 通过一项混合设计用户研究（N=42）和离线消融实验，SPHERE显著减少了修正编辑和物理负担，避免了对浅层物体特征的偏置，生成了几何稳健且与用户画像对齐的布局。

Conclusion: SPHERE将孤立的3D场景合成转变为持续的、人机协作的沉浸式创作流程，通过从自然多模态交互中提取持久的空间偏好并用分层约束建模，以实现几何稳健和用户画像对齐的布局生成。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) advance 3D indoor scene synthesis, current pipelines fail to retain user-specific preferences across sessions, making immersive authoring a repetitive and physically fatiguing process. We present SPHERE, an adaptive VR generation framework that transforms isolated synthesis into continuous human-AI co-creation. SPHERE extracts persistent spatial preferences from natural multimodal interactions (speech and controller edits). To ensure geometric resilience against spatial distortions, it abstracts these raw edits into hierarchical constraints modeling both local functional and global topological contexts. Furthermore, a human-in-the-loop reinforcement learning mechanism dynamically updates retrieval policies based on the user's final edited scenes. A mixed-design user study ($N=42$) and an offline ablation demonstrate that SPHERE significantly reduces corrective edits and physical demand, preventing bias toward shallow object-level traits to yield geometrically resilient, profile-aligned layouts. Ultimately, SPHERE demonstrates how capturing demonstrated spatial logic enables controlled spatial adaptation, establishing a reliable, governed human-AI collaboration framework for immersive authoring. Project page and source code will be available at: https://github.com/hyeonmin11/SPHERE

</details>


### [140] [Global Coherence: When Every Agent Is Right and the Team Is Still Wrong - A Local-to-Global Semantic Foundation for Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02036)
*Xin Heng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 局部智能无法替代缺失的全局共享状态：Observation-Aliasing不可能性定理证明了在观测混淆下的不可修复性，并通过语义模型与实验展示了用权威共享状态或提交机制来修补这一缺口的必要性与有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别并形式化多智能体或分布式系统中常见的“局部正确但全局错误”现象，明确这类失败是观察混淆（shared-state缺失）而非模型智力不足；给出严格界定与可操作的修复路径。

Method: 提出并证明Observation-Aliasing Impossibility Theorem（观测混淆不可能性定理）；构建局部到全局的运行时语义模型X=(H,C,G,F;D)，用拓扑、范畴、群群oid与层（sheaf）形式化局部视图的重合、状态变更、可逆转换与粘合检测；定义harness（执行器/仲裁器）负责共享状态与提交控制；用九个基准实验（controlled revision, TeamBench, tau2-bench Telecom等）检验理论边界与实用效果。

Result: 理论结果给出严格的可保证边界：只有当每个观察等价类内所有可能世界共享一个可接受动作时，策略才能保证合法行动；若存在大小为k的等价类且每个世界需要互斥动作，则最优的随机化最坏成功率为1/k。实验上，当关键信息对决策可见时系统达到完美，否则表现与理论预测一致，恢复单点权威事实能够完全恢复正确性；引入harness/提交机制能消除违规。

Conclusion: 本论文结论是：当多个智能体基于不可区分的局部观察作决策时，若这些不可区分世界需要不同且互不相容的合法动作，则不可能通过更多的内部推理、角色划分、消息或样本来保证全局合法性；唯一可行的修复是引入或恢复能够区分那些世界的共享（权威）状态或提交机制。

Abstract: AI agents can each make locally valid decisions yet jointly produce an invalid result. We call this the global coherence problem: a failure of shared state, not merely of model intelligence.
  Our Observation-Aliasing Impossibility Theorem gives the exact boundary. A policy can guarantee a valid action exactly when all worlds producing the same observation share an admissible action. If k indistinguishable worlds require pairwise-disjoint actions, the best randomized worst-case success is 1/k; more reasoning, roles, messages, or samples cannot recover the missing distinction. A stronger model can reason better within its context, but it cannot see beyond it.
  We then give local-to-global runtime semantics X = (H, C, G, F; D): topology H records overlapping scopes; category C governs state-changing actions; groupoid G retains reversible translations; sheaf F tests whether local views glue into one world; and minimal history D keeps only distinctions that alter legal futures. Models propose; the harness owns shared state and governs commit.
  Nine studies test both the failure and its boundary. On a controlled revision benchmark, the same frontier model scores 40/40 when the deciding event is visible; when it is hidden, tested arms score 12--17/40, consistent with chance (1/3); restoring one authoritative fact returns 40/40. On TeamBench, ordinary teams exceed a shared budget in 5/5 runs, a visible live count leaves 4/5 violations, and commit enforcement leaves 0/5. In tau2-bench Telecom, current-state checks score 0.07 after silent reverts, while the harness scores 1.00. Where a conventional solver already owns the complete relevant state, it ties the harness as predicted. The counterintuitive conclusion is that local intelligence cannot substitute for missing global state.

</details>


### [141] [Mimir: Physics-Grounded LLM Agents for Long-Horizon Irrigation Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02038)
*Yimeng Liu,Mi Zhang,Younsuk Dong,Zhichao Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Mimir 用“数值验证的即时修正 + 持久上下文学习”两级机制，把基于 LLM 的语义推理和严格的物理/数值决策分层结合，在灌溉长期控制任务上显著降低控制成本并节水约一半，同时显示验证与持久化信息对性能贡献大于单纯放大 LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 LLM 代理多数评估于即刻反馈的短期任务，但长期物理控制（例如灌溉）面临动作影响长期状态、误差累积与不能改写物理规则的挑战。研究目标是在这种长期、连贯的决策-物理动力学闭环下，使智能体能从经验中持续改进，同时保证执行安全与物理一致性。

Method: 提出名为 Mimir 的物理导向 LLM 智能体。方法包括：快速时尺度——用结构化的物理接口和确定性模拟器把 LLM 输出转为待执行提案，随后用数值检查与验证性修正（verified revision）并在一个有界的确定性动作选择器内实施；慢速时尺度——把反复出现的失败模式抽象成持久的上下文原则（persistent context），用于条件化未来提案。物理模型、评估器和执行约束保持不可变。使用统一的事后评估器在多站点、多作物、多年数据上比较参考方法，并做消融研究与模型尺度/家族研究。

Result: 在共有的事后评估器下，Mimir 在被评估的参考方法中取得了最低的聚合控制成本，并比历史灌溉计划回放节水约 51%。消融实验显示去掉前向仿真、验证性修正或持久上下文都会提升控制成本；增大 LLM 尺寸或更换模型家族并不带来单调收益。

Conclusion: 长期物理控制中的持久智能体可以在保留物理真理和执行权威的前提下，将语义推理与有界、基于证据的自我改进结合起来。两级修复（快速的数值验证与缓慢的持久上下文学习）是可行且有效的架构。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly combine reasoning, tool use, and action, but most evidence comes from episodic tasks with relatively immediate feedback and reset failures. Long-running physical control operates in a different regime: actions alter future states, errors compound across decisions, and an agent must improve from experience without being allowed to rewrite the physical rules that make execution safe. We study this regime through irrigation, where daily decisions interact with soil-water dynamics over entire growing seasons. We present Mimir, a physics-grounded LLM agent organized around two repair timescales. At the fast timescale, a structured physical interface and deterministic simulator turn an LLM output into a proposal that we numerically check, revise, and subject to bounded deterministic action selection before execution. At the slow timescale, recurrent failure patterns are consolidated into persistent contextual principles that condition future proposals, while the physical model, evaluator, and execution constraints remain immutable. Under a common retrospective evaluator across multiple sites, crops, and years, Mimir attains the lowest reported aggregate control cost among the evaluated references and uses about 51% less irrigation than the historical schedule replay. The ablation study show higher control cost when forward simulation, verified revision, or persistent context is removed; model-scale and model-family studies show no monotonic gain from increasing LLM size. The resulting lesson show that persistent physical agents can combine semantic reasoning with bounded, evidence-driven self-improvement while reserving physical truth and actuator authority for explicit numerical mechanisms.

</details>


### [142] [HydroJEV: A one-second, training-free screen for cyber-attack and fault attribution in water distribution networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02048)
*Tianwei Mu,Shengyan Jiang,Mingzhe Yuan,Qing Luo,Min Xiao,Wenhong Wang,Jun Li,Manhong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Jev是一个快速、无训练的首轮SCADA告警三类筛查器，可显著减轻LLM/人工复核负担，在低标注或未见子类场景中表现良好并具可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运维人员需在警报触发时快速判定原因（网络攻击/物理故障/瞬态/传感器故障），但公用事业通常缺乏足够标注事件以训练监督模型；且大型LLM推理太慢，不适合作为首轮筛查。寻求一种无需训练、快速且能提供类别概率的首轮筛查器以减轻人工与昂贵模型的负担。

Method: 在EPANET的C-Town水网上构建四类因果归属基准（含事件子类型），将Jev与手写规则树、一个有监督分类器及七个云端LLM在四轮封闭预注册的相同证据上比较。对Jev仅采用无标签的先验校正，并评估宏F1、速度和在标签稀缺情形下的表现，同时测试将‘仅当规则树也判定为良性才放行’的级联策略在新集和两个额外网络上的迁移性。

Result: Jev经先验校正后在四轮测试中宏F1为0.62–0.64，与规则树的0.56–0.61相当；在监督分类器未见的事件子类型上，Jev超出0.36–0.42；当每类标注事件少于约4个时Jev优于监督模型；推理速度比前沿LLM快20–40倍。仅接受规则树也判定为良性的Jev判决，可在新封闭集上节省35–38%需LLM复核的窗口而不损失宏F1；不改动该级联即可迁移到另外两个网络并保持非劣效界内。

Conclusion: 在SCADA警报的初筛中，Jev作为无训练、快速产生类别概率的模型，可在保持复核准确性的同时承担约三分之一的审查负载，且在低标注数据场景和未见子事件上优于监督分类器，与手写规则树性能相当并显著快于前沿大语言模型。

Abstract: When a SCADA alarm is raised in a water distribution network, operators must decide quickly whether it reflects a cyberattack, a physical fault, a normal transient or a faulty sensor. Supervised classifiers need labelled incidents that utilities rarely have, and frontier large language models (LLMs) take tens of seconds per decision. We tested whether Jev, a training-free model that returns class probabilities in about one second, can serve as the first tier of this triage. On a four-class cause-attribution benchmark built on the C-Town network in EPANET, Jev was compared with a hand-written rule tree, a supervised classifier and seven cloud LLMs on identical evidence in four sealed, pre-registered rounds. With only a label-free prior correction, Jev matched the rule tree (macro-F1 0.62-0.64 against 0.56-0.61 in distribution) and exceeded the supervised classifier by 0.36-0.42 on event subtypes absent from its labels, in all four rounds, and it outperformed the classifier whenever fewer than about four labelled events per class were available. Jev also decided 20-40 times faster than frontier LLMs. Accepting only benign Jev verdicts confirmed by the rule tree spared an LLM reviewer 35-38% of windows on fresh sealed sets without loss of macro-F1. Transferred unchanged to two further networks, this gated cascade stayed within the non-inferiority margin of its reviewer on all four sets. A fast, training-free screen can therefore take over about a third of the review load in SCADA anomaly triage while preserving the accuracy of deliberate review.

</details>


### [143] [External Observers May See More Clearly: Cross-Model Span-Level Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models via Hidden State Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02066)
*Kingshuk Gupta,Davide Buscaldi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Use layer-wise hidden states to detect exact token spans where LLMs start and continue hallucinating; cross-model observers can detect onsets as well or better than the generator itself.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Token-wise binary classification fails to capture structured sequential boundaries of hallucination; need fine-grained detection of where hallucination starts and continues using internal model signals rather than external retrieval.

Method: Inspect layer-wise activation patterns from LLMs to train detectors that predict hallucination onset and continuation tokens; evaluate precision-recall AUC and propose cross-model detection where one model observes another's internal representations.

Result: The approach isolates hallucination onsets with substantial improvements in Precision-Recall AUC over random baselines despite class imbalance; cross-model observers, even smaller models, can match or outperform generator's self-detection.

Conclusion: The paper introduces a hidden-state-based method for fine-grained, span-level hallucination onset and continuation detection in LLM generations, outperforming random baselines and showing that cross-model observers can match or exceed self-detection.

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly serve as foundational reasoning engines, their tendency to hallucinate remains a critical vulnerability. While recent internal state probes offer a promising alternative to slow external retrieval systems, they largely reduce hallucination detection to a token-wise binary classification task, failing to capture the structured, sequential boundaries of semantic drift. Here, we introduce an internal hidden state framework for fine-grained, span-level hallucination detection. By inspecting layer-wise activation patterns, we attempt to detect the exact hallucination onset and continuation tokens in an LLM generation. Our experiments show that this approach successfully isolates hallucination onsets, achieving substantial improvements in Precision-Recall AUC over random baselines despite extreme class imbalance. Ultimately, we propose a novel cross-model detection framework in which one model observes the internal representations elicited by another model's generation. We find that an external observer can match or exceed a generator's self-detection of its own hallucination onsets, including when the observer is the smaller model, suggesting that self-detection is not the ceiling for onset localisation.

</details>


### [144] [Causal Memory Policy: Making Memory Utility Identifiable by Intervening on Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02070)
*Arman Behnam,Binghui Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出通过干预检索来恢复记忆效用的因果识别（CMP），给出无偏估计与方差解，并在多项基准与部署系统中验证了识别缺失与CMP带来的AUC改进，但指出单查询效用的识别并不足以直接支持跨查询的记忆保留策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于存储层干预的记忆保留判断只能依赖被检索到的记忆，未被检索的记忆的效用无法识别，导致检索层的‘正性违背’（positivity violation）——使诊断方法无法发现部分记忆的真实用途。

Method: 引入Causal Memory Policy：在检索阶段保留固定数量的上下文槽位，按已知倾向性采样记忆并使用自归一化逆倾向加权（self-normalized IPS）在平衡分配设计下估计记忆效用。证明了记忆效用通过检索的因果分解、估计量的无偏性与精确方差表达，以及在不可逆操作下的最优决策规则。

Result: 理论上恢复了效用的可识别性并给出无偏且方差可解的估计器；实证上发现在LongMemEval和LoCoMo上分别有54%和67%的必需记忆无法识别，且线上系统也存在此问题；采用CMP后在区分必需/非必需记忆上的AUC从0.54提升到0.66；但即便单查询上的效用可识别（每查询AUC可达0.78），可用来汇总到保留决策的聚合方法仍不足以在未见查询上预测记忆的价值。

Conclusion: CMP解决了检索不可达记忆导致的可识别性缺失，通过对检索本身进行干预来恢复对记忆效用的因果识别，但仅靠在单次查询上识别到的效用并不足以直接构建可靠的保留策略。

Abstract: Memory-augmented large language models must decide which memories to retain, and recent systems do so by estimating each memory's effect on task performance. However, these estimates rely entirely on retrieved memories. When a memory is never retrieved, store-level interventions produce identical outcomes, leaving its utility unidentified. This is a retrieval-level positivity violation, invisible to diagnostics that examine only memory operations. We introduce Causal Memory Policy (CMP), a causal framework that restores identification by intervening on retrieval itself, reserving a fixed number of context slots for memories sampled with known propensities. CMP estimates memory utility by self-normalized inverse propensity weighting under a balanced assignment design. We prove the causal factorization of memory utility through retrieval, the unbiasedness and exact variance of the estimator, and the optimal decision rule under irreversible operations. Empirically, identification fails for 54% of required memories on LongMemEval and 67% on LoCoMo, and the failure persists in a deployed memory system. CMP improves discrimination between required and non-required memories from 0.54 to 0.66 AUC. Finally, we show that identified memory utility alone is insufficient for retention decisions: per-query utility reaches 0.78 AUC on the query for which it is estimated, yet no aggregation available to a retention policy predicts a memory's value on unseen queries. Code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/cmp-release-D0C3/.

</details>


### [145] [PyPottery: an AI-powered end-to-end suite for pottery processing and publication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02072)
*Lorenzo Cardarelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 开源的PyPottery通过四个AI模块半自动化陶器文件化流程，在小规模真实手绘数据集上显著节省时间，强调“增强（augmentation）”而非完全自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 陶器记录的后期处理耗时且是发表瓶颈，期望通过AI技术半自动化流程以减轻劳动强度并加快出版进程，同时把认知劳动由重复劳动转向增能协作。

Method: 提出一个开源套件PyPottery，包含四个模块：PyPotteryScan（自动图像提取与手写识别）、PyPotteryInk（铅笔稿自动上墨）、PyPotteryTrace（语义感知向量化）和PyPotteryLayout（自动排版）；在50张手绘表单（240件陶器图）上进行功能性与可用性评价。

Result: 在Terramara di Montale数据集上，参与可用性研究的用户报告中位感知加速为40×（范围17.5×–120×），系统带来显著时间节省并获得用户认可。

Conclusion: PyPottery表明用AI半自动化工具可以显著加速陶器出土文件化流程，同时将人类工作重心从重复性制作转向判断与校正，具有实用及出版流程改进的潜力。

Abstract: The study of ceramic materials constitutes a cornerstone of archaeological research, yet the post-production workflow for pottery documentation remains labor-intensive and creates significant publication bottlenecks. This paper presents PyPottery, an open-source, AI-powered suite designed to semi-automate the complete ceramic documentation pipeline. The suite comprises four integrated modules: PyPotteryScan for automated image extraction and handwriting recognition; PyPotteryInk for automatic inking of pencil drawings; PyPotteryTrace for semantically-aware vectorization; and PyPotteryLayout for automated layout generation. Evaluated on 50 hand-drawn sheets containing 240 pottery drawings from the Terramara di Montale (Italy), the framework achieved substantial time savings confirmed by usability study participants, who reported a median perceived speedup of 40$\times$ over traditional workflows (range: 17.5$\times$--120$\times$). These results highlight the potential of AI-assisted tools in archaeological documentation, while the paper addresses the strategic redistribution of cognitive labor toward augmentation rather than automation.

</details>


### [146] [Homomorphic Advantage Operator: Stabilizing Reinforcement Learning Under Fully Homomorphic Encryption Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02074)
*Abid Mohamed Nadhir,Ahmad Al Hanbali,Beggas Mounir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HAO通过对TD目标做零均值线性投影，有效抑制FHE下多项式近似误差导致的贝尔曼漂移，使加密强化学习在不增加同态乘法深度或bootstrapping的条件下稳定训练并提升策略质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在云端对机密数据执行强化学习时，完全同态加密（FHE）能提供端到端的数据保密，但非线性操作需用多项式近似替代，近似误差在时序递归更新中会累计并产生灾难性的贝尔曼漂移，阻碍FHE下RL的实际部署。

Method: 将优势函数的零均值中心化投影引入时序差分（TD）目标，对TD目标做线性投影以消除驱动贝尔曼漂移的统一状态值基线，从而保持每个状态下动作排序不变，并且不引入额外的非线性乘法深度。

Result: 在三个层次的实验（表格MDP、基于真实CKKS的加密CartPole、20节点物流路由）中，HAO严格约束网络预激活在安全的多项式近似域内，零次边界越界（0%），而仅用正则化的模型在部分种子上仍有越界，未稳定化基线在83.8%的回合发生越界；HAO在表格域提高了最优策略准确率18个百分点，并在加入类似DP-SGD的高斯噪声时仍保持稳定。

Conclusion: 提出的Homomorphic Advantage Operator (HAO) 解决了FHE环境下深度强化学习中由多项式近似引发的贝尔曼漂移问题，稳定了训练并提升策略准确率，且无需增加同态乘法深度或昂贵的密文引导（bootstrapping）。

Abstract: Privacy-preserving machine learning presents significant deployment challenges on the cloud for intelligent systems with confidential data. Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) offers a compelling solution for secure computation, preserving data confidentiality of cloud computations. However, applying FHE to reinforcement learning (RL) requires replacing non-linear operations with polynomial approximations, which diverge catastrophically due to a unique recursive error phenomenon known as the Bellman drift. This article introduces the Homomorphic Advantage Operator (HAO), a stabilization framework designed to prevent polynomial approximation divergence in FHE-based deep RL. HAO adapts the zero-mean centering projection from advantage-based value estimation directly to temporal-difference (TD) targets. This linear projection annihilates the uniform state-value baseline that drives the Bellman drift, maintaining per-state action rankings while requiring zero additional non-linear multiplicative depth and avoiding expensive ciphertext bootstrapping. The proposed HAO framework was evaluated using a three-tier experimental methodology, including a tabular Markov Decision Process (MDP), an encrypted CartPole environment using real CKKS cryptographic operations, and a 20-node logistics routing benchmark with dense continuous features. The results demonstrate that the proposed HAO strictly bounds network pre-activations within the safe polynomial approximation domain. The proposed HAO RL agents achieved 0% boundary breaches across all random seeds used, whereas regularization alone (L2 weight decay and gradient clipping) breached the bound on 3 of 5 seeds and the unstabilized baseline did so in 83.8% of episodes. Finally, HAO agents improve optimal policy accuracy by 18.0 percentage points in tabular domains and remain stable when DP-SGD-style Gaussian noise is added to the clipped gradients.

</details>


### [147] [A Comparative Explainability Framework for DeBERTa-v3 in Zero-Shot Medical Abstract Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02116)
*Javier Diaz Esteban-Herreros,David Muñoz-Valero,Raquel Martínez-España,Jose M. Juarez,Juan Moreno-Garcia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个用于审计Transformer零样本医疗文本分类的比较可解释性框架，发现解释稳定性随预测确定性变化，推荐组合多种归因方法与一致性指标并优先使用细化临床本体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对XAI中不同归因方法对同一输入/预测给出冲突解释的问题，尤其在医疗文本分类这种高风险场景下，需要量化并理解解释的稳定性与局限，指导可信模型审计与部署。

Method: 基于Medical Abstracts语料，构建零样本自然语言推理引擎：每个诊断类别用5条增强假设表示，按类别平衡抽取1000条文本；比较5种归因方法（SHAP、LIME、Occlusion、Input×Gradient、Attention×Gradient），通过“top-token”归一化解释并以Jaccard指数计算成对一致性；结合定性错误审计识别系统性失效模式。

Result: 在语义明确的临床领域取得高预测准确率且归因稳定；在语义模糊或重叠类别中，预测性能与解释一致性显著下降；识别出三类系统性失败：词汇过敏性（对单词变动敏感）、语义重叠（类别边界模糊）和归因连贯性丧失；实证支持多方法联合审计与一致性度量。

Conclusion: 作者展示了在医疗摘要零样本分类下对DeBERTa-v3进行可解释性审计的可行性与必要性，证明解释方法间的一致性与预测确定性高度相关，并建议同时使用多种解释方法与一致性度量，以及优先采用细化临床本体而非宽泛诊断标签。

Abstract: A comparative explainability framework is presented to audit DeBERTa-v3 under zero-shot classification of medical abstracts. The work addresses the disagreement problem in Explainable Artificial Intelligence, where different attribution methods produce divergent explanations for the same input and prediction. A natural language inference engine is implemented over the Medical Abstracts corpus with five enriched hypotheses per diagnostic category and a balanced sample of one thousand texts per class. Five explanation methods are compared: SHAP and LIME as model-agnostic approaches, occlusion and Input x Gradient as deep-learning-specific approaches, and Attention x Gradient as a transformer-specific approach. Explanations are standardized through top-token attribution, and pairwise agreement is quantified using the Jaccard index. High predictive accuracy is achieved across well-defined clinical domains, whereas performance degrades under high semantic ambiguity. Explanatory stability directly mirrors predictive certainty, exhibiting strong convergence in univalent categories and a marked drop under diagnostic uncertainty. Furthermore, qualitative error auditing uncovers three systemic failure mechanisms: lexical hypersensitivity, semantic overlap, and loss of attribution coherence. The results support the combined use of several explanation methods and quantitative agreement metrics when auditing transformer-based models in medical text classification, and suggest prioritizing specific clinical ontologies over broad diagnostic labels.

</details>


### [148] [VISTA: A Visual Harness for Reasoning in an Interactive World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02200)
*Qiushi Han,Keya Hu,Linlu Qiu,Cathy Wu,Kaiming He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过无损视觉记忆与主动检索的简单视觉 harness（VISTA），解锁多模态模型的长时程视觉推理，从而在多个交互式视觉基准上大幅提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明并放大多模态模型的推理能力，使其能在多样的交互式视觉环境中完成长时程任务，弥补短视窗与丢失历史信息带来的能力瓶颈。

Method: VISTA 保存无损视觉记忆（以原始观测形式），允许模型主动检索并在推理过程中重组视觉输入，使模型能在长时程任务中参考历史帧与局部区域。评估使用 Claude Opus 5.0，并在 ARC-AGI-3 及三个额外视觉基准上与简易 harness 的基线比较。

Result: 在 ARC-AGI-3 上将 Claude Opus 5.0 的 Relative Human Action Efficiency 从 40.68 提升到 100.00，25 个公开关卡全部通关，所用动作比首次人类参与者少 57.4%。在另外三项视觉游戏/谜题基准中显著优于使用相同底层模型但 harness 简化的基线。

Conclusion: VISTA 是一种视觉 harness，赋予通用多模态模型长期视觉记忆与主动检索能力，从而显著提升模型在复杂交互式视觉环境中的推理与操作表现。

Abstract: We show that multimodal models possess strong reasoning abilities and that an appropriate harness can unlock their potential to solve tasks across diverse interactive environments. We introduce VISTA, a visual harness that gives a general-purpose multimodal model long-horizon vision. VISTA allows the model to directly perceive the environment through visual observations and maintains a lossless visual memory that preserves past observations in their original form. The model can actively retrieve these observations and reorganize its visual input as it reasons. On ARC-AGI-3, VISTA improves Claude Opus 5.0's Relative Human Action Efficiency score from 40.68 to a perfect 100.00, with the model completing all 25 public games using 57.4% fewer actions than first-time human participants. VISTA's simple design also allows it to extend naturally to diverse visual environments with minimal adaptation. Across three additional benchmarks covering a diverse range of visual games and puzzles, it substantially outperforms baselines using the same underlying model with minimal harnesses. Our results highlight VISTA's potential as a general-purpose visual harness for advancing multimodal agents in complex visual environments.

</details>


### [149] [ScholarCatalyst: A Benchmark for Retrieving Papers That Inspire New Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02202)
*Sohyeon Kim,Yoonho Lee,Bo Liu,Dayoon Ko,Rulin Shao,Seungone Kim,Graham Neubig,Pang Wei Koh,Aakanksha Chowdhery,Akari Asai,Omar Khattab,Yejin Choi,Gunhee Kim,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 构建了基于作者注释的 ScholarCatalyst 基准并证明：在真实科学检索任务上，现有检索与代理方法表现有限，凸显需要新训练范式以获得专家级的文献检索直觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究科学家为何能在海量文献中识别对新问题有帮助的先验想法；用作者一线知识构建更真实的检索评估，推动能把半成想法指向必要先研成果的科学代理发展。

Method: 通过可扩展的自动化流水线，邀请 184 位一作对 207 篇计算机科学论文标注哪些先前候选论文确实或可能推进了他们的工作（并给出理由），基于这些作者判断定义了一个时间切分的检索任务；比较了基于嵌入的检索、‘agentic’ 搜索（用检索器作为工具）以及基于大模型（Claude Fable 5.1）的代理在 Recall@20 上的表现。

Result: 嵌入检索 Recall@20=0.48，agentic 搜索 0.42（低于嵌入检索），Claude Fable 5.1 代理为 0.51；总体表现仍较低，表明当前方法难以复现作者的检索选择。

Conclusion: 作者构建了 ScholarCatalyst 数据集并证明现有检索与代理方法在模拟科学家寻找先前工作这一任务上表现有限，表明需要新的训练方法来赋予模型专家级的检索直觉。

Abstract: What makes great scientists great? Even as AI systems start to make progress on open problems, scientists remain far ahead of them at sensing which prior idea, buried in an ever-growing archive of research, a new problem needs. To study this skill, we draw on researchers who know firsthand which earlier work advanced their completed projects, with papers serving as pointers to the ideas within. Using our automated pipeline that makes author annotation scalable, we build ScholarCatalyst by having 184 lead authors of 207 recent computer science papers label which candidates did or could have advanced their project, each with a detailed rationale. We introduce a retrieval task with author-provided judgments: given an initial research question, retrieve these papers from only the literature available when the project began. Agentic search does no better than embedding retrieval (0.42 vs. 0.48 Recall@20) despite calling that same retriever as a tool. Even an agent built on Claude Fable 5.1, which may have seen the completed papers during training, reaches only 0.51 R@20. These results highlight the need for new training recipes that equip models with expert intuition for searching broad corpora. We envision ScholarCatalyst as a step toward scientific agents that can take a half-formed idea and point to the prior research it needs.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [Software Project Management with LLM-Based Automation: Coordination, Validation, and Governance in Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00027)
*Ronnie de Souza Santos,Cleyton Magalhaes,Italo Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究通过对一家大型软件组织项目经理的案例研究，揭示了LLM自动化如何改变项目管理的日常活动与学习需求，但没有引入全新的管理流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件工程研究多聚焦于任务层面和开发者中心的LLM应用，缺乏关于项目管理层面、组织实践中LLM整合影响的实证理解。

Method: 探索性案例研究：在一家大型多项目软件组织中对软件项目经理进行定性访谈/观察，分析其对LLM自动化带来的工作与学习需求变化的感知。

Result: 基于项目经理经验的分析表明：LLM嵌入日常开发流程，影响估算与规划、协调与监控及治理，导致产出质量波动、需更多复核、降低执行可见性，并使学习呈现为渐进的体验式过程，集中于识别能力局限、评估生成产物及引导团队负责任使用。

Conclusion: LLM-based automation does not create new formal management practices but reshapes existing project management activities—planning, estimation, coordination, monitoring, and governance—by becoming embedded in daily work, producing uneven productivity effects, increasing review/validation needs, and reducing visibility into execution, which together raise reliance on managerial judgment.

Abstract: As software engineering has evolved, development environments have increasingly integrated automated support for tasks such as testing, analysis, and code generation, requiring project management to coordinate both human work and automated workflows. In this paper, we investigate how software project managers perceive changes in their work and learning demands associated with LLM-based automation. Motivated by a gap in software engineering research, which has largely focused on task-level and developer-centered uses of LLMs, we adopted an exploratory case study approach to capture managerial perspectives on automation in practice. Based on the experience of software project managers working in a large, multi-project software organization, our analysis indicates that LLM-based automation influences planning, estimation, coordination, monitoring, and governance activities rather than introducing new formal management practices. Participants described LLMs as becoming embedded in everyday project work, producing uneven effects on productivity, increasing the need for review and validation, and reducing visibility into task execution. These effects contribute to greater reliance on managerial judgment and coordination. Learning demands were perceived as experiential and incremental, centered on understanding LLM capabilities and limitations, critically assessing generated artifacts, and guiding responsible use within teams. The findings provide empirical evidence on how software project management work is adapted in contexts where LLM-based automation is integrated into ongoing software development practice.

</details>


### [151] [Localizing Post-Wire Semantic Changes in MCP Agent Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00182)
*Aditi Patodiya*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种基于回放的差分测试方法，揭示了多种代理集成在保留结构化信息和错误语义上存在的差异，强调回归测试应同时声明所需信息和消费者的读取策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MCP消息格式有效，实际集成中框架可能改变或丢失下游消费者依赖的信息细粒度，导致不可见的回归。需要一种可复现、接口特定的方法来发现这些语义或结构上的差异。

Method: 设计固定工具结果并通过每个框架的公共接口回放，检查显式的消费者需求（18个设计夹具，在四个固定的Python集成中执行）；对比每条路径的输出以识别夹具—任务层面的差异（结构化值、声明错误、富内容）；并通过探索性重放和故障挑战评估解析策略与检测器的鲁棒性。

Result: 在18个夹具中发现13处独特的夹具—任务差异：包含结构化值丢失、错误声明变化和富内容处理差异。Google ADK在观测路径上满足主要契约；其他集成出现接口特定修改或执行失败。对OpenAI严格富内容失败的大部分次要原因是将缺失的可选字段视作null的差异。解析JSON文本可以恢复更多结构化值但会引入错误和伪造字段。文档化设置在冲突输出情形下有帮助，但不能替代单独的结构化槽。还发现了或acles弱点并在新案例上验证了修复。

Conclusion: 本文提出并验证了一种差分测试方法，用以检测不同代理框架在公共接口上是否保留下游软件所需的信息区分，结论是：MCP消息有效并不足以保证语义保真；需要在回归测试中明确声明消费者所需信息以及读取方式。

Abstract: Valid Model Context Protocol (MCP) messages do not guarantee that an agent framework preserves the distinctions downstream software needs. We present a differential testing method that follows a fixed tool result through each framework's public interfaces and checks explicit consumer requirements. Applied to 18 designed fixtures in four pinned Python integrations, the method identifies 13 unique fixture-task divergences involving structured values, declared errors, and rich content. Google ADK satisfies every primary contract in its observed path; the other integrations show interface-specific changes or an execution failure. Whether a change matters depends on the consumer: treating absent optional fields as equivalent to null explains most of OpenAI's strict rich-content failures. In an exploratory replay, parsing JSON text recovers more structured values but also returns incorrect values and values for fields absent at the source. Documented settings help in conflicting-output stress cases without restoring a separate structured slot. Fault challenges expose an oracle weakness and test its repair on fresh cases. The study provides reproducible, interface-specific evidence from controlled cases, not production failure rates or model-behavior measurements. Its practical implication is that post-wire regression tests need to specify both the information required and how the consumer reads it.

</details>


### [152] [Testing and Verification of Quantum Compilers through Assurance Contracts and Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00255)
*Furqan Nasir,Muhammad Arif Shah,Iftikhar Alam*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对量子编译器保证技术做了可审计的综合比较与实践指导，指出单一方法不足并提出回归架构，但该架构尚需基于独立判定的编译器变更事件做受控评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子编译器的保证需求复杂多样，现有验证、等价检查和测试技术各有优劣，缺乏整合的指导与可审计的实践以在交付接口上揭露违规。

Method: 对比综述：系统化收集文献并构建源目录与提取矩阵，将方法按形式保证、经验发现、解析推导和实践建议分类；记录覆盖范围更新与源选择决策；通过失败案例与示例演示问题；提出一个回归架构并给出适用性与不确定性处理指导。

Result: 产出：可审计的文献目录与提取矩阵、四个具体失败例、一个详尽示例、修订场景下的故障检测与归因分离、适用性与互补性建议，以及未经过完整评估的回归架构；并指出现有公开数据无法形成统一有效性排行榜。

Conclusion: 作者认为应以与预期使用和可观测性相匹配的语义关系来保证量子编译器；单一技术不足以覆盖所有威胁，需组合方法并对修改事件进行可审计的回归架构验证。

Abstract: Quantum compiler assurance requires a semantic relation that matches the intended use and observations that can expose violations at the delivered interface. This critical integrative survey compares verified transformations, equivalence checking, differential and metamorphic testing, property-based testing, and evidence for assurance across revisions. A source catalogue and focused extraction matrix distinguish formal guarantees, reported empirical findings, analytical deductions, and proposed practice. A recorded coverage update and source-level selection decisions make the review auditable within its declared scope. Four documented failure cases connect conditional applicability, parameter association, termination, and phase conventions to method selection. A worked example involving layout metadata, parameter bindings, and ancillary qubits demonstrates why validating a circuit under a reconstructed interpretation can leave the delivered interface unchecked. An illustrative revision scenario then separates fault detection from change attribution. The resulting guidance specifies applicability, complementary observations, and treatment of inconclusive outcomes. Published bug counts, mutation results, and processing benchmarks support different claims and do not establish a common effectiveness ranking. The proposed regression architecture remains unevaluated as a complete system; its practical value requires controlled comparisons on independently adjudicated compiler-change events.

</details>


### [153] [Code That Works, Environments That Don't: Measuring Environment Reproducibility in AI-Generated Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00425)
*Bhanu Prakash Vangala,Tanu Malik*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出并量化‘环境依赖说明’这一新评价轴，证明当前代码生成代理在该轴上存在系统性泛化失败，建议将环境可移植性与功能正确性一并作为训练与评估目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然代码生成功能正确性已是研究重点，但实际可执行与可移植的软件还需要准确的环境依赖说明；本文旨在定义、测量并揭示编码代理在环境说明上的缺陷。

Method: 提出一种代理协议并构建三层依赖框架（声明层 declared、运行时已安装层 runtime-installed、必要且充分层 necessary-and-sufficient），基于此对三种编程代理、四种编程语言和50个编程任务进行评估，量化代理在不同层次上的依赖说明差异与一致性。

Result: 发现依赖集合一致性极低（同一任务不同代理间最低仅7%一致），新一代代理并未显著改善该问题；声明层与运行时层差异最大，表明模型的环境先验（训练分布中的默认环境假设）是主要驱动因子。

Conclusion: 环境依赖说明（environment specification）是代码生成质量的一个独立且可测量的维度；当前大型模型驱动的编程代理在该维度上存在系统性失败，表现为不一致、冗余或不完整的依赖声明，这些问题不会被仅基于功能性测试的基准检测到。

Abstract: Code generation has emerged as a central capability of large language models, with coding agents now able to produce functionally correct software projects from natural language prompts. However, functional correctness alone does not capture a critical dimension of generation quality: environment specification, defined as the accurate identification of the dependencies required to execute generated code, is equally critical. We develop an agent protocol for environment specification and introduce a three-layer framework comprising declared, runtime-installed, and necessary-and-sufficient dependencies to systematically assess coding agents for environment specification. Using this protocol, we evaluate the extent to which coding agents systematically misspecify software environment dependencies and how this misspecification varies across three agents, four languages, and fifty programming tasks. Our results show that current coding agents exhibit systematic generalization failures along this dimension, producing dependency specifications that are inconsistent, redundant, or incomplete in ways that functional tests do not detect. Across agents, dependency set agreement is as low as 7% for identical tasks, and newer agents show no meaningful improvement, suggesting the failure is not resolved by scale or recency. The largest divergence occurs between the declared and runtime dependency layers, implicating environment priors learned from the models' training distributions as the primary driver. Our findings establish environment specification as a distinct, measurable axis of code generation quality that current benchmarks do not capture, and motivate training objectives and evaluation protocols that jointly optimize for functional correctness and environmental portability.

</details>


### [154] [Extending LLM-based support for software engineers with ADHD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00555)
*Aarsh Shah,Ronnie de Souza Santos,Italo Santos,Cleyton Magalhaes,Kiev Gama*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Tether 2.0 is an LLM-based workflow assistant built to support developers with ADHD through structured modes, context awareness, and memory; preliminary expert and single-participant evaluation indicates improved task initiation, sustained progress, and resumption after interruptions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing software engineering workflows and tooling often fail to accommodate developers with ADHD who face difficulties with task initiation, sustained attention, and completing tasks. Though LLMs are integrated into programming tools, they typically lack support for neurodiversity and for guiding structured progression across development tasks.

Method: Extended the original open-source Tether system to implement Tether 2.0: an LLM-based assistant with structured interaction modes (planning, coding, debugging, review), activity-aware contextualization, and persistent memory. Evaluated via expert feedback and hands-on use by a software engineer with ADHD.

Result: Expert feedback and a case study with a software engineer with ADHD showed Tether 2.0 aided requirement clarification, task decomposition, incremental implementation, debugging, and task completion, and helped users maintain progress and resume work after interruptions.

Conclusion: Tether 2.0 demonstrates that an LLM-based assistant, designed with structured workflow modes, activity-aware context, and persistent memory, can aid software engineers with ADHD in task clarification, decomposition, incremental implementation, debugging, and resumption after interruptions, suggesting LLM assistants can support direction and sustained progress when tailored to workflow and neurodiversity needs.

Abstract: Software engineering workflows are often not designed to accommodate the needs of developers with Attention Deficit Hyperactivity Disorder (ADHD), despite known challenges related to task initiation, sustained attention, and completion. At the same time, large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into programming tools, but existing systems do not account for neurodiversity or support structured progression across development tasks. In this work, we present Tether 2.0, an LLM based assistant designed to support software engineers with ADHD through workflow oriented interaction across planning, coding, debugging, and review. The tool was named Tether 2.0 because it builds upon the open source code and foundations of the original Tether system. Our approach combines structured interaction modes, activity aware context, and persistent memory to support task progression and continuity. We evaluate the system through expert feedback and hands on use with a software engineer with ADHD. Our results indicate that Tether 2.0 supports requirement clarification, task decomposition, incremental implementation, debugging, and completion, while enabling users to maintain progress and resume work after interruptions. These findings suggest that LLM based assistants can support direction, sustained progress, and learning in software development when designed with workflow structure and context aware interaction.

</details>


### [155] [Understanding and Mitigating Library-Related Issues in LLM-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00622)
*Yacine Majdoub,Rinad Hamid,Eya Ben Charrada,Ahmad Abdellatif,Haifa Touati*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: A documentation-grounded, agentic code-synthesis pipeline substantially mitigates library-usage errors in LLM-generated Python code for evolving frameworks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs frequently produce incorrect library usage (invalid imports, outdated APIs, hallucinated dependencies), causing compilation/runtime failures and reducing reliability of AI-assisted software development.

Method: Conduct an exploratory study on 100 LLM-generated files to identify error taxonomies; design an agentic pipeline that performs task analysis, retrieves and grounds against library documentation, generates code, and runs automated validation; evaluate on 300 real-world tasks from rapidly evolving Python frameworks (LangChain, AutoGen) across five LLMs (GPT-5, DeepSeek-V3, Qwen3, Mistral, Llama 3).

Result: The approach reduces library-related errors by 38.1%–54.6% and increases code correctness by up to 16% across evaluated models.

Conclusion: Agentic approach that combines task analysis, documentation grounding, code generation, and automated validation can significantly reduce library-related errors in LLM-generated code and improve overall code correctness.

Abstract: Software practitioners increasingly rely on Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate code that integrates external libraries. However, LLMs often produce incorrect library usage, such as invalid imports, outdated API calls, and hallucinated dependencies, leading to compilation or runtime failures that reduce the reliability of AI-assisted software development. In this paper, we propose an agentic approach to mitigate libraryrelated errors in LLM-generated code. More specifically, we first conduct an exploratory study to characterize the library-related issues produced by LLMs. Our analysis of 100 LLM-generated code files reveals that 84% of generated files contain at least one library-related error, with recurring patterns including incorrect import paths, missing imports, hallucinated libraries, deprecated library usage, and unused imports. Based on these findings, we design an agentic approach that integrates task analysis, documentation grounding, code generation, and automated validation to improve library usage during code synthesis. We evaluate our approach on 300 code generation tasks derived from realworld implementations of rapidly evolving Python frameworks, including LangChain and AutoGen, across five LLMs: GPT-5, DeepSeek-V3, Qwen3, Mistral, and Llama 3. The results show that our approach consistently improves code generation quality across all evaluated models, reducing library-related errors by 38.1% - 54.6% and increasing code correctness by up to 16%.

</details>


### [156] [ASAD: Adaptive Software Agents for Debugging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00629)
*Yacine Majdoub,Eya Ben Charrada,Haifa Touati*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出ASAD，通过动态配置代理数量与角色、由协调器迭代组织，按缺陷复杂度自适应调配调试团队，实验显示更高的修复率和更低的资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Static, one-size-fits-all multi-agent debugging frameworks waste resources on simple bugs and lack sufficient coordinated expertise for complex bugs; adaptivity can match team configuration to defect heterogeneity.

Method: Analyze faulty code to estimate complexity, dynamically decide agent count, roles, and collaboration strategy; a central coordinator performs iterative planning, reflection and execution, using either single-pass repairs for simple bugs or assembling specialized teams for complex cases; evaluated with multiple LLMs on Defects4J, DebugBench, and CodeFlaws.

Result: ASAD improves bug-fix rates by 12–20% over CoT prompting, outperforms static multi-agent systems by 4–9% in precision, and reduces average agent usage by 32% across benchmarks and LLMs (DeepSeek-V3, Qwen-3, GPT-5).

Conclusion: ASAD dynamically configures multi-agent debugging teams according to bug complexity, yielding higher fix rates and lower agent usage compared to chain-of-thought prompting and static multi-agent systems.

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into multi-agent systems has shown great potential for automated debugging. Yet nearly all current frameworks rely on rigid, predefined architectures: the number of agents, their roles, and their interaction patterns are fixed before any analysis of the bug occurs. This one-size-fits-all approach is fundamentally mismatched to the heterogeneous nature of software defects. Simple bugs waste resources on unnecessary coordination, while complex ones suffer from insufficient or poorly aligned expertise. This paper introduces ASAD, an adaptive agentic system for debugging that configures its team according to the nature and complexity of each bug.
